

               ṭhe ṣaḍdarśanasamuccaya of Haribhadra 
         witḥ Guṇaratna's Commentary ṭarkarahasyadīpikā,
                  Based on ṣuali ed. and ñain ed.
                Input by Muneo ṭokunaga, ḍec. 2004


Prathamo'dhikārah[Prastāvanā]

P: surapati.kṛtasevaḥ śrī.mahā.vīra.devaḥ 
P: yad asamasamayābdeś cāru.gāmbhīrya.bhājaḥ /
P: sakalanayasamūhā bindu.bhāvam bhajante //1//
P: śri.vīraḥ sa jinaḥ śriye bhavatu yat syād.vāda.dāvānale
P: bhasmī.bhūta.kutarka.kāṣṭhanikare tṛṇyante sarve.apy aho /
P: samśīti.vyavahāra.lub.byatikarāniṣṭhāvirodhaprabhā-
P: bādhā.sambhavasamkaraprabhṛtayo doṣāḥ parai ropitāḥ //2//
P: vāg.devī samvide naḥ syāt sadā yā sarva.dehinām /
P: cintitārthān niparti.iha kalpavallī.iva sevitā //3//
P: natvā nija.gurūn bhaktyā ṣaḍ.darśanasamuccaye /
P: ṭīkām samkṣepato kurve svānyopakṛti.hetave //4//
P1: iha hi jagati garīyaś.cittavatām mahatām paropakārasampādanam
eva sarvottamā svārthasampattir iti matvā
P: paropakāraikapravṛtti.sāraś
caturdaśa.śatasamkhya.śāstraviracanā.janita.jagaj.jantu.upakāraḥ 
P: śrī.jina.śāsanaprabhāvanā.prabhātābirbhāvana.bhāskaro
yākinī.mahattarāvacanān(?)  avabodha.labdha.bodhi.bandhuro
bhagavān śrī.hari.bhadrasūriḥ ṣaḍ.darśanī 
P: vācyarūpam jijnāsūnām tat
tadīya.granthavistarāvadhāraṇa.śakti.vikalānām sakalānām
vineyānām anugrahavidhitsayā 
P: svalpa.grantham mahārtham sad.bhūtanāmānvayam ṣaḍ.darśanasamuccayam śāstram
prārabhamāṇah
P: śāstrārambhe mangalābhidheyayoḥ sākṣād
abhidhānāya sambhandhaprayojanayoś ca samśūcanāya prathamam
ślokam enam āha //
P: sad.darśanam jinam natvā vīram syād.vāda.deśakam /
P: sarva.darśanavācyo 'rthaḥ samksepeṇa nigadyate //1//
P: sat śaśvad.vidyamānam chādmasthika.jnānāpekṣayā praśastam vā
P: darśanam upalabdhir jnānam kevalākhyam yasya, sa sad.darśanaḥ /
P: athavā sat.praśastam darśanam kevala.darśanam tad.avyabhicāritvāt
keevala.jnānam ca yasya, sa sad.darśanah, 
sarvajnaḥ sarva.darśī ca.ity arthah, tam /
P: anena viśeṣaṇena śrī.vardhamānasya bhagavato jnānātiśayam
āvirabībhavat / 
P: athavā sad.arcitam sakalanarāsurāmara.indrādhibhir abhyarcitam darśanam matam
yasya sa sad.darśanas tam /
P: anena ca tadīya.darśanasya tri.bhuvanapūjyatām abhidadhānah
śrī.vardhamānasya tri.bhuvanavibhoḥ sutarām tri.bhuvanapūjyatām
vyanakti.iti pūjātiśayam prācīkaṭat /
P: tathā jayati rāga.dveṣādi.śatrūn
iti jinas tam /
P:  anenāpāyāpagamātiśayam udabībhavat //
P: tathā syāt kathamcit
sarva.darśanasammatasad.bhūtavastv.amśānām mithaḥ sāpekṣatayā
vadanam syād.vādaḥ /
P: 
sad.asan.nityānityasāmānyaviśeṣābhilāpyānabhilāpyobhayātmānekānta
ity arthaḥ //
P: nanu katham sarva.darśanānām parasparaviddha.bhāṣiṇām abhīṣṭā
vastv.amśāḥ sad.bhūtāḥ 
P:31.p.3) sambhaveyuḥ yeṣām mithah.sāpekṣatayā syād.vādah
sat.pravādaḥ syāt iti cet / ucyate /
P:31.p.3) yady apy
sdarśanāni nijanijamata.bhedena parasparam virodham bhajante,
tathāpi tair ucyamānāḥ santi te.api vastv.amśā ye
mithah.sāpekṣāḥ santaḥ samīcīnatām ancanti /
P:31.p.3) tathā.āha, saugatair anityatvam, sāmkhyair nityatvam, 
P:31.p.3) naiyāyikair vaiśeṣikaiś ca parasparavivikte nityānityatve
sad.asattve sāmānyaviśeṣau ca,
P:31.p.3) mīmāmsakaiḥ (text.mīmāmsākaih)  syāt śabdavarjam bhinnābhinne
nityānityatve sad.asad.amśau sāmānyaviśeṣau śabdasya nityatvam
ca, kaiścit kālasvabhāvaniyati.karmapuruṣādi.iti
jagat.karaṇāni, 
P:31.p.3) śabda.brahma.jnānādvaitavādibhiś ca
śabda.brahma.jnānādvaitāni ca.ityādayo ye ye vastv.amśāḥ parair
angī.kriyante,
P:31.p.3) te sarve.api sāpekṣāḥ santaḥ paramārthasatyatām pratipadyante
nirapekṣās tv anyonyena nirasyamānā nabho.nalināyanta ity alam
vistareṇa //
P:31.p.3) syād.vādasya deśakaḥ samyag.vaktā syād.vāda.deśakas tam /
P:31.p.3) anena vacanātiśayam acakathat /
P:31.p.3) tad evam catvāro 'trātiśayāḥ śāstra.kṛtā sākṣād ācacakṣire /
P:31.p.3) teṣām hetu.hetumad.bhāva evam bhāvyaḥ /
P:31.p.3) yata eva nihśeṣa.doṣa.śatru.jetā, tata eva sarvajnaḥ /
P:31.p.3) yata eva sarvajnas tata eva sad.bhūtārthavādī /
P:31.p.3) yata eva sad.bhūtārthavādī, tata eva tri.bhuvanābhyarcya iti /
P:31.p.3) evam atiśaya.catuṣṭayī.pravaram vīram mahā.vīram
vartamāna.tīrthādhipatim śrī.vardhamānāparābhidhānam natvā
P:31.p.3) manasā tad.atiśaya.cintanena vācā
tad.uccāvare.na(text.uccāraṇena)  kāyena bhūmau
śiro.lagnena(text.śirolaganena)  ca praṇidhāya.ity.arthaḥ /
P:31.p.3) etenādimam mangalam abhidadhau /

ityādinā 
P:41.p.4) jinamata.kīrtannena kīrtayiṣyati / antyamangalam
punar abhidheya.tātparyārthaḥ paryālocyaḥ subuddhibhir ity atra
subhuddhi.śabdasamśabdanena vakṣyati /
P:41.p.4)  tasya tri.vidhasyāpi phalam idam /
P:41.p.4) tam mangalamāīe majjhe pajjamtae ya satthassa /
P:41.p.4) paḍhamam satthasmā.vigghapāra.gamaṇāe niddiṭṭham //
P:41.p.4) tasmevāvigghattham majjhimayam antimam ca tasseva /

ktvā.pratyayasyottara.kriyā.sāpekṣatvān nigadyata iti
kriyā.padam atra sambandhanīyam / ko nigadyate /
P:41.p.4) sarva.darśanavācyo 'rthaḥ /
P:41.p.4) sarvāṇi mūla.bhedāpekṣayā samastāni yāni darśanāni
bauddhādīni tais teṣām vā vācyo 'bhidheyo 'rthād eva
tattvapramāṇādi.lakṣaṇaḥ samkṣepeṇa samāsena
nigadyate.abhidīyate /
P:41.p.4) mayā.ity anuktam apy atrārthād gamyate / etena
sākṣād.abhidheyama bhyadhāt //
P:41.p.4) sambandhaprayojane tu sāmarthyād avaseye /
P:41.p.4) sarva.darśanavaktavya.deva.tattvādi.jnānam upeyam, idam śāstram
tasyopāyah, evam upāyopeya.lakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ sūcito
draṣṭavyaḥ /
P:41.p.4) prayojanam tu dvedhā, kartuḥ śrotuś ca /
P:41.p.4) dvayam api dvedhā, anantaram paramparam ca /
P:41.p.4) punar heyopādeya.darśanāni jnātvā heyāny apahāyopādeyam
copādāya paramparayānanta.catuṣṭayātmikā siddir iti //

samyak.parijnāyaiva paropakārāya prastutam śāstram dṛbdhavān, tat katham

sarva.darśanānām cānādeyavacano vacanīyatām ancati //
P:51.p.5) nanv evam tarhy asyācāryasya na para.kupakārārthā pravṛttiḥ /
kuta evam bhāṣase /  nanv eṣa darśayāmi /
P:51.p.5) ye kecana mādṛśāḥ śrotāraḥ svayam alpa.buddhitvena
heyopādeya.darśanānām vibhāgam na jānīyus teṣām
sarva.darśanasatattvam niśamya pratyutaivam buddhir bhavet /
P:51.p.5) sarva.darśanāni tāvan mitho.viruddhābhidhāyīni /

darśanair durjnānaiḥ prayojanam /
P:51.p.5) yad eva hi svasmai rocate tad evānuṣṭheyam iti /
P:51.p.5) evam.vidhāś cāvibhāgajnā asmin kāle bhūyāmso 'nubhūyante /
P:51.p.5) tad evam śāstra.kārasya sūrer upakārāya pravṛttasya pratyuta
prabhūtānām apakārāyāpi pravṛttiḥ prababhūva, tataś ca lābham
icchato mūla.hānir ajaniṣṭā.iti cet /
P:51.p.5) na, śāstra.kārāt sarvopakārāyaiva

heyopādeyavibhāgasyāpi katipayasahṛdayasamvedyasya
samsūcanāt /

P:91.p.9) vyākhyā // ananta.dharmātmakasya vastuno ya eka.deśo 'nya.deśa
nirapekṣas tasya yad avadhāraṇam so 'pariśuddho nayaḥ / sa eva ca
vacanamārga ucyate /
P:91.p.9) evam cānanta.dharmātmakasya vasvasya vastuna eka.deśānām
itarāmśanirapekṣāṇām yāvanto 'vadhāraṇaprakārāḥ sambhavanti,
tāvanto nayā apariśuddhā bhavanti / te ca vacanamārgā ity
ucyante // tato 'yam gāthārthaḥ /
P:91.p.9) sarvasmin vastuni yāvanto yāvat.samkhyā vacanapathā vacanānām
anyonyaika.deśavācakānām śabdānām mārgā avadhāraṇaprakārā
bhavanti,
P:91.p.9) tāvanta eva bhavanti nayavādāh, nayānām
tat.tad.eka.deśāvadhāraṇaprakārāṇām vādāḥ pratipādakāh
śabdaprakārāḥ /
P:91.p.9) yāvanto nayavādā ekaikāmśāvadhāraṇavācaka.śabdaprakāh,
tāvanta eva parasamayā para.darśanāni bhavanti,
P:91.p.9) sva.icchā.prakalpitavikalpanibandhanatvāt parasamayānām
vikalpānām cāsamkhyatvāt /
P:91.p.9) ayam bhāvaḥ / yāvanto jane tat.tad.aparāparavastv.eka.deśānām
avadhāraṇapratipādakāḥ śabdaprakārā bhaveyus tāvanta eva
parasamayā bhavanti /
P:91.p.9) tatas teṣām aparimitatvam eva sva.kalpanā.śilpi.caṭitavikalpānām
aniyatatvāt tad.utthapravādānām api tat.samkhyā-

B: ayam bhāvaḥ --- ātmātmīyasambandhena paraparakīyādi.sambandhena vā yato rāga.dveṣādayaḥ samudbhavanti sa samudayo nāma tattvam bauddhamata udāhṛtaḥ kathitaḥ /
B: atrottarārdhe.saptanavākṣarapāda.dvaye chando 'ntarasadbhāvāt.chando.bhaGga.doṣo na cintyah, ārṣatvāt prastuta.śāstrasya //6//
B57: atha duhkhasamudaya.tattvayoḥ samsārapravṛttinimittayor vipakṣa.bhūte mārganirodha.tattve prapañcayann āha --
B: kṣaṇikāḥ sarvasamskārā ity evam vāsanā yakā /[?]
B: sa mārga iha vijñeyo nirodho mokṣa ucyate //7//
B58: paramanikṛṣṭaḥ kālaḥ kṣaṇah, tatra bhavāḥ kṣaṇikāḥ kṣaṇamātrāvasthitaya ity.arthaḥ /
B: sarve ca samskārāś ca padārthāḥ sarvasamskārāḥ kṣaṇavinaśvarāḥ sarve padārthā ity.arthaḥ /
B: tathā ca bauddhā abhidadhati --- sva.kāraṇebhyaḥ padārtha utpadyamānaḥ kim vinaśvarasvabhāva utpadyate /
B: avinaśvarasvabhāvo vā /
B: yady avinaśvarasvabhāvah, tadā tad.vyāpikāyāḥ krama.yaugapadyābhyām artha.kriyāyā abhāvāt padārthasyāpi vyāpyasyābhāvaḥ prasajati /
B: tathā.hi -- ``yad evārtha.kriyā.kāri tad eva paramārthasat'' iti /
B: sa ca nityo 'rtho 'rtha.kriyāyām pravartamānaḥ krameṇa vā pravarteta, yaugapadyena vā /
B: na tāvat krameṇa; yato hy ekasyā artha.kriyāyāḥ karaṇa.kāle tasyāparārtha.kriyāyāḥ karaṇasvabhāvo vidyate na vā /
B: yadi vidyate kutaḥ krameṇa karoti /
B: atha sahakāry.apeksayā iti cet tena sahakāriṇā tasya nityasya kaścid atiśayaḥ kriyate na vā /
B: yadi kriyate tadā kim pūrvasvabhāvaparityāgena kriyate, aparityāgena vā /
B: yadi parityāgena tato 'tādavasthyāpatter anityatvam /
B: atha pūrvasvabhāvāparityāgena tatas tasya nityasya tat.kṛtopakārābhāvāt kim sahakāry.apekṣayā kartavyam /
B: athākimcitkaro 'pi sahakārī tena viśiṣṭa.kāryārtham apekṣyate, tad ayuktam yataḥ ---
B: ``apekṣyate paraḥ kaścid yadi kurvīta kimcana /
B: yad akimcit.karam vatu kim kenacid apekṣyate //1// (pravā.3.279)
B: atha tasya prathamārtha.kriyā.karaṇa.kāle.aparārtha.kriyā.karaṇasvabhāvo na vidyate, athā ca sati spaṣṭā.eva nityatā.hāniḥ /
B: athāsau nityo 'rtho yaugapadyenārtha.kriyām kuryāt, tathā sati prathama.kṣaṇa evāśeṣārtha.kriyāṇām karaṇād dvitīya.kṣaṇe tasyākartṛvam syāt /
B: tathāca sā.evānityatā.āpattiḥ /
B: atha tasya tat.svabhāvatvāt tā evārtha.kriyā bhūyo hūyo dvitīyādi.kṣaṇeṣv api kuryāt, tad asāmpratam; kṛtasya karaṇābhāvād iti /
B: kim ca, dvitīyādi.kṣaṇasādhyā apy arthasārthāh(?) prathā.kṣaṇa eva prāpnuvanti tasya tat.svabhāvatvāt, atat.svabhāvatve ca tasyānityatvaprāptir iti / 
B: tad evam nityasya krama.yaugapadyābhyām artha.kriyā.virahān na sva.kāraṇebhyo nityasyotpāda iti /
B59: atha vinaśvarasvabhāvaḥ samutpadyate; tathā ca sati vighnābhāvād āyātam asmad.uktam aśeṣapadārtha.jātasya kṣaṇikatvam /
B: tathā coktam -- ``jātir eva hi bhāvānām vināśe hetur iṣyate /
B: yo jātaś ca na ca dhvasto naśyet paścāt sa kena ca //1//''
B60: nanv anityatve saty api yasya ghaṭādikasya yadā.eva mudgarādi.sāmagrī.sālakyam tadā.eva tad.vinaśvaram ākalpate na punaḥ prati.kṣaṇam /
B: tato vināśa.kāraṇāpekṣāṇām anityānām api padārthānām na kṣaṇikatvam iti; tad etad anupāsita.guror vacah;(?) yato mudgarādi.samnidhāne sati yo 'sya ghaṭādikasyāntyāvasthāyām vināśasvabhāvah, sa svabhāvas tasyaivotpatti.samaye vidyate, na vā /
B: vidyate ced āpatitam tarhi tad.utpatti.samanantaram eva vinaśvaratvam /
B: atha na vidyate sa svabhāva utpatti.samaye; tarhi katham paścāt sa bhavet /
B: atha.īdṛśa eva tasya svabhāvo yad uta kiyantam api kālam sthitvā tena vinaṣṭavyam iti cet, tarhi mudgarādi.samnidhāne.apy eṣa eva tasya svabhāvaḥ syāt, tato bhūyo 'pi tena tāvat.kālam stheyam, evam ca mudgarādi.ghāta.śatapāte.api na vināśo bhavet, jātam kalpāntasthāyitvam ghaṭādeh, tathā ca ``jagad.vyavahāravyavasthā.vilopapātakapaGkiltā, ity abhyupeyam anicchunāpi(?) kṣaṇa.kṣayitvam padārthānām /
B: prayogas tv evam --- yad vinaśvarasvabhāvam tad utpatti.samaye.api tat.svarūpam yathā antyakṣaṇavarti.ghaṭasya svarūpam, vinaśvarasvabhāvam ca rūparasādikam udayata ārabhya.iti svabhāva.hetuḥ /
B: tad evam vināśa.hetor akimcit.karatvāt sva.hetuta eva padārthānām antyānām evotpatteḥ kṣaṇikatvam avasthitam iti /
B61: nanu yadi kṣaṇa.kṣayiṇo bhāvāh, katham tarhi sa evāyam ity jñānam /
B: ucyate -- nirantarasadṛśāparāpara.kṣaṇanirīkṣaṇa.caitanyodayād avidyānbandhāc ca pūrva.kṣaṇapralaya.kāla eva dīpa.kalikāyām dīpa.kalikāntaram iva tat.sadṛśam aparam kṣaṇāntaram udayate, tena samānākāra.jñānaparamparāparicaya.ciratarāriṇāmān nirantarodayāc ca pūrva.kṣaṇānām atyantocchede.api sa evāyam ity adhyavasāyaḥ prasabham prādurbhavati /
B: dṛśyate ca yathā lūnapunar.utpanneṣu nakha.keśa.kalāpādiṣu sa evāyam iti pratīhiḥ tathā.ihāpi kim na sambhāvyate sujanena /
B: tasmāt siddham idam yat sat tat kṣaṇikam iti /
B: ata eva yukti.yuktam uktam etat kṣaṇikāḥ sarvasamskārā iti /
B62: atha prastutam prastūyate -- kṣaṇikāḥ sarvasamskārā ity atra iti.śabdāt prakārārthāt nāsyātmā kaścana, kimtu jñāna.kṣaṇasamtānā eva santi.ityādikam apy atra gṛhyate /
B: tato 'yam arthaḥ -- kṣaṇikāḥ sarve padārthāh, nāstyātmā.ityādyākārā evam īdṛśī yakā svārthe kapratyaye, yā vāsanā pūrva.jñāna.janitā tad.uttara.jñāne śaktiḥ kṣaṇaparmparā.prāptā mānasī pratītir ity arthah, sa mārgo nāmāryasatyam, iha bauddhamate vijñeyo 'vagantavyaḥ /
B: sarvapadārtha.kṣaṇikatvanairātmyādyākāraś cittaviśeṣo mārga ity.arthaḥ /
B: sa ca nirodhasya kāraṇam draṣṭavyam /
B63: atha caturtham āryasatyam āha -- nirodho nirodhanāmakam tattvam mokṣo 'pavarga ucyate.abhidhīyate /
B: cittasya nihkleśāvasthā.rūpo nirodho muktir nigadyata ity.arthaḥ /
B: etāni duhkhādīny āryasatyāni catvāri yāni grantha.kṛtātrānantaram evoktāni tāni sautrāntikamatenaiva.iti vijñeyam //7//
B64: vaibhāṣikādi.bhedanirdeśam vinā sāmānyato bauddhamatena tu dvādaśaiva ye padārthā bhavanti tān api samprati vivakṣuḥ ślokam enam āha --
B: pañca.indriyāṇi śabdādyā viṣayāḥ pañca mānasam /
B65: dharmāyatanam etāni dvādaśāyatanānica //8//
B: vyākhyā -- pañcasamkhyāni.indriyāṇi śrotra.cakṣur.ghrāṇarasanasparśanarūpāṇi /
B: śabdādyāḥ śabdarūparasa.gandhasparśā pañca viṣayā indriya.gocarāḥ /
B: mānasam cittam yasya śabdāyatanam iti nāmāntaram /
B: dharmāḥ sukha.duhkhādayas teṣām āyatanam gṛham śarīram ity.arthaḥ /
B: etāny anantaroktāni dvādaśasamkhyāny āyatanāny āyatanasamjñāni tattvāni, caḥ samuccaye, na kevalam prāg.uktāni catvāri duhkhādīny eva, kimtv etāni dvādaśāyatanāni ca bhavanti /
B: etāni cāyatanāni kṣaṇikāni jñātavyāni /
B: yato bauddhā atraivam abhidadhate /
B: artha.kriyā.lakṣaṇam sattvam prāg.uktā.nyāyenākṣaṇikān nivartamānam kṣaṇikeṣv evāvatiṣṭhate /
B: tathā ca sati sulabham kṣaṇikatvānumānam -- yat sat tat kṣaṇikam, yathā pradīpa.kalikādi /
B: santi ca dvādaśāyatanāni /
B: anena cānumānena dvādaśāyatanavyatiriktasyāparasyārthasyābhāvād dvādaśasv āyataneṣv eva kṣaṇikatvam vyavasthitam bhavati.iti /
B66: tad evam sautrāntikametena catvāri duhkhādīni tattvāni, sāmānyato bauddhamatena cāyatanarūpāṇi dvādaśa tattvāni pratipādya, samprati pramāṇasya viśeṣa.lakṣaṇam atrābhidhānīyam, tac ca sāmāna.lakṣaṇāvinābhāvi.iti prathamam pramāṇasya sāmānya.lakṣaṇam ucyate -- ``pramāṇam avisamvādi jñānam'' (pravā.1.3) iti /
B: avisamvādakam jñānam pramāṇam /
B: avisamvādakatvam cārthaprāpakatvena vyāptam, arthāprāpakasyāvisamvāditvābhāvāt keśoṇḍuka.jñānavat /
B: arthaprāpakatvam ca pravartakatvena vyāpi, apravartakasyārthāprāpakatvāt /
B: tadvad eva pravartakatvam api viṣayopadarśakatvena vyānaśe /
B: na hi jñānam haste gṛhītvā puruṣam pravartayati, svaviṣayam tu.upadarśayat pravartakam ucyate prāpakam ca.iti /
B: svaviṣayopadarśakatvavyatirekeṇa nānyat.prāpakatvam /
B: tac ca śakti.rūpam /
B: uktam ca ``prāpaṇa.śaktiḥ prāmāṇyam tad eva ca prāpakatvam'' [      ] iti /
B: svaviṣayopadarśake ca pratyakṣānumāna eva, na jñānāntaram /
B: atas te eva lakṣaṇārhe, tayoś ca dvayor apy avisamvādakatvam asti lakṣaṇam /
B: pratyakṣeṇa hy artha.kriyā.sādhakam vastu dṛṣṭatayāvagatam sat pradarśitam bhavati, anumānena tu dṛṣṭa.liGgavyabhicāritayādhyavasitam sat pradarśitam bhavati.ity anayoḥ svaviṣayapradarśakatvam eva prāpakatvam /
B: yady api ca pratyakṣasya kṣaṇo grāhyah, sa ca nivṛttatvān na prāpyate, tathāpi tat.samtāno 'dhyavaseyaḥ pravṛttau prāpyata iti samtānaviṣayam pradarśitārthaprāpakatvam adhyakṣasya prāmāṇyam /
B: anumānasya tu liGga.darśanena vikalpyaḥ svākāro grāhyo na bāhyo 'rthah, prāpyas tu bāhyaḥ svākārābhedenādhyavasita iti /
B: tad.viṣayam asyāpi pradarśitārthaprāpakatvam prāmāṇyam /
B: tad uktam -- ``na hy ābyām artham paricchidya pravartamāno 'rtha.kriyāyām visamvādyate'' [   ] iti /
B67; prāpyamāṇam ca vastu niyata.deśa.kālākāram. prāpyata iti tathā.bhūtavastupradarśakayoḥ pratyakṣānumānayor eva prāmāṇyam na jñānāntarasya /
B: tena pīta.śaGkhādi.grāhi.jñānānām api prāpakatvāt prāmāṇyaprasaktir na bhavati, teṣām pradarśitārthāprāpakatvāt /
B: yad.deśa.kālākāram hi vastu taiḥ pradarśitam, na tat tathā prāpyate, yac ca yathā prāpyate na tais tat tathā pradarśitam, deśādi.bhedena vastu.bhedasa niścitatvād iti na teṣām pradarśitārthaprāpakatā, tato na prāmāṇyam api /
B: nāpi pramāṇa.dvayavyatiriktam śabdādikam pradarśitārthaprāpakatvena pramāṇam, tat.pradarśitasya deśādy.aniyatasyārthasyāsattvena prāptum aśakteḥ /
B: tat.pradarśitārthasyāniyatatvam ca sākṣāt.pāramparyeṇa vā pratipādyāder.arthasyānupapatteḥ /
B: tataḥ sthitam pradarśitārthaprāpaṇa.śakti.svabhāvam avisamvādakatvam prāmāṇyam dvayor eva /
B68; prāpaka.śaktiś.ca pramāṇasyārthāvinābhāvanimittam darśanapṛṣṭha.bhāvinā vikalpena niścīyate / tathā.hi -- pratyaktṣam darśanāparanāmakam yato 'rthād.utpanam tad darśakam ātmānam svānurūpāvasāyotpādanān niścinvad arthāvinābhāvitvam prāpaṇa.śaktinimittam prāmāṇyam svato niścitnoti.ity ucyate, na punar jñānāntaram tan niścāyakam apekṣate.arthānubhūtāv iva /
B: tato 'visamvādakatvam eva pramāṇa.lakṣaṇam yuktam //8//
B69; atha pramāṇasya viśeṣa.kalṣaṇam vivakṣuḥ prathamam pramāṇasamkhyām niyamayann āha -- 
B: pramāṇe dve ca vijñeye tathā saugata.darśane /
B: pratyakṣam anumānam ca samyag.jñānam dvidhā yataḥ //9//
B70; vyākhyā -- tathā.śabdaḥ prāg.ukta.tattvāpekṣayā samuccaye, ca.śabdo 'vadhāraṇe /
B: tato 'yam.arthaḥ -- saugata.darśane dve eva pramāṇe vijñeye, na punar ekam trīṇi catvāri pañca ṣa; vā pramāṇāni /
B: etena cārvākasāmkhyādi.parikalpitam pramāṇasamkhyāntaram bauddhā na manyanta ity āveditam bhavati /
B: ke te dve pramāṇe ity āha pratyakṣam anumānam ca /
B: kuto dve eva pramāṇe ity āha -- samyag.aviparītam visamvādarahitam iti yāvaj.jñāam yato hetor vidhā /(?) 
B: sarvam vākyam sāvadhāraṇam iti nyāyād dvidhā.eva na tv ekadhā tridhā vā.iti /
B71; atra kecid āhuḥ -- yathātra dvidhā.ity.ukte hi dvidhā.evana tv ekadhā tridhā vāity.evam anya.yogavyavacchedah, tathā caitro dhanurdhara ityādiṣv api caitrasya dhanurdharatvam eva syān na tu śauryāudārya.dhairyādayah, tad ayuktam; yataḥ sarvam vākyam sāvadhāraṇam iti nyāye.apy āśaGkitasyaiva vyavacchedaḥ /
B: parārtham hi vākyam abhidhīyate / yad eva pareṇa vyāmohād āGkitam tasyaiva vyavacchedaḥ /
B: caitro dhanurdhara ityādau ca caitrasya dhanurdharatvāyoga eva parair āśaGkita iti tasyaiva vyavacchedo nānya.dharmasya /
B: iha cārvākasāmkhyādaya aikadhyam anekadhā ca samyag.jñānam āhuh, ato niyata.dvaidhyapradarśanenaikatva.bahutve samyag.jñānasya pratikṣipati /
B: evam cāyam eva.kāro viśeṣaṇena viśeṣyeṇa kriyayā ca saha bhāṣyamāṇaḥ krameṇāyogānya.yogātyantāyogavyavacchedakārritvāt tridhā bhavati yad.viniścayaḥ --
B: ``ayogam yogam aparair atyantāyogam eva ca /
B: vyavacchinatti dharmasya nipāto vyatirecakaḥ //1//
B: nipāta eva.kārah, vyatirecako nivartakaḥ --
B: viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyābhyām kriyayā yaḥ sahoditaḥ /
B: vivaikṣāto 'prayoge.api tasyārtho 'yam pratīyate //2//
B: vyavacchedaphalam vākyam yataś caitro dhanurdharaḥ /
B: pārtho dhanurdharo nīlam sarojam iti vā yathā //3// (pravā.4.190-92)
B72; samyag.jñānasya ca dvaividhyam pratyakṣaparokṣaviṣaya.dvaividhyāad avaseyam /
B: yato 'tra pratyakṣaviṣayād anyaḥ sarvo 'pi parokṣo viṣayaḥ /
B: tato viṣaya.dvaividhyāt tad.grāhake samyag.jñāne api dve eva bhavato na nyūnādhike /
B: tatra yat parokṣārthaviṣayam samyag.jñānam tat svasādhyena dharmiṇā ca sambaddhād anyataḥ sakāśāt sāmānyenākāreṇa parokṣārthasya pratipatti.rūpam, tatas tad.anumāne.antar.bhūtam iti pratyakṣānumāna.lakṣaṇe dve eva pramāṇe /
B: tathā.hi -- na parokṣo 'rthaḥ sākṣāt.pramāṇena pratīyate, tasyāparokṣatvaprasakteḥ /
B: vikalpamātrasya ca svatantrasya rājyādi.vikalpavad apramāṇatvāt, parokṣārthāpratibaddhasyāvaśyatayā tad.avyabhicārābhāvāt /
B: na ca svasādhyena vinā bhūto 'rthaḥ parokṣārthasya gamakah, atiprasakteḥ /
B: dharmiṇā cāsambaddhasyāpi gamakatve pratyāsatti.viprakarṣābhāvāt sa sarvatra pratipattihetur bhavet /
B: tato yad evamvidhārthapratipattinibandhanam pramāṇam tad anumānam eva, tasyaivam.lakṣaṇatvāt /
B: tathā ca prayogaḥ -- yad.apratyakṣam pramāṇam tad anumānāntar.bhūtam yathā liGga.bala.bhavi(?), apratyakṣapramāṇam ca śābdādikam pramāṇāntaratvenābhyupagamyamānam iti svabhāva.hetuḥ /
B: yac ca yatrāntar.bhūtam tasya na tato bahir.bhāvaḥ yathā prasiddhāntar.bhāvasya kvacitkasyāpi, antar.bhūtam ca.idam pratyakṣād anyat pramāṇam anumānam iti svabhāvaviruddhopalabdhih, antar.bhāva.bahir.bhāvayoḥ parasparaparihārasthita.lakṣaṇatayā virodhāt /
B73; āha paraḥ -- bhavatu parokṣaviṣayasya pramāṇasyānumāne.antar.bhāvah, arthāntaraviṣayasya ca śabdādes tasyāntar.bhāvo na yukta iti cet, na; pratyakṣaparokṣābhyām anyasya prameyasya arthasyābhāvāt, prameyarahitasya ca pramāṇasya prāmāṇyāsambhavāt, pramīyate.anenārtha iti pramāṇam iti vyutpattyā saprameyasyaiva tasya pramāṇatvavyavasthiteḥ /
B: tathā.hi -- yad avidyamānaprameyam na tat prāaṇam yathā śokoṇḍukādi.jñānam, avidyamānaprameyam ca pramāṇa.dvayātiriktaviṣayatayābhyupagamyamānam pramāṇāntaram iti kāraṇānupalabdhih, prameyasya sākṣāt pāramparyeṇa vā pramāṇam prati kāraṇatvāt /
B: tad uktam -- ``nānanukṛtānvayavyatirekam kāraṇam, nākāraṇam viṣayah'' iti /
B74; pratyakṣaparokṣātiriktam prameyāntaram nāsti.iti cādhyakṣeṇaiva pratipādyate /
B: adhyakṣam hi purah.sthitārthasāmarthyād upajāyamānam tad.gatātmaniyatapratibhāsāvabhāsād eva tasyārthasya pratyakṣavyavahāra.kāraṇam bhavati, tad.anyārthātmatām ca tasya vyavacchindānam anyat parokṣam artha.jātam sakalam rāśy.antaratvena vyavasthāpayat tṛtīyaprakarābhāvam ca sādhayati, adhyakṣeṇāpratīyamānasya sakalasyārtha.jātasyānyatvena parokṣatayā vyavasthāpanāt /
B: anyathā tasya tad.anyārtharūpatāvyavacchede svīyarūpatayāpi paricchedo na bhaved iti na imcit pratyakṣeṇāvagatam bhavet /
B: pratiniyatasvarūpatā hi bhāvānām pramāṇato vyavasthitā /

B75; anumānato 'pi tad.abhāvaḥ pratīyata eva, anyonyavyavacchedarūpāṇām itaraprakāravyavacchedena tad.itaraprakāravyavasthāpanāt /

B88; vyākhyā -- sādhya.dharmaviśiṣṭo dharmī pakṣah, tasya dharmaḥ pakṣa.dharmah, tad.bhāvaḥ pakṣa.dharmatvam / pakṣa.śabdena cātra kevalo dharmy evābhidhīyate, avayave samudāyopacārāt /

B: idam ekam rūpam

B: eṣām udāharaṇāni prāg evopadarśitāni, tathāpi punaḥ svabhāva.hetur.udāhriyate, sarvam kṣaṇikam iti pakṣah, sattvād iti hetuh, ayam hetuḥ sarvasmin vartata iti pakṣa.dharmatvam, yat sat tat kṣaṇikam yathā vdyud.ādi.iti sapakṣasattvam, yat kṣaṇikam na bhavati, tat sad api na bhavati yathā khapuṣpam /

B93; sautrāntikamatam punar idam -- rūpavedanā.vijñānasamjñā.samskārāḥ sarva.śarīriṇām ete pañca skandhā vidyante, na punar ātmā /
B: ta eva hi para.loka.gāminaḥ /
B: tathā ca tat siddhāntaḥ -- pañca.imāni bhikṣavaḥ samjñāmātram pratijñāmātram samvṛti.mātram vyavahāramātram /
B: katamāni pañca /
B: atīto 'ddhā, anāgato 'ddhā, sahetuko vināśah, ākāśam, pudgala iti /
B: atra pudgala.śabdena paraparikalpito nityatvavyāpakatvādi.dharmaka ātmā.iti /
B: bāhyo 'rtho nityam apratyakṣa eva, jñānākārān yathānupapattyā tu sann avagamyate /
B: sākāro bodhaḥ pramāṇam /
B: tathā kṣaṇikāḥ sarvasamskārāḥ /
B: svalakṣaṇam paramārthaḥ /
B: yad āhus tadvādinaḥ -- ''pratikṣaṇam viśarāravo(?) rūparasa.gandhasparśaparamāṇavo jñānam ca.ity eva tattvam''[   ] iti /
B: anyāpohaḥ śabdārthaḥ /
B: tad.utpatti.tad.ākāratābhyām arthaparicchedaḥ /
B: nairātmya.bhāvanāto jñānasamtānocchedo mokṣa iti /
B94; yaugācāramatam tv idam -- vijñānamātram idam bhuvanam /
B: nāsti bāhyo 'rthaḥ / 
B: jñānādvaitasyaiva.tāttvikatvāt /
B: aneke jñānasamtānāḥ /
B: sākāro bodhaḥ pramāṇam /
B: vāsanā.paripākato nīlapītādi.pratibhāsāḥ /
B: ālayavijñānam hi sarvavāsanādhāra.bhūtam /
B: ālayavijñānaviśuddhir evāpavarga iti /
B95; mādhyamika.darśane tu -- śūnyam idam /
B: svapnopamaḥ pramāṇaprameyayoḥ pravibhāgaḥ /
B: ''muktis tu śūnyatā.dṛṣṭeḥ tad.artham śeṣa.bhāvanā'' [pravā.1.256]
B: kecit tu mādhyamikāḥ svastham jñānam āhuḥ / tad uktam --
B: ''artho jñānasamanvito matimatā vaibhāṣikeṇocyateṇ
B: pratyakṣo na hi bāhyavastu.visaraḥ sautrāntikair āśritaḥ /
B: yaugācāramatānugair abhimatā sākāra.buddhiḥ parāṇ
B: manyante bata madhyamāḥ kṛta.dhiyaḥ svasthām parām samvidam //1//'' [  ] iti /
B: jñānapāramitā.ādyā daśa granthāḥ / tarka.bhāṣā hetu.bindus taṭ.ṭīkārcaṭa.(?).tarkanāmnī pramāṇavārttikam tattvasamgraho nyāya.binduḥ kamala.śīlo nyāyapraveśakaś ca.ityādayas tad.granthā iti /
B96; evam bauddhamatam abhidhāya tad eva samcikṣipsur uttaram cābhisamdhitsur āha --
B: bauddharāddhāntavācyasya samkṣepo 'yam niveditaḥ /

ṣḍṣ: ṇyāya

ṇ: atha dvitīyo 'dhikāraḥ /
ṇ: naiyāyikamatasya.itaḥ kathyamāno niśamyatām //12//
ṇ1: naiyāyikamatasya śaiva.śāsanasya samkṣepa ita ūrdhvam kathyamāno niśamyatām śrūyatām /
ṇ: athādau naiyāyikānām yaugāparābhidhānānām liGgādi.vyaktir ucyate /
ṇ: te ca daṇḍadharāh, prauḍha.kaupīnaparidhānāh, kambalikā.prāvṛtāh, jaṭādhāriṇah, bhasmoddhūlanaparāh, yajnopavītinah, jalādhāra..pātra.karāh, nīrasāhārāh, prāyo.vanavāsino dor.mūle, tumbakam bibhrāṇāh, kandamūlaphalāśinaḥ (phalāśinah), ātithya.karmaniratāhṇsastrīkāh, nihstrīkāś (nistrīkāś) ca /
ṇ: nihstrīkās (nistrīkās) teṣ.uttamāḥ /
ṇ: te ca pañcāgni.sādhanaparāh, kare jaṭā.ādau ca prāṇa.liGga.dharāś cāpi bhavanti /
ṇ: uttamām samyamāvasthām prāptās tu nagnā bhramanti /
ṇ: ete prātar.dantapādādi.śaucam vidhāya śivam dhyāyanto bhasmanāGgam tris triḥ spṛśanti /
ṇ: yajamāno vandamānaḥ kṛtāñjalir vakti ``om namaḥ śivāha'' iti /
ṇ: gurus tathaiva ``śivāya namah'' iti prativakti /
ṇ: te ca samsady evam vadanti ---
ṇ: ``śaivīm dīkṣām dvādaśābdīm sevitvā yo 'pi muñcati /
ṇ: dāsī dāso 'pi bhavati so 'pi nirvāṇam ṛcchati //1//''

ṇ2: teṣām īśvaro devaḥ sarvajñaḥ sṛṣṭi.samhārādi.kṛt /
ṇ: tasya cāṣṭādaśāvatārā amī -- nakulo 1, śoṣyakauśikaḥ 3, gārgyaḥ 3, maitryaḥ 4, akauruṣaḥ 5, īśānaḥ 6, pāragārgyaḥ 7, kapilāṇḍaḥ 8, manuṣyakaḥ 9, kuśikaḥ 10, atriḥ 11, piGgalaḥ 12, puṣpakaḥ 13, bṛhad.āryaḥ 14, agastiḥ 15, samtānaḥ 16, rāśīkaraḥ 17, vidyā.guruś ca 18 /
ṇ: ete teṣām tīrtha.īśāḥ pūjanīyāḥ /
ṇ: eteṣām pūjā.praṇidhānavidhis tu tad.āgamād veditavyaḥ /

ṇ3: teṣām sarva.tīrtheṣu bharaṭā eva pūjakāḥ /
ṇ: devānām namas.kāro na sanmkhaiḥ kāryaḥ /
ṇ: teṣu ye nirvikārās te svamīmāmsā.gatam idam padyam darśayanti --
ṇ: ``na svardhunī na phaṇino na kapāla.dāma, na.indoḥ kalā na girijā na jaṭā na bhasma /
ṇ: yatrānya.deva ca na kimcid upāsmahe tad.rūpam purāṇamuni.śīlitam īśvarasya //1//(?)
ṇ: sa eva yoginām sevyo hy arvācīnas tu bhoga.bhāk /
ṇ: sa dhyāyamāno rājyādi.sukha.lubdhair niṣevyate //2//''
ṇ: uktam ca taiḥ sva.yoga.śāstre --
ṇ: ``vītarāgam smaran yogī vītarāgatvam aśnute /
ṇ: sarāgam dhyāyatas tasya sarāgatvam niścitam //3//
ṇ: yena yena hi bhāvena yujyate yantravāhakaḥ /
ṇ: tena tanmayatām yāti viśvarūpo maṇir yathā //4// iti /

ṇ4: etat sarvam liGgaveṣa.devādi.svarūpam vaiśeṣikamate.apy avasātavyam /
ṇ: yato naiyāyikavaiśeṣikāṇām hi mithaḥ pramāṇa.tattvānām samkhyā.bhede saty apy anyonyam tattvānām antar.bhāvane.alpīyān eva bhedo jāyate, tenaiteṣām prāyo mata.tulyatā /
ṇ: te ca śaivādi.bhedena caturdhā bhavanti / tad uktam --
ṇ: ``ādhāra.bhasma.kaupīna.jaṭā.yajnopavītinaḥ /
ṇ: svasvācārādi.bhedena caturdhā syus tapasvinaḥ //1//
ṇ: śaivāḥ pāśupatāś caiva mahā.vrata.dharās tathā /
ṇ: turyāḥ kālamukhā mukhyā bhedā ete tapasvinām //2//''

ṇ5: teṣām antar.bhedā bharaṭa.bhaktara(?).laiGgika.tāpasādayo bhavanti /
ṇ: bharaṭādīnām vrata.grahaṇe brāhmaṇādi.varṇaniyamo nāsti /
ṇ: yasya tu śive bhaktiḥ sa vratī bharaṭādir bhavet /
ṇ: param śāstreṣu naiyāyikāḥ sadā śiva.bhaktatvāt.śaivā ity ucyante, vaiśeṣikās tu pāśupatā /
ṇ: tena naiyāyika.śāsanam śaivam ākhyāyate, vaiśeṣika.darśanam ca pāśupatam iti /
ṇ: idam mayā yathā.śrutam yathā.dṛṣṭam cātrābhidadhe /
ṇ: tat.tad.viśeṣas tu tad.granthebhyo vijñeyaḥ //12//

ṇ6: atha pūrvapratijñātam naiyāyikamatasamkṣepam evāha ---
ṇ: akṣapādamate.(ākṣapadamate) devaḥ sṛṣṭi.samhāra.kṛt śivaḥ / (ākṣa- is the correct reading)
ṇ: vibhur nityaikasarvajño ntya.buddhi.samāśrayaḥ //13//

ṇ7: vyākhyā -- akṣapādenādyena guruṇā yataḥ praṇītam naiyāyikamatasya mūlasūtram tena naiyāyikā ākṣapādā abhidhīyante, tan.matam cākṣapādamatam iti /
ṇ: tasminn ākṣapādamate śivo mahā.īśvarah, sṛṣṭiś carācarasya (or carācarasya) jagato nirmāṇam, samhāras tad.vināśah, dvandve sṛṣṭi.samhārau, tāv asāv acintya.śakti.māhātmyena karoti.iti sṛṣṭi.samhārakṛt /
ṇ: kevalāyāḥ sṛṣṭeḥ karaṇe nirantarotpādyamāno 'samkhyaḥ prāṇi.gaṇo bhuvana.traye.api na māyād iti sṛṣṭivat samhārasyāpi karaṇam /
ṇ: atra prayogam evam śaivā vyāharante -- bhū.bhū.dharasudhā.kara.dina.karamakar.ākarādikam buddhimat.pūrvakam, kāryatvāt, yad yat kāryam tat tad buddhimat.pūrvakam, yathā ghaṭah, kāryam ca.idam, tasmād buddhimat.pūrvakam /
ṇ: yaś cāsya buddhimān sraṣṭā sa īśvara eva.ity anvayaḥ /
ṇ: vyatireke gaganam /
ṇ: na cāyam asiddho hetuh, bhū.bhū.dharādīnām svasva.kāraṇa.kalāpa.janyatvenāvayavitayā vā kāryatvasya jagati suprasiddhatvāt /
ṇ: nāpi viruddho 'naikāntiko vāqqvipakṣād atyantam vyāvṛttatvāt /
ṇ: nāpi kāātyayāpadiṣṭah, pratyakṣāgamābādhyamānasādhya.dharma.dharmi.viṣaye hetoḥ pravartanāt /
ṇ: nāpi prakaraṇasamahqqtat.pratipanthi.padārthasvarūpasamarthanaprathitapratyanumānodayābhāvāt /

ṇ8: atha nirvṛtātmavad aśarīratvād eva na sambhavati sṛṣṭi.samhāra.kartā.īśvara iti pratyanumānodayāt katham na prakaraṇasama iti cetqqucyate --
ṇ: atra tvadīyānumāne sādhyamāna īśvaro dharmī tvayā pratītah, apratīto vābhipreyate? apratītaś cet, tadā tvat.parikalpita.hetor āśrayāsiddhi.doṣaḥ prasajyate /
ṇ: pratītaś cet, tarhi yena pramaṇena pratītas tenaiva svayam udbhāvitanija.tanur api kim.iti nābhyupeyata itikatham aśarīratvam /
ṇ: tato na prakaraṇasama.doṣatā hetoḥ /
ṇ: ataḥ sādhu.uktam `sṛṣṭi.samhāra.kṛt śivah' iti /

ṇ9: tathā vibhur ākāśavat sarva.jagad.vyāpakaḥ /
ṇ: niyataikasthānavartitve hy aniyatapradeśavartinām padārthānām prtiniyata.yathāvan nirmāṇānupapatteḥ /
ṇ: na hy ekasthānasthitaḥ kumbha.kāro 'pi dūratara.ghaṭādi.ghaṭanāyām vyāpriyate, tasmād vibhuḥ /

ṇ10: tathā nityaikasarvajñaḥ /
ṇ: nityaś cāsāv ekaś ca nityaikaḥ sa cāsau sarvajñaś ca.iti viśeṣaṇa.trayasamāsaḥ /
ṇ: tatra nityo 'pracyutānutpannasthiraikarūpaḥ kūṭasthaḥ /
ṇ: īśvarasya hy anityatve parādhīnotpatti.savyapekṣayā kṛtakatvaprāptiḥ /
ṇ: svotpattāv apekṣitaparavyāpāro hi bhāvaḥ kṛtaka iṣyate /
ṇ: kṛtakaś cej jagat.kartā syāt, tadā tasyāpy apareṇa kartrā bhāvyam, anityatvād eva /
ṇ: bhāvyam, anityatvād eva /
ṇ: aparasyāpi ca kartur anyena kartrā bhavanīyam ity anavasthā.nadī dustarā syāt /
ṇ: tasmān nitya evābhyupagamanīyaḥ /

ṇ11: nityo 'pi sa eko 'dvitīyo mantavyaḥ /
ṇ: bahūnām hi jagat.kartṛtvasvīkāre parasparam pṛthak pṛthag anyānyānyavisadṛśam ativyāpṛtatvenaikaikapadārthasya visadṛśanirmāṇe sarvam asamañjasam āpadyeta.iti yuktam eka iti viśeṣaṇam /

ṇ12: eko 'pi sa sarvajñaḥ sarvapadārthānām sāmastyena jñātā /
ṇ: sarvajñatvābhāve hi vidhitsitapadārthopayogi.jagat.prasṛmaraviprakīrṇaparamāṇu.kaṇapracayasamyak.sāmagrī.mīlanākṣamatayā yāthātathyena padārthānām nirmāṇam durghaṭam bhavet /
ṇ: sarvajñatve punaḥ sakalaprāṇinām sammīlitasamucita.kāraṇa.kalāpānurūpyeṇa kāryam vastu nirmimāṇaḥ svārjitapuṇyapāpānumānena.(anusāreṇa) casvarganarakayoḥ sukha.duhkhopabhogam ddānaḥ sarvathāucitīm nātivarteta / tathā coktam tad.bhaktaiḥ --
ṇ: ''jñānam apratigham yasya vairāgyam ca jagat.pateḥ /
ṇ: aiśvaryam caiva dharmaś ca sahasiddham catuṣṭayam //1//''
ṇ: ''ajño jantur anīśo 'yam ātmanaḥ sukha.duhkhayoḥ /
ṇ: īśvaraprerito gacchet svargam vā śvabhram eva vā //2//'' [mahābhā.vanaparvan 30.28)

ṇ13: athavā nityaikasarvajña ity ekam eva viśeṣaṇam vyākhyeyam /
ṇ: nityaḥ sadā.eko.dvitīyaḥ sarvajño nityaikasarvajñaḥ /
ṇ: etenānādi.sarvajñam īśvaram ekam vihāyānyaḥ ko 'pi sarvajñaḥ kadāpi na bhavati /
ṇ: yata īśvarād anyeṣām yoginām jñānāny aparam sarvam atīndriyam artham jānānāny(?) api svātmānam na jānate, tatas te katham sarvajñāḥ syur ity āveditam bhavati /

ṇ14: tathā nitya.buddhi.samāśrayo nityāyā buddher jñānasya sthānam, kṣaṇika.buddhimato hi parādhīna.kāryāpekṣaṇena mukhya.kartṛtvābhāvād anīśvaratvaprasaktir iti /
ṇ: īdṛśaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭo naiyāyikamate śivo devaḥ //13//
ṇ: atha tan.mete tattvāni vivariṣuḥ prathamam teṣām samkhyām nāmāni ca samākhyāti --
ṇ: tattvāni ṣoḍaśāmutra pramāṇādīni tad.yathā /
ṇ: pramāṇam ca prameyam ca samśayaś ca prayojanam //14//
ṇ: dṛṣṭānto 'py atha siddhānto 'vayavās tarkanirṇayau /
ṇ: vādo jalpo vitaṇḍā ca hetv.ābhāsāś chalāni ca //15//
ṇ: jātayo nigrahasthānāny eṣām evam prarūpaṇā /
ṇ: arthopalabdhi.hetuḥ syāt pramāṇam tac caturvidham //16 (tribhir viśeṣakam)

ṇ14: vyākhyā -- amutrāsmin prakrānte naiyāyikamate pramāṇādīni pramāṇaprameyaprabhṛtīni ṣoḍaśa tattvāni bhavanti /
ṇ: tad.yathā.ity upadarśane /
ṇ: pramāma, ca ity ādi /
ṇ: tatra pramitir upalabdhir jñānam yena janyate taj.jñānasya janakam kāraṇam pramāṇam /
ṇ: pramīyate jñānam janyate.anena.iti pramāṇam iti vyutpatteḥ /
ṇ: jñānasya ca janakam dvividham -- acetanam jñānam ca /
ṇ: tatrācetanam indriya.tad.arthasamnikarṣapradīpa.liGga.śabdādikam jñānasya kāraṇatvāt pramāṇam /
ṇ: jñānam ca jñānāntara.janmani yad vyāpriyate tad api jñāna.janakatvāt pramāṇam /
ṇ: jñānasyājanakam tu pramāṇasya phalam bhaven na punaḥ pramāṇam 1/
ṇ: prameyam pramāṇa.janya.jñānena grāhyam vastu 2/
ṇ: dolāyamānā pratītiḥ samśayaḥ /
ṇ: ca.kārās trayo 'pi pramāṇādīnām anyonyāpekṣayā samuccayārthāḥ 3/
ṇ: prayojanam abhīṣṭam sādhanīyam phalam 4/
ṇ: dṛṣṭānto vādi.prativādi.sammatam nidarśanam 5/
ṇ: apiḥ samuccaye /
ṇ: atha.śabda ānantarye /
ṇ: siddhāntaḥ sarva.darśanasammata.śāstraprabhṛtiḥ 6/
ṇ: avayavāḥ pakṣādayo 'numānasyāGgāni 7/
ṇ: samdehād ūrdhvam anvaya.dharma.cintanam tarkah, sthānur atrādhunā sambhavati.iti 8/
ṇ: sthāṇur evāyam ity avadhāraṇam nirṇayaḥ /
ṇ: dvandve tarkanirṇayau 9/
ṇ: guruṇā samam tattvanirṇayārtham vadanam vādaḥ 10/
ṇ: pareṇa samam jigīṣayā jalpanam jalpaḥ 11/
ṇ: aparāmṛṣṭavastu.tattvam maukharyamātram vitaṇḍā 12/
ṇ: hetuvad.ābhāsamānā hetv.ābhāsā na samyagg.hetava ity arthaḥ 13/
ṇ: paravacanavighātārtham vikalpotpādanāni chalāni 14/
ṇ: jātayo 'samyag.dūṣaṇāni 15/
ṇ: yair uktair vaktā nigṛhyate tāni nigrahasthānāni 16/
ṇ: eṣām anantaroktānām pramāṇādīnām evam ittham prarūpaṇā svarūpapradarśanā bhavati /

ṇ15: tatrādau pramāṇasya prarūpaṇām cikīrṣuḥ prathamatas tasya sāmānya.lakṣaṇam samkhyām ca prāha --
ṇ: 'arthopalabdhi.hetuḥ syāt pramāṇam' /
ṇ: arthasya grāahyasya bāhyasya stambha.kumbhāmbhoruhādeh, āntarasya ca jñānasukhāder upalabdhir jñānam arthopalabdhiḥ /
ṇ: vyākhyānato viśeṣapratipattir iti nyāyād atrāvyabhicāriṇyavyapadeśyā vyavasāyātmikā cārthopalabdhir grāhyā, na tu.upalabdhi.mātram /
ṇ: tasyā yo hetuḥ kāraṇam sa pramāṇam syād bhavet /
ṇ: arthopalabdhis tu pramāṇasya phalam /
ṇ: ayam atra bhāvaḥ -- avyabhivārādi.viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭārthopalabdhi.janikā sāmagrī tad.ekadeśo vā cakṣuh.pradīpa.jñānādir bodharūpo 'bodharūpo vā sādhakatamatvāt pramāṇam /
ṇ: taj.janakatvam ca tasya prāmāṇyam /
ṇ: taj.janyā tv arthopalabdhirḥ phalam iti /
ṇ: indriyajatva.liGgajatvādi.viśeṣaṇaviśeṣitā sā.evopalabdhir yataḥ syāt, tad eva pratyakṣādi.pramāṇasya viśeṣa.lakṣaṇam vakṣyate /
ṇ: kevalam atrāvyapadeśyam iti viśeṣaṇam na śābde sambandhanīyam tasya śabda.janyatvena vyapadeśyatvāt /
ṇ: atra pramāṇasya bhedanān āha -- 'tac caturvidham', tat pramāṇam caturvidham catur.bhedam //14-16//

ṇ16: atha tac cāturvidhyam evāha --
ṇ: pratyakṣam anumānam copamānam śābdikam tathā /
ṇ: tatra.indriyārthasamparkotpannam avyabhicāri ca //17//
ṇ: vyavasāyātmakam jñānam vyapadeśavarjitam /
ṇ: pratyakṣam anumānam tu tat.pūrvam trividham bhavet //18//
ṇ: pūrvavat.śeṣavac.caiva dṛṣṭam sāmānyatas tathā /
ṇ: tatrādyam kāraṇāt kāryānumānam iha gīyate //16//

ṇ17: vyākhyā -- pratyakṣam adhyakṣam, anumānam laiGgikam, ca.kāraḥ samuccayārthah, upamānam upamitih, tathā.śabdasya samuccayārthatvāt śābdikam ca śabde bhavam śābdikam āgama ity.arthaḥ /
ṇ: atha pratyakṣasya lakṣaṇam lakṣayati /
ṇ: 'tatra.indriyārtha' ityādi /
ṇ: tatra.iti teṣupramāṇeṣu prathamam pratyakṣam ucyate /
ṇ: atrāsya.idam akṣapādapraṇītam sūtram -- ''indriyārthasamnikarṣotpannam jñānam avyapadeśyam avyabhicāri vyavasāyātmakam pratyakṣam /'' iti [ṇṣ 1.1.4]
ṇ: indriyam cakṣur.ādi.manah.paryantam (manas), tasyārthah, paricchedya indriyārtha indriyaviṣaya.bhūto 'rtho rūpādih, ''rūpādayas tad.arthāh'' [  ] iti vacanāt /
ṇ: tena samnikarṣaḥ pratyāsattir indriyasya prāptiḥ sambandha iti yāvat /
ṇ: sa ca ṣoḍhā indriyeṇa sārdham dravyasya samyoga eva 1/
ṇ: rūpādi.guṇānām samyuktasamavāya eva dravye samavetatvāt 2/
ṇ: rūpatvādiṣu guṇasamaveteṣu samyuktasamavetasamavāya eva 3/
ṇ: śabde samavāya evākāśasya śrotratvena vyavasthitatvāt, śabdasya ca tad.guṇatvena tatra samavetatvāt 4/
ṇ: śabdatve samavetasamavāya eva śabde samavetatvāt 5/
ṇ: samavāyābhāvayor viśeṣaṇaviśeṣya.bhāva eva /
ṇ: uktarūpapañcavidhasambandhasambaddheṣu vastuṣu samavāya.ghaṭādi dṛśyābhāvayor viśeṣaṇatvam viśeṣyatvam vā bhavati.ity arthaḥ /
ṇ: tad.yathā -- tantavaḥ paṭasamavāyavantaḥ tantuṣu paṭasamavāya iti /
ṇ: ghaṭa.śūnyam bhūtalam iha bhūtale ghaṭo nāsti.iti 6 ṣoḍhā samnikarṣaḥ /

ṇ18: atha nikarṣa.grahaṇam evāstu sam-grahaṇam (sam.grahaṇam, samgrahaṇam) vyartham, naqqsam.śabda.grahaṇasya samnikarṣa.ṣaṭkapratipādanārthatvāt /
ṇ: etad eva samnikarṣa.ṣaṭkam jñānotpāde samartham kāraṇam, na samyuktasamyogādikam iti 'sam'grahaṇāl labhyate (samgrahaṇāl labhyate) /

ṇ19: indriyārthasamnikarṣād utpannam jātam /
ṇ: utpatti.grahaṇam kārakatva.jñāpakārtham /
ṇ: atrāyam bhāvaḥ -- indriyam hi naikaṭyād arthena saha sambadhyate, indriyārthasambandhāc ca jñānam utpadyate /
ṇ: yad uktam -- ''ātmā sahaiti manasā mana indriyeṇa, svārthena ca.indriyam iti krama eṣa śīghraḥ /
ṇ: yogo 'yam eva manasaḥ kim agamya asti, yasmin mano vrajati tatra gato 'yam ātmā //11//'' [  ]

ṇ20: jñānasamgrahaṇam sukhādinivṛtty.artham sukhādīnām ajñānarūpatvāt /
ṇ: sukhādayo hy āhlādādi.svabhāvā grāhyatayānubhūyante, jñānam tv arthāvagamasvabhāvam grāhakatayānubhūyata iti jñānasukhādyor bhedo 'dhyakṣasiddha eva /

ṇ21: avyavadeśyam nāma.kalpanā.rahitam nāma.kalpanāyām hi śābdam syāt /
ṇ: avyapadeśyapada.grahaṇābhāve hi vyapadeśaḥ śabdas tena.indriyārthasamnikarṣeṇa cobhābhyām yad utpāditam jñānam tad apy adhyakṣaphalam syāt, tan.nivṛtty.artham avyapadeśyapadopādānam /
ṇ: tat.śabda.indriyobhaya.janyatve.api prabhūtaviṣayatvena śabdasya prādhānyāt śābdam iṣyate, na punar adhyakṣam iti /

ṇ22: indriya.janyasya maru.marīcikā.sūdaka.jñānasya, śukti.śakale laka.dhauta.bodhādeś ca (?) nivṛtty.artham avyabhicāri.padopādānam /
ṇ: yad atasmims tad ity utpadyate tad vyabhicāri jñānam, tad.vyavacchedena tasmims tad iti jñānam avyabhicāri /

ṇ23: vyavasīyate.anena.iti vyavasāyo viśeṣa ucyate /
ṇ: viśeṣa.janitam vyavasāyātmakam /
ṇ: athavā vyavasāyātmakam niścayātmakam /
ṇ: etena samśaya.jñānam anekapadārthālambanatvād aniścayātmakatvāc ca pratyakṣaphalam na bhavati.iti jñāpitam /

ṇ24: nanv evam api jñānapadam anarthakam antyaviśeṣaṇābhyām jñānasya labdhatvāt, naqqdharmi.pratipādanārthatvād asya, jñānapadopātto hi dharmi.indriyārthasamnikarṣajatvādibhir viśeṣyate /
ṇ: anyathā dharmy.abhāve kvāvyabhicārādīn dharmāms tat.padāni pratipādayeyuḥ /

ṇ25: kecit punar evam vyācakṣate -- avyapadeśyam vyavasāyātmakam iti pada.dvayena nirvikalpakasavikalpaka.bhedena pratyakṣasya dvaividhyam āha, śeṣāṇi tu jñānaviśeṣaṇāni.iti /

ṇ26: atra ca sūtre phalasvarūpasāmagrī.viśeṣaṇaprakṣās trayaḥ sambhavanti /
ṇ: teṣu svarūpaviśeṣaṇapakṣo na yuktaḥ /
ṇ: yathā.uktaviśeṣaṇam jñānam pratyakṣam iti hi tatrārthaḥ syāt /
ṇ: tathā cākārakasya jñānasya pratyakṣatvapraktih, na cākārakasya pratyakṣatvam yuktam asādhakatamatvāt sādhakatamasyaiva ca pramāṇatvāt /
ṇ: tulā.suvarṇādīnām pradīpādīnām samnikarṣa.indriyādīnām cābodharūpāṇām apratyakṣatvaprasaGgaś ca /
ṇ: iṣyate caiṣām sūtrakṛtā pratyakṣatvam /
ṇ: tn na svarūpaviśeṣaṇapakṣo yuktaḥ /

ṇ27: nāpi sāmagrī.viśeṣaṇapakṣah, sāmagrī.viśeṣaṇapakṣe hy evam sūtrārthaḥ syāt -- pramātṛ.prameya.cakṣur.ādi.indriyālokādikā jñāna.janikā sāmagrī indriyārthasamnikarṣotpannatvādi.viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭa.jñāna.jananād upacāreṇa.indriyārthasamnikarṣotpannatvādi.viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭā satī pratyakṣam it /
ṇ: evam ca sāmagryāḥ sūtropāttaviśeṣaṇa.yogitvam tathāvidhaphala.janakatvād upacāreṇaiva bhavati, na tu svata iti /
ṇ: na tu yuktas tat.pakṣo 'pi /

ṇ28: phalaviśeṣaṇapakṣas tu yukti.saGgataḥ /
ṇ: atra pakṣe 'yatah' ity adhyāhāryam /
ṇ: tato 'yam arthaḥ -- indriyārthasamnikarṣotpannatvādi.viśeṣaṇam jñānam yata indriyārthasamnikarṣāder bhavati, sa indriyārthasamnikarṣādiḥ pratyakṣam pramāṇam /
ṇ: jñānam ca pratyakṣapramāṇaphalam /
ṇ: yadā tu tato 'pi jñānādd. hānopādānādi.buddhaya utpadyante, tadā hānādi.buddhy.apekṣayā jñānam pramāṇam hānādi.buddhayas tu phalam /
ṇ: ''yadā jñānam pramāṇam tadā hānādi.buddhayaḥ phalam'' [ṇBhs 1.1.3] iti vacanāt /
ṇ: yathā cānubhava.jñānavamśa.jāyāḥ smṛtes tathā tathā cāyam ity etaj jñānam indriyārthasamnikarṣajatvāt pratyakṣaphalam /
ṇ: tat.smṛtes tu pratyakṣatā /
ṇ: sukha.duhkhasambandhasmṛtes tv indriyārthasamnikarṣasahakāritvāt tathā cāyam iti sārūpya.jñāna.janakatvenādhyakṣapramāṇatā /
ṇ: sārūpya.jñānasya ca sukhasādhano 'yam ity ānumānikaphala.janakatvenānumānapramāṇatā /
ṇ: na ca sukhasādhanatva.śakti.jñānam indriyārthasamnikarṣajam śakter asaMnihitatvāt /
ṇ: ātmano mana.indriyeṇa (manas) samnikarṣe sukhādi.jñānam phalam /
ṇ: mana.indriyasya (manas) tat.samnikarṣasya ca pratyakṣapramāṇatā /
ṇ: evam anyatrāpi yathārham pramāṇaphalavibhāgo 'vagantavya iti /

ṇ29: etad eva.indriyārthasamnikarṣādi.sūtram grantha.kāraḥ padya.bandhānulomyena.ittham āha /
ṇ: 'indriyārthasamparkotpannam' ityādi /
ṇ: atra samparkaḥ sambandhaḥ /
ṇ: 'avyabhicāri ca' ity atra ca.kāro viśeṣaṇasamuccayārthaḥ /
ṇ: avyabhicārikam iti pāṭhe tv avyabhicāry evāvyabhicārikam svārthe kapratyayaḥ /
ṇ: vyapadeśo nāma.kalpanā / 
ṇ: atrāpi vyākhyāyā 'yatah' ity adhyāhāryam /
ṇ: bhāvārthaḥ sarvo 'pi prāgvad eva.iti /

ṇ30: atha pratyakṣa.tat.phalayor abhedavivakṣayā pratyakṣasya bhedā ucyante /
ṇ: pratyakṣam dvedhā, ayogi.pratyakṣam yogi.pratyakṣam ca /
ṇ: yad asmad.ādīnām indriyārthasamnikarṣāj jñānam utpadyate tad ayogi.pratyakṣam /
ṇ: tad api dvividham nirvikalpam savikalpam ca /
ṇ: tatra vastu.svarūpamātrāvabhāsakam nirvikalpakam yathā prathamākṣasamnipātajam jñānam /
ṇ: samjñā.samjñi.sambandhollekhena jñānotpattinimittam savikalpakam yathā devadatto 'yam daṇḍī.ityādi /

ṇ31: yogi.pratyakṣam tu deśa.kālasvabhāvaviprakṛṣṭārtha.grāhakam /
ṇ: tad dvividham yuktānām pratyakṣam viyuktānām ca /
ṇ: tatra samādhy.aikāgryavatām yogaja.dharma.īśvarādi.sahakṛtād ātmāntahkaraṇasamyogād eva bāhyārthasamyoganirapekṣam yad aśeṣārtha.grahaṇam tad yuktānām pratyakṣam /
ṇ: etac ca nirvikalpakam eva bhavati, vikalpataḥ samādhy.aikāgryānupapatteḥ /
ṇ: idam cotkṛṣṭa.yogina eva vijñeyam yogi.mātrasya tad.asambhavāt /
ṇ: asamādhy.avasthāyām yoginām ātmamano.bāhya.indriyarūpādyāśraya.catuṣkasamyogād rūpādīnām ātmamanah.śrotra.trayasamyogāt śabdasya, ātmamano.dvayasamyogāt sukhādīnām ca yad grahaṇam tad viyuktānām pratyakṣam /
ṇ: tac ca nirvikalpakam savikalpakam ca pratipattavyam /
ṇ: vistarārthinā tu nyāyasāra.ṭīkā vilokanīyā.iti /

ṇ32: athānumānapakṣaṇam āha 'anumāmam tu tat.pūrvakam trividham bhavet śeṣavac caiva' ityādi /
ṇ: atra caiva.śabdau pūrvavad.ādīnām artha.bāhulyasūcakau /
ṇ: tathā.śabdaś ca.kārārthaḥ samuccaye /
ṇ: śeṣam tu sūtravyākhyayā.eva vyākhyāsyate /
ṇ: sūtram tv idam -- ''tat.pūrvakam trividham anumānam, pūrvavat śeṣavat sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam ca'' [  ] iti /
ṇ: eka vyākhyāyanti -- atraikasya pūrvaka.śabdasya sāmānya.śrutyā luptanirdeśo draṣṭavyaḥ /
ṇ: tat.pūrvakam ity atra tat.śabdena pramāṇam abhisambadhyate /
ṇ: tat.pūrvakam pratyakṣaphalam liGga.jñānam ity arthaḥ /
ṇ: tat.pūrvakapūrvakam liGgi.jñānam /
ṇ: ayam atra bhāvaḥ -- pratyakṣād dhūmādi.jñānam utpadyate, dhūmādi.jñānāc ca vahnyādi.jñānam iti /
ṇ: indriyārthasamnikarṣotpannatvavarjāṇi ca jñānādi.viśeṣaṇāni pratyakṣasūtrād atrāpi sambandhanīyam iti /
ṇ: eṣām ca vyavacchedyāni prāg.uktānusāreṇa svayam paribhāvyāni /

ṇ33: tathā dvitīya.liGga.darśanapūrvikāyā avinābhāvasambandhasmṛtes tat.pūrvakapūrvakatvāt taj.janakasyānumānatvanivṛtty.artham arthopalabdhi.grahaṇam kāryam, smṛtes tv artham vināpi bhāvāt /
ṇ: tato 'yam arthaḥ /
ṇ: arthopalabdhi.rūpam avyabhicaritam avyapadeśyam vyavasāyātmakam jñānam tat.pūrvakapūrvakam yato liGgādeḥ samupajāyate tad anumānam iti /
ṇ: tathā te dve pratyakṣe liGga.liGgi.sambandha.darśanam liGga.darśanam ca pūrvam yasya tat.tat.pūrvakam iti vigrahaviśeṣāśrayād anumānasyādhyakṣa.dvayapūrvakatvam jñāpitam draṣṭavyam /
ṇ: tathā tāni pratyakṣādi.sarvapramāṇāni pūrvam yasya tat.tat.pūrvakam iti vigrahaveśeṣāśrayaṇena sarvapramāṇapūrvakatvam apy anumānasya labhyate /
ṇ: na ca teṣām pūrvam aprakṛtatvāt katham tat.śabdena parāmarśa iti preryam /
ṇ: yataḥ sākṣād.aprakṛtatve.api pratyakṣasūtre vyavacchedyatvena prakṛtatvād iti /
ṇ: asyām vyākhyāyām nāvyāptyādi.doṣaḥ kaścanāpi /

ṇ34: ye tu pūrva.śabdasyaikasya luptasya nirdeśam nābhyupagacchanti teṣām pratyakṣaphale.anumānatvaprasaktih, tat.phalasya pratyakṣapramāṇapūrvakatvāt /
ṇ: athākārakasyāpramāṇatvāt kārakatvam labhyate, tato 'yam arthaḥ -- avyabhicāritāvyapadeśyavyavasāyātmikārthopalabdhi.janakam evādhyakṣaphalam liGga.jñānam anumānam iti cet ucyate -- evam api viśiṣṭa.jñānam evānumānam prasajyate /
ṇ: na ca jñānasyaivānumānatvam, ''smṛty.anumānāgamasamśayapratibhā.svapna.jñāna.ūhāḥ sukhādi.pratyakṣam icchā.ādayaś ca manaso liGgāni'' [ṇBhs 1.1.16] iti vacanāt sarvasya bodhābodharūpasya viśiṣṭaphala.janakasyānumānatvād ity avyāptir lakṣaṇa.doṣaḥ /(?)
ṇ: ato 'rthopalabdhir avyabhicārādi.viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭā tat.pūrvakapūrvikā yatas(?) tad anumānam ity eva vyākhyānam yuktimat /

ṇ35: nanv atrāpi trividha.grahaṇam anarthakam iti cet, naqqanumānavibhāgārthatvāt /
ṇ: pūrvavad.ādi.grahaṇam ca svabhāvādi.viṣayapratiṣedhena pūrvavad.ādi.viṣaya.jñāpan.artham / 
ṇ: pūrvavad.ādy eva trividhavibhāgena vivakṣitam na svabhāvādikam iti prathamam vyākhyānam /

ṇ36: apare tv evam sūtram vyācakṣate -- tat.pūrvakam pratyakṣapūrvakam trividham iti tri.bhedam anumānam /
ṇ: ke punar bhedā ity āha -- pūrvavad ityādi /
ṇ: pūrva.śabdenānvayo vyapadiśyate, vyatirekāt prāg.avasīyamānatvāt pūrvo 'nvayah, sa evāsti yasya tat pūrvavat kevalānvayy.anumānam (anvayi.anumānam) //1//
ṇ: śeṣo vyatirekah, sa evāsti yasya tat śeṣavat kevalavyatireka ca //2//
ṇ: sāmānyenānvayavyatirekayoḥ sādhanāGgayor yad dṛṣṭam tat sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam anvayavyatireki ca.iti //3//

ṇ37: athavā trividham iti tri.rūpam / kāni trīṇi rūpāṇi.ity āha pūrvavad ityādi /
ṇ: pūrvam upādīyamānatvāt pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ so 'syāsti.iti pūrvavat pakṣa.dharmatvam /
ṇ: śeṣa upayuktād anyatvāt sādharmya.dṛṣṭāntaḥ so 'sty atra.iti śeṣavat sapakṣe sattvam /
ṇ: sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam iti vipakṣe manāg api yan na dṛṣṭam vipakṣe sarvatrāsattvam tṛtīyam rūpam /
ṇ: ca.śabdāt pratyakṣāgamāviruddhatvāsat.pratipakṣatvarūpa.dvayam ca /
ṇ: evam ca pañcarūpa.liGgālambanam yat tat.pūrvakam tad anvayavyatireky.anumānam (vyatireki.anumānam) /
ṇ: vipakṣāsattvasapakṣasattvayor anyatararūpasyānabhisambandhāt tu catū.rūpa.liGgālambanam (catur.rūpa.liGgālambanam) kevalānvayi kevalavyatireki cānumānam /
ṇ: tatra 'anityaḥ śabdah, kāryatvāt, ghaṭādivad ākāśādivac ca' ity anvayavyatirekī hetuḥ //1//
ṇ: 'adṛṣṭādīni kasyacit pratyakṣāṇi, prameyatvāt, kara.talādivat' ity atra kasyacit pratyakṣatve sādhye.apratyakṣasya kasyāpi vastuno vipakṣasyābhāvād eva kevalānvayī //2//
ṇ: sarvavit.kartṛ.pūrvakam sarvam kāryam, kādācitkatvāt, yat sarvavit.kartṛ.pūrvakam na bhavanti tan na kādācitkam yathā.ākāśādi /
ṇ: atra sarvasya kāryasya pakṣī.kṛtatvād eva sapakṣābhāvāt kevalavyatirekī /
ṇ: prasaGga.dvāreṇa vā kevalavyatirekī /
ṇ: yathā na.idam nirāatmakam jīvat.śarīram aprāṇādimattvaprasaGgāl loṣṭavad it prasaGgaḥ /
ṇ: prayogas tv ittham -- idam jīvat.śarīram sātmakam, prāṇādimattvāt, yan na sātmakam tan na prāṇādimad yathā loṣṭam iti prasaGgapūrvakaḥ kevalavyatireki.iti //3//

ṇ38: evam anumānasya bhedān svarūpam ca vyākhyāya viṣayasya traividhyapratipādanāyaivam āhuḥ -- athavā tat.pūrvakam anumānam trividham tri.prakāram /
ṇ: ke punas trayaḥ prakārā ity āha pūrvavad ityādi, pūrvam kāraṇam vidyate yatrānumāne tat pūrvavat, yatra kāraṇena kāryam anumīyate, yathā viśiṣṭameghonnatyā bhaviṣyati vṛṣṭir iti /
ṇ: atra kāraṇa.śabdena kāraṇa.dharma unnatatvādi.grāhyaḥ /
ṇ: prayogas tv evam, amī meghā vṛṣṭy.utpādakāh, gambhīra.garjitatve.aciraprabhāvatve.(ciraprabhāvatve) ca saty atyunnatatvāt, ya evam te vṛṣṭy.utpādakā yathā vṛṣṭy.utpādakapūrvameghāh, tathā cāmī, tasmāt tathā /

ṇ39: nanu.unnatatvādi.dharma.yuktānām api meghānām vṛṣṭy.ajanakatva.darśanāt katham aikāntikam kāraṇāt kāryānumānam iti cet /
ṇ: na, viśiṣṭasyonnatatvāderi dharmasya gamakatvena vivakṣitatvāt /
ṇ: na ca tasya viśeṣo nāsarvajñena niścetum pāryata iti vaktum śakyam, sarvānumānocchedaprasakteḥ /
ṇ: tathā.hi -- maśakādi.vyāvṛtta.dhūmādīnām api svasādhyāvyabhicāritvam asarvavidā na niścetum śakyam iti vaktum śakyata eva /
ṇ: atha ''suvivecitam kāryam kāraṇam na vyabhicarati'' ity nyāyād dhūmāder gamakatvam, tat tarhy atrāpi samānam /
ṇ: yo hi bhaviṣyad.vṛṣṭy.avyabhicāriṇam unnatatvādi.viśeṣam avagantum samarthaḥ sa eva tasmāt tām anuminoti, nāgṛhītaviśeṣaḥ / tad uktam -- ''anumātur ayam aparādho nānumānasya'' iti /

ṇ40: śeṣaḥ kāryam tad asyāsti tat śeṣavat, yatra kāryeṇa kāraṇam anumīyate, yathā nadī.pūravarśanād vṛṣṭiḥ /
ṇ: atra kārya.śabdena kārya.dharmo liGgam avagantavyam /
ṇ: prayogas tv ittham -- uparivṛṣṭimad deśasambandhinī nadī, śīghratarasrotastve phalaphenasamūha.kāṣṭhādi.vahanatve ca sati pūrṇatvāt tad.anyanadīvat /

ṇ41: sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam nāma akārya.kāraṇa.bhūtenāvinābhāvinā liGgena yatra liGginā.upagamah, yathā balākayā salilasya.iti /
ṇ: prayogas tv ayam -- balākājahad.vṛttiḥ pradeśo jalavān balākāvattvāt, sampratipanna.deśavat /
ṇ: yathā vānyavṛkṣoparidṛṣṭasyādityasyānyaparvatoparidarśanena gater avagamaḥ /
ṇ: prayogaḥ punaḥ -- raver anyatra darśanam gaty.avinābhūtam, anyatra darśanatvāt, devadattāder anyatra darśanavat /
ṇ: atra yathā devadattāder anyatra darśanam vrajyā.pūrvam, tathā.ādityasyāpi.iti, anyatra darśanam ca na gateḥ kāryam samyogāder gati.kāryatvāt /

ṇ42: anye tv evam varṇayanti /
ṇ: samāna.kālasya sparśasya rūpād akārya.kāraṇa.bhūtāt pratipattiḥ sāmānyato.dṛṣṭānumānaprabhavā /
ṇ: atra prayogah, īdṛśasparśam idam vastram evam.vidharūupatvāt, tad.anya.tādṛśavastravat /
ṇ: ekam cūtam phalitam dṛṣṭvā puṣpitā jagati cūtā iti pratipattir vā / prayogas tu, puṣpitā jagati cūtāś cūtatvāt, dṛṣṭa.cūtavad ityādi /

ṇ43: athavā pūrveṇa vyāpti.grāhakapratyakṣeṇa tulyam vartata iti pūrvavat sambandha.grāhakapratyakṣeṇa viṣaya.tulyatvāt kathamcit pariccheda.kriyāyā api tulyatātrānumāne samasti.iti kriyā.tulyatve vateḥ (kriyā.tulyatvavatah) prayogaḥ siddhah, tena pūrvapratipattyā tulyā pratipattir yato bhavati, tat pūrvavad.anumānam / (?)
ṇ: icchā.ādayaḥ paratantrā guṇatvād rūpādivad iti /

ṇ44: śeṣavan nāma pariśeṣah, sa ca prasaktānām pratiṣedhe.anyatra prasaGgāsambhavāt śiṣyamāṇasya sampratyayah, yathā guṇatvād icchā.ādīnām pāratantrye śarīrādiṣu prasakteṣu pratiṣedhaḥ /
ṇ: śarīraviśeṣa.guṇā icchā.ādayo na bhavanti, tad.guṇānām rūpādīnām svaparātmapratyakṣatvena.icchā.ādīnām ca svātmapratyakṣatvena vaidharmyāt /
ṇ: nāpi.indriyāṇām viṣayāṇām vā guṇā upahateṣv apy anusmaraṇa.darśanāt /
ṇ: na cānyasya prasaktir asti, ataḥ pariśeṣād ātmasiddhiḥ /
ṇ: prayogaś cātra, yo 'sau paraḥ sa ātma.śabdavācyah, icchā.ādyādhāratvāt /
ṇ: ye tv ātma.śabdavācyā na bhavanti, ta icchā.ādyādhārā api na bhavanti, yathā śarīrādayaḥ /
ṇ: atra pratyakṣeṇāgṛhītvānvayam kevalavyatireka.balād ātmanaḥ pramā śeṣavataḥ phalam /

ṇ45: yatra dharmī sādhana.dharmaś ca pratyakṣaḥ sādhya.dharmaś ca sarvadā pratyakṣaḥ sādhyate tat sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam /
ṇ: yathā.icchā.ādayaḥ paratantrā guṇatvād rūpavat /
ṇ: upalabdhir vā karaṇasādhyā kriyātvāt chidi.kriyāvat /
ṇ: asādhāraṇa.kāraṇapūrvakam jagad vaicitryam citratvāc citrādi.vaicitryavad ityādi sāmānyato.dṛṣṭasyānekam udāharaṇam mantavyam /

ṇ46: nanu sādhya.dharmasya sarvadā.pratyakṣatvena sādhyena hetoḥ katham vyāpti.grahaṇam iti cet, ucyate /
ṇ: dharmiṇa icchā.ādeḥ pratyakṣapratipannatvam guṇatva.kāryatvāder api sādhanasya tad.dharmatvam pratipannam eva /
ṇ: pāratantryeṇa ca svasādhyena tasya vyāptir adhyakṣato rūpādiṣv avagatā.eva /
ṇ: sādhyavyāvṛttyā sādhanavyāvṛttir api pramāṇāntarād evāvagatā /

ṇ47: nanv evam pūrvavat.śeṣavat.sāmānyato.dṛṣṭānām parasparataḥ ko viśeṣaḥ /
ṇ: ucyate -- icchā.ādeḥ pāratantryamātrapratipattau guṇatvam kāryatvam vā pūrvavat, tad evāśrayāntara.bādhayā viśiṣṭāśrayatvena bādhakena pramāṇenāvasīyamānam śeṣavataḥ phalam, tasya sādhya.dharmasya dharmy.antare pratyakṣasyāpi tatra dharmiṇi sarvadā.pratyakṣatvam sāmānyato.dṛṣṭavyapadeśanibandhanam /atas trayāṇām ekam evodāharaṇam /

ṇ48: tad evam kāraṇādi.traividhyāt tri.prakāram liGgam pramitim janayat tat.pūrvakam sad.anumānam(?) iti dvitīyam vyākhyātam /
ṇ: atra vyākhyā.dvaye prathamavyākhyānam eva bahūnām adhyayanaprabhṛtīnām abhimatam /
ṇ: tatra ca pūrvavad.ādīnām vyākhyā dvitīyavyākhyāne yā catuh.prakārābhihitā sā.eva draṣṭavyā.iti /
ṇ: atha śāstra.kāra eva bālānām asammohārtham śeṣavyākhyā.prakārān upekṣyānumānasya trividhasya viṣaya.jñāpanāya pūrvavad.ādīni padāni vyākhyānayann āha 'tatrādyam' ityādi /
ṇ: tatra teṣu pūrvavad.ādiṣv ādyam pūrvavad.anumānam kim ity āha -- kāraṇāl liGgāt kāryasya liGgino 'numānam jñānam kāryānumānam, ihānumānaprastāve, gīyate procyate /
ṇ: kāraṇāt kāryam anumānam ihoditam iti pāṭho vā /
ṇ: tatrāsti.iti.śabdādhyāhāre kāraṇāt kāryam asti.ity anumānam /
ṇ: kāraṇāt kāryam asti.iti jñānam ihānumānaprastāva uditam proktam /
ṇ: pāṭha.dvaye.apy atra yal liGgi.jñānam anumāna.śabdena.ūce, tad dvitīyavyākhyānakāriṇām matena, na tu prathamavyākhyāna.kartṛ.matena /
ṇ: prathamavyākhyākāri.matena hi jñānasya hetur evānumāna.śabdavācyaḥ syāt /
ṇ: evam śeṣavaty api jñeyam /
ṇ: yatra kāraṇāt sva.jñānaviśiṣṭāt kāryasya jñānam bhavati, tat pūrvavad.anumānam /
ṇ: atra hy arthopalabdhi.hetuḥ pramāṇam iti vacanāt kārya.jñānam anumānasya phalam, tadd.hetus tv anumānam pramāṇam /
ṇ: tenātra kāraṇam vā taj.jñānam vā kārya.kāraṇapratibandhasmaraṇam vā kāryam jñāpayat pūrvavad.anumānam iti (jñāpayat.pūrvavad.anumānam) //17-18-19//

ṇ49: tasyodāharaṇam / yathā --
ṇ: rolamba.gavalavyāla.tamālamalina.tviṣaḥ /
ṇ: vṛṣṭim vyabhicaranti.iha naivam.prāyāḥ payo.mucaḥ //20//
ṇ: vyākhyā -- 'yathā' iti nidarśana.darśanārthaḥ /
ṇ: rolambā bhramarāh, gavalā araṇya.jātamahiṣāh, vyālā duṣṭa.gajāḥ sarpāś ca, tamālās tāpicchavṛkṣāḥ /
ṇ: tadvan malināḥ śyāmalās tviṣaḥ kāntayo yeṣām te tathā /
ṇ: etena meghānām kāntimattā vacanenānirvacanīyā kāpy atiśaya.śyāmatā vyajyate, 'evam.prāyāh' evam.śabda idam.prakāravacanaḥ /
ṇ: prāya.śabdo bāhulyavācakaḥ /
ṇ: tata evam idam prakārāṇām prāyo bāhulyam yeṣu ta evam.prāyā īdṛk.prakāra.bāhulyā ity.arthaḥ /
ṇ: etena gambhīra.garjitatvāciraprabhāvavattva.(ciraprabhāvavattva).ādi.prakārāṇām bāhulyam megheṣu sat sūcitam /
ṇ: uktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭā meghā iha jane vṛṣṭim na vyabhicaranti, vṛṣṭi.karā eva bhavanti.ity.arthaḥ /
ṇ: prayogas tu sūtravyākhyāvasarokta evātrāpi vaktavyaḥ //20//

ṇ50: atha śeṣavad.vyākhyānam āha /
ṇ: kāryāt kāraṇānumānam yac ca tat śeṣavat matam /
ṇ: tathā.vidhanadī.pūrād devo vṛṣṭo yathā.upari //21//

ṇ51: vyākhyā -- kāryāl liGgāt kāraṇasya liGgino 'numānam jñānam yat, ca.kāraḥ prāg.uktapūrvavad.apekṣayā samuccaye, tat śeṣavat matam /
ṇ: ayam atra tattvārthaḥ / yatra kāryāt kāraṇa.jñānam bhavati, tat śeṣavad.anumānam /
ṇ: atrāpi prāgvat kāraṇa.jñānasya hetuḥ kāryam kārya.darśanam tat.sambandhasmaraṇam cānumāna.śabdena prtipattavyam /
ṇ: yathā.ity udāharaṇopanyāsārthaḥ prathamam atra yojyaḥ /
ṇ: tathā.vidhaḥ śīghratarasrotastvaphalaphenādi.vahanatvobhaya.taṭavyāpitva.dharmaviśiṣṭo yo nadī.pūras tasmāl liGgād upari.deśe devo megho vṛṣṭa iti jñānam / atra prayogaḥ prāgvat //21//
ṇ: yac ca sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam tad evam gati.pūrvikā /
ṇ: pumsi deśāntaraprāptir yathā sūrye.api sā tathā //22/

ṇ52: vyākhyā -- caḥ punar.arthe, yat punaḥ kārya.kāraṇa.bhāvād anyatra sāmānyato 'vinābhāva.balena dṛṣṭam liGgam sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam, tad evam /
ṇ: katham ity āha -- yathā pumsy ekasmād deśād deśāntaraprāptir gati.pūrvikā tathā sūryo 'pi sā deśāntaraprāptis tathā gati.pūrvikā /
ṇ: atra deśāntaraprāpti.śabdena deśāntara.darśanam jñeyam /
ṇ: anyathā deśāntaraprāpter gati.kāryatvena śeṣavato 'numānād asya bhedo na syāt /
ṇ: yady api gagane samcarataḥ sūryasya netrāvalokaprasarābhāvena gatir nopalabhyate, tathāpy udayācalāt kālalantareṇastācala.cūlikā.ādau tad.darśanam gatim gamayati / prayogaḥ punaḥ pūrvam ukta eva /

ṇ53: athavā deśāntaraprāpter gati.kāryatvam loko na pratyeti.iti idam udāharaṇam kārya.kāraṇa.bhāvāvivakṣayātropanyastam etat.prayogas tv evam, sūryasya deśāntaraprāptir gati.pūrvikā deśāntaraprāptitvād devadatta.deśāntaraprāptivat //22/

ṇ54: upamāna.lakṣaṇam āha --
ṇ: prasiddhavastu.sādharmyād aprasiddhasya sādhanam /
ṇ: upamānam samākhyātam yathā gaur gavayas tathā //23//

ṇ55: vyākhyā -- ''prasiddhasādharmyāt sādhyasādhanam upamānam'' [ṇṣ 1.1.6] iti sūtram /
ṇ: atra yata ity adhyāhāryam, tataś ca prasiddhena vastunā gavā yat sādharmyam samāna.dharmatvam tasmāt prasiddhavastu.sādharmād aprasiddhasya gavaya.gatasya sādhyasya samjñā.samjñi.sambandhasya sādhanam pratipattir yataḥ sādharmya.jñānād bhavati tad upamānam samākhyātam /
ṇ: sādharmyasya ca prasiddhir āgamapūrvikā /
ṇ: tata āgamasamsūcanāyāha -- yathā gaus tathā gavaya iti /
ṇ: gavayo 'raṇya.gavayaḥ /
ṇ: ayam atra bhāvaḥ -- kaścit prabhuṇā gavayānayanāya preṣitas tad.artham ajājānas tam evāprākṣīt kīdṛg gavaya iti, sa proce yādṛg gaus tādṛg gavaya iti /
ṇ: tataḥ so 'raṇye paribhraman samānam artham yadā paśyati, tadā tasya tad.vākyārthasmṛti.sahāya.indriyārthasamnikarṣād go.sadṛśo 'yam iti yat sārūpya.jñānam utpadyate, tat prtyakṣaphalam, tad evāvyabhicāryādi.viśeṣaṇamayam sa gavaya.śabdavācya iti samjñā.samjñi.sambandhapratipattim janayad upamānam /
ṇ: samjñā.samjñi.sambandhapratipattis tu.upamānasya phalam /
ṇ: na punar āgamiko sā, śabdasya taj.janakasya tadānīm abhāvāt /
ṇ: gavayapiṇḍaviṣaye ca heyādi.jñānam yad utpadyate tad indriyārthasamnikarṣa.janyatvāt pratyakṣaphalam //23//

ṇ53: atha turyam śābdam āha --
ṇ: śābdam āptopadeśas tu mānam evam catur.vidham /
ṇ: prameyam tv ātma.dehādyam buddhi.indriyasukhādi ca //24//

ṇ54: vyākhyā -- śabda.janitam śābdam āgama ity.arthaḥ /
ṇ: tur bhinna.krame, śābdam tu pramāṇam āptopadeśaḥ /
ṇ: āpa ekāntena satyavādī hitaś ca, tasyopadeśo vacanam āptopadeśaḥ /
ṇ: taj.janitam tu jñānam śābdasya phalam /
ṇ: mānam pramāṇam evam uktavidhinā catur.vidham /

ṇ56: tad evam prathamam pramāṇa.tattvam vyākhyāya samprati dvitīyam prameya.tattvam vyākhyātum āha --
ṇ: ''prameyam tv ātma.dehādyam'' prameyam tu pramāṇaphalasya grāhyam punar ātma.dehādyam, ātmā jīvah, deho vapuh, tāv ādyau yasya tad ātma.dehādyam /
ṇ: buddhi.indriyasukhādi ca prameyam /
ṇ: buddhir jñānam, indriyam cakṣur.ādi.manah.paryantam, sukham sātam tāny ādir yasya tad buddhi.indriyasukhādi /
ṇ: ca.kāra ātma.dehādy.apekṣayā samuccaye /
ṇ: 'atra viśeṣaṇa.dvaya ādya.śabdenādi.śabdena ca śeṣāṇām api saptānām prameeyānām (pādam) samgraho draṣṭavyaḥ /
ṇ: tathā ca naiyāyikasūtram -- ātma.śarīra.indriya.buddhi.manah.pravṛtti.doṣapretya.bhāvaphala.duhkhāpavarga.bhedena dvādaśavidham tad iti prameyam'' [ṇṣ 1.1.9] /
ṇ: tatra śarīrādi.duhkhaparyantam heyam, apavarga upādeyah, ātmā tukathamcidd heyah, kathamcid upādeyah, sukha.duhkhādi bhoktṛtayā heyaḥ tad.unmuktatayā.upādeya iti /
ṇ: tatra.icchā.dveṣaprayatnasukha.duhkha.jñānādīnām āśraya ātmā /
ṇ: sacetanatva.kartṛtvasarva.gatatvādi.dharmair ātmā pratīyate 1/
ṇ: tad.bhogāyatanam śarīram 2/
ṇ: pañca.indriyāṇi ghrāṇarasana.cakṣus.tvak.śrotrāṇi 3/
ṇ: pañcārthā rūparasa.gandhasparśa.śabdāḥ /
ṇ: tatra gandharasarūpasparśāś catvāraḥ pṛthivī.guṇāh, rūparasasparśās trayo 'pām guṇāh, rūpasparśau tejaso guṇau, ekaḥ sparśo vāyor guṇah, śabda ākāśasya guṇa iti 4/
ṇ: buddhir upalabdhir jñānam ity.arthah, sā kṣaṇikā, bhogas.svabhāvatvāc ca samsāra.kāraṇam iti heyā 5/
ṇ: indriyārthasamnikarṣe saty api yugapaj.jñānānutpāddād āntarasukhādi.viṣayopalabdheś ca bāhya.gandhādi.viṣayopalabdhivat karaṇasādhyatvād āntaram karaṇam mano 'numīyate, tat sarvaviṣayam tac cāṇu vegavad āśu.samcāri nityam ca 6/
ṇ: vān.manah.kāyavyāpāraḥ (vāc.manas.kāya) śubhāśubhaphalaḥ pravṛttiḥ 7/
ṇ: rāga.dveṣamohās trayo doṣāh, īrṣyā.ādīnām eteṣv evāntar.bhāvah, tat.kṛtaś caiṣa samsāraḥ 8/
ṇ: pravṛtti.doṣa.janitam sukha.duhkhātmakam mukhyam phalam, tat.sādhanam tu gauṇam 10/
ṇ: pīḍā.samtāpasvabhāvajam duhkham, phala.grahaṇenākṣiptam api.idam sukhasyāpi duhkhāvinābhāvitvāt duhkhatva.bhāvanārtham upadiśyate //11/
ṇ: ātyantiko duhkhaviyogo 'pavargah, sarva.guṇaviyuktasyātmanaḥ svarūpeṇāvasthānam /
ṇ: sukha.duhkhayor vivekena hānasyāśakyatvād duhkham jihāsuḥ sukham api jahyād /
ṇ: yasmāj janma.jarāmaraṇaprabandhocchedarūpaḥ paramaḥ puruṣārtho 'pavargah, sa ca tattva.jñānād avāpyate 12 //24//

ṇ59: samśayaprayojanayoḥ svarūpam prāha --
ṇ: kim etad iti samdigdhaḥ pratyayaḥ samśayo mataḥ /
ṇ: pravartate tad.arthitvāt tata sādhyam prayojanam //25//

ṇ60: vyākhyā -- ayam kim.śabdo 'sti kṣepe 'kim.sakhā yo 'bhidruhyati' asti praśne 'kim te priyam' asti nivāraṇe 'kim te ruditena' asty apalāpe 'kim te.aham dhārayāmi' (?) asty anunaye 'kim te.aham priyam karomi' asty avajñāne 'kas tvām ullāpayate' asti vitarke 'kim idam dūre dṛśyate' iha tu vitarke dūrāvalokanena padārthasāmānyam avabudhyamānas tad.viśeṣam samdihāno vitarkayati, 'etat pratyakṣam ūrdhvasthitam vastu kim, tarke sthāṇur vā puruṣo vā' iti /
ṇ: yaḥ samdigdho 'neka.koṭi.parāmarśī pratyato vimarśah, sa samśayo mataḥ sammata iti //

ṇ61: atha prayojanam, yad.arthitvād yasya phalasyārthitvam abhilāṣukatve yad.arthitvam, tasmāt pravartate tat.tadīyasādhaneṣu yatnam kurute, tat tu tat punaḥ sādhyam kartavyatayā.iṣṭam prayojanam phalam yasya vāñchayā kṛtyeṣu pravartate tat prayojanam ity.arthaḥ /
ṇ: prayojanamūlatvāc ca pramāṇopanyāsapravṛtteḥ prameyāntar.bhūtam api prayojanam pṛthag upadiśyate //25//

ṇ62: atha dṛṣṭāntasiddhāntau vyācikhyāsur āha --
ṇ: dṛṣṭāntas tu bhaved eṣa vivādaviṣayo na yaḥ /
ṇ: siddhāntas tu catur.bhedaḥ sarva.tantrādi.bhedataḥ //26//

ṇ63: vyākhyā -- dṛṣṭo 'nto niścayo 'tra.iti dṛṣṭāntah, dṛṣṭāntaḥ punar eṣo 'yam bhavet /
ṇ: eṣa ka ity āha -- ya upanyastaḥ san vivādaviṣayo vādi.prativādinor mitho viruddho vādo vivādah, tasya viṣayo gocaro na bhavati, vādi.prativādinor ubhayoḥ sammata evānumānādau dṛṣṭānta upanyastavya ity arthaḥ /
ṇ: pañcasv avayaveṣu vakṣyamāṇo 'pi dṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhyasādhana.dharmayoḥ pratibandha.grahaṇasthānam iti pṛthag ihopadiśyate /
ṇ: tāvad eva hy anvayavyatireka.yukto 'rthaḥ skhalati, yāvan na spaṣṭa.dṛṣṭāntāvaṣṭambhaḥ /
ṇ: uktam ca -- ''tāvad eva calaty artho mantur viṣayam āgataḥ /
ṇ: yāvan nottambhanenaiva dṛṣṭāntenāvalambyate //1//''

ṇ64: siddhāntas tu siddhāntaḥ punaś catur.bhedo bhavet /
ṇ: kuta ity āha -- sarva.tantrādi.bhedataḥ sarva.tantrādi.bhedena /
ṇ: prathamaḥ sarva.tantrasiddhāntah, ādi.śabdāt pratitantrasiddhānto 'dhikaraṇasiddhānto 'bhyupagamasiddhāntaś ca veditavyaḥ /
ṇ: iha tantra.śabdena śāstram vijñeyam /
ṇ: tatra sarva.tantrāviruddhaḥ svatatantre.adhikṛto 'rthaḥ sarva.tantrasiddhāntaḥ sarveṣām śāstrāṇām sampratipatti.viṣayah, yathā pramāṇāni prameyasādhanāni, ghrāṇādīni.indriyāṇi, gandhādayas tad.arthāh, pramāṇena prameyasya pariccheda ityādi /
ṇ: samāna.tantraprasiddhaḥ paratantrāsiddhaḥ pratitantrasiddhāntaḥ yathā bhautikāni.indriyāṇi yaugānām kāṇādādīnām ca, abhautikāni sāmkhyānām /
ṇ: tathā sāmkhyānām sarvam sad evotpadyate na punar asat, naiyāyikādīnām sarvam asad evotpadyate sāmagrī.vaśāt, jainānām tu sad.asad utpadyata ityādi /
ṇ: yasya siddhāv anyasya prakriyamāṇasya pratijñārthasya prasaGgenādhikasya siddhih, so 'dhikaraṇasiddhāntah, yathā kāryatvādeḥ kṣityādau buddhimat.kāraṇasāmānyasiddhāv anyasya tat.kāraṇasamarthasya nitya.jñāna.icchā.prayatnādhārasya tat.kāraṇasya siddhir iti /
ṇ: prauḍhavādibhiḥ sva.buddhy.atiśaya.cikhyāpayiṣayā yat.kimcid vastv aparīkṣitam abhyupagamya viśeṣaḥ parīkṣyate, so 'bhyupagamasiddhāntah, yathāstu dravyam śabdah, sa tu kim nityo 'nityo vā.iti śabdasya dravyatvam aniṣṭam abhyupagamya nityānityatvaviśeṣaḥ parīkṣyate / evam caturvidhaḥ siddhāntaḥ //26//

ṇ65: avayavādi.tattva.trayasvarūpam prarūpayati /
ṇ: pratijñā.hetu.dṛṣṭāntopanayā nigamas tathā / 
ṇ: avayavāḥ pañca tarkaḥ samdehoparame bhavet //27//
ṇ: ūrdhvam samdeha.tarkābhyām pratyayo nirṇayo mataḥ //28// yugmam //

ṇ66: vyākhyā -- avayavāḥ pañca, ke pañca.ity āha pratijñā hetur dṛṣṭānta upanayo nigama.śabdena nigamanam ca.iti /
ṇ: tatra pratijñā pakṣaḥ dharma.dharmi.vacanam, kṛṣānumān ayam sānumān ityādi /
ṇ: hetuḥ sādhanam liGgavacanam, dhūmavattvād ityādi /
ṇ: dṛṣṭānta udāharaṇābhidhānam, tad dvividham, anvayamukhena vyatirekamukhena ca /
ṇ: anvayamukhena yathā, yo yo dhūmavān sa sa kṛśānumān, yathā mahānasam ityādi /
ṇ: vyatirekamukhena yathā, yo yaḥ kṛśānumān na bhavati sa sa dhūmavān na bhavati, yathā jalam ityādi /
ṇ: upanayo hetor upasamhārakam vacanam, dhūmavāmś cāyam ityādi /
ṇ: nigamanam hetu.upadeśena sādhya.dharmopasamharaṇam, dhūmavattvāt kṛśānumān ityādi /

ṇ67: atha tarka.tattvam / 'tarkaḥ samdehoparame bhavet' /
ṇ: samyag.vastu.svarūpānavabodhe kim ayam sthāṇur vā puruṣo vā.iti samdehaḥ samśayas tasyoparame vyapagame tarko 'nvaya.dharmānveṣaṇarūpo bhavet /
ṇ: katham ity āha -- 'yathā kākādi.ityādi' yathā.ity upadarśane sthāṇunā kīlakena bhāvyam bhavitavyam /
ṇ: hi.śabdo 'tra niścayotprekṣaṇārtho draṣṭavyaḥ /
ṇ: samprati hi vane.atra mānavasyāsambhavāt sthāṇu.dharmāṇām eva darśanāc ca sthāṇur evātra ghaṭata iti /
ṇ: tad uktam -- ''āraṇyam etat savitāstam āgato, na cādhunā sambhavati.iha mānavaḥ / dhruvam tad etena khagādi.bhājā, bhāvyam smarārāti.samānanāmnā (smarārāti) //1//'' ity eṣa tarkaḥ / (?)

ṇ68: atha nirṇaya.tattvam āha -- 'ūrdhvam ityādi' pūrvoktasvarūpābhyām samdeha.tarkābhyām ūrdhvam anantaram yaḥ pratyayaḥ sthāṇur evāyam puruṣa eva vā.iti pratītiḥ sa nirṇayo niścayo mato 'bhīṣṭaḥ /
ṇ: yat.tadāv arthasambandhād anuktāv api kvacana gamyete, tenātra tau vyākhyātau / 
ṇ: evam anyatrāpi mantavyam //27-28//

ṇ69: atha vāda.tattvam āha --
ṇ: ācārya.śiṣyayoḥ pakṣapratipakṣaparigrahāt /
ṇ: yā kathābhyāsa.hetuḥ syād asau vāda udāhṛtaḥ //29//

ṇ70: vyākhyā -- vādi.prativādinoḥ pakṣapratipakṣaparigrahaḥ kathā, sā dvividhā, vītarāga.kathā vijigīṣu.kathā ca /
ṇ: yatr avītarāgeṇa guruṇā saha śiṣyas tattvanirṇayārtham sādhanopalambhau karoti, sādhanam svapakṣe, upālambhaś ca parapakṣe.anumānasya dūṣaṇam, sā vītarāga.kathā vādasamjñayā.evocyate /
ṇ: vādam pratipakṣasthāpanā.hīnam api kuryāt /
ṇ: praśna.dvāreṇaiva yatra vijigīṣur jigīṣuṇā saha lābhapūjā.khyāti.kāmo jayaparājayārtham pravartate, vītarāgo vā parānugrahārtham jñānāGkurasamrakṣaṇārtham ca pravartate, sā catur.aGgā vādi.prativādi.sabhāpati.prāśnikāGgā vijigīṣu.kathā jalpavitaṇḍā.samjñā.uktā / 
ṇ: tathā coktam -- ''tattvādhyavasāyasamrakṣaṇārtham jalpavitaṇḍe, bījaprarohasamrakṣaṇārtham kaṇṭaka.śākhā.āvaraṇavat'' [ṇṣ 4.2.50] iti /
ṇ: ''yathā.ukta.lakṣaṇopapannaś chala.jātinigrahasthānasādhanopālambho jalpaḥ / sa pratipakṣasthāpanā.hīno vitaṇḍā'' [ṇṣ 1.2.2-3] iti/ vāda.jalpavitaṇḍānām vyaktiḥ /

ṇ71: atha prakṛtam prastumah, ācāryo 'dhyāpako guruh, śiṣyo 'dhyetā vineyaḥ /
ṇ: tayor ācārya.śiṣyayoḥ 'pakṣapratipakṣaparigrahāt' pakṣaḥ pūrvapakṣaḥ pratijñā.ādi.samgrahah, pratipakṣa uttarapakṣaḥ pūrvapakṣapratipanthī pakṣa ity arthah, tayoḥ parigrahāt svīkārād abhyāsasya hetur abhyāsa.kāraṇam yā kathā prāmāṇikī vārttā asau kathā vāda udāhṛtaḥ kīrtitaḥ /
ṇ: ācāryaḥ pūrvapakṣam svīkṛtyācaṣṭe śiṣyaś cottarapakṣam urarīkṛtya pūrvapakṣam khaṇḍayati /
ṇ: evam pakṣapratipakṣasamgraheṇa nigrāhakasabhāpati.jayaparājaya.chala.jātyādy.anapekṣatayābhyāsārtham yatra guru.śiṣyau goṣṭhīm kurutah, sa vādo vijñeyaḥ //29//

ṇ72: atha jalpavitaṇḍe vivṛṇoti --
ṇ: vijigīṣu.kathā yā tu chala.jātyādi.dūṣaṇā /
ṇ: sa jalpaḥ sā vitaṇḍā tu yā pratipakṣavarjitā //30//

ṇ73: vyākhyā -- yā tu yā punar vijigīṣu.kathā vijayābhilāṣibhyām vādi.prativādibhyām prārabdhā pramāṇa.goṣṭhī, katham.bhūtā, chalāni jātayaś ca vaṣkyamāṇa.lakṣaṇāni, ādi.śabdān nigrahasthānādi.parigrahah, etaiḥ kṛtvā dūṣaṇam paropanyastapakṣāder doṣotpādanam yasyām sā chala.jātyādi.dūṣaṇā, sa vijigīṣu.kathā.rūpo jalpaḥ /
ṇ: 'udāhṛta' iti pūrva.ślokāt sambandhanīyam /

ṇ74: nanu chala.jātyādibhiḥ parapakṣāder dūṣaṇotpādanam satām kartum na yuktam iti cet na /
ṇ: san.mārgapratipattinimittam tasyāhyanujñātatvāt /
ṇ: anujñātam hi svapakṣasthāpanena san.mārgapratipattinimittatayā chala.jātyādy.upanyāsair api paraprayogasya dūṣaṇotpādanam / tatha coktam ---

ṇ: ''duhśikṣita.kutarkāmśaleśavācālitānanāḥ / (word-cut suspended)
ṇ: śakyāḥ kim anyathā jtum vitaṇḍā.āṭopamaṇḍitāḥ //1//
ṇ: gatānugatiko lokaḥ kumārgam tat.pratāritaḥ /
ṇ: mārgād iti chalādīni prāha kāruṇiko muniḥ //2//'' [ṇyāyamapramā.pṛ. 11] iti /
ṇ: samkaṭe prastāve ca sati chalādibhir api svapakṣasthāpanam anumatam /
ṇ: paravijaye hi dharma.dhvamsādi.doṣasambhavah, tasmād varam chalādibhir api jayaḥ /

ṇ75: 'sā vitaṇḍā tv ityādi' tu.śabdo 'vadhāraṇārtho bhinna.kramaś ca /
ṇ: sā tu sā.eva vijigīṣu.kathā.eva pratipakṣavivarjitā vādi.prayuktapakṣapratipanthī prativādy.upanyāsaḥ pratipakṣas tena vivarjitā rahitā pratipakṣasādhana.hīnā.ity.arthah, vitaṇḍā.udāhṛtā /
ṇ: vaitaṇḍiko hi svāmy.upagatapakṣam asthāpayan yat.kimcid vādena paroktam eva dūṣayati.ity.arthaḥ //30//
ṇ: atha hetv.ābhāsādi.tattva.trayasvarūpam prakaṭayati --
ṇ: hetv.ābhāsā asiddhādyāś chalam kūpo navodakaḥ /
ṇ: jātayo dūṣaṇābhāsāḥ pakṣādir dūṣyate na yaiḥ //31//

ṇ77: -- asiddhaviruddhānaikāntika.kālātyayāpadiṣṭaprakaraṇasamāḥ pañca hetv.ābhāsāḥ /
ṇ: tatr pakṣa.dharmatvam yasya nāsti so 'siddhah, anityaḥ śabaś cākṣuṣatvād iti 1/
ṇ: vipakṣe san sapakṣe cāsan viruddhah, nityaḥ śabdaḥ kāryatvād iti 2/
ṇ: pakṣādi.trayavṛttir anaikāntikah, anityaḥ śabdaḥ prameyatvād iti 3/
ṇ: hetoḥ prayoga.kālaḥ pratyakṣāgamānupahatapakṣaparigrahasamayas tam atītyāpadiṣṭaḥ prayuktaḥ pratyakṣāgamaviruddhe pakṣe vartamāna ity.arthah, hetuḥ kālātyayāpadiṣṭah, anuṣṇo 'giḥ kṛtakatvāt, brāhmaṇena surā peyā drava.dravyatvāt kṣīravad iti 4/
ṇ: svapakṣasiddhāv iva parapakṣasiddhāv api tri.rūpo hetuḥ prakaraṇasamah, prakaraṇe pakṣe pratipakṣe ca tulya ity.arthaḥ / 
ṇ: anityaḥ śabdaḥ pakṣasapakṣayor anyataratvāt, sapakṣavad ity ekenokte dvitīyaḥ prāha yady anena prakāreṇānityatvam sādhyate, tarhi nityatā.siddir apy astu, yathā nityaḥ śabdaḥ pakṣasapakṣayor anyataratvāt sapakṣavad iti, athavānityaḥ śabdo nitya.dharmānupalabdher ghaṭavat, nityaḥ śabdo 'nitya.dharmānupalabdher ākāśavad iti /
ṇ: na caiteṣ anyatarad api sādhanam balīyo yad itarasya bādhakam ucyate /
ṇ: nigrahasthānāntar.gatā apy amī hetv.ābhāsā nyāyapravivekam kurvanto vāde vastu.śuddhim vidadhati.iti pṛthag evocyante /

ṇ78: ''chalam kūpo navodakah'' iti / paropanyastavāde svābhimata.kalpanayā vacanavighātaś chalam /
ṇ: tat trividham vāk.chalam sāmānya.chalam upacāla.chalam ca /
ṇ: parokte.arthāntara.kalpanā vāk.chalam /
ṇ: yathā navyaḥ kambalo 'sya.ity abhiprāyeṇa nava.kambalo māṇavaka ity ukte chalavādy āha, kuto 'sya navasamkhyāḥ kambalā iti //1//

ṇ71: sambhāvanayātiprasaGgino 'pi sāmānyasyopanyāse hetutvāropaṇena tan.niṣedhaḥ sāmānya.chalam / 
ṇ: yathā aho nu khalv asau brāhmaṇo vidyā.caraṇasampanna iti brāhmaṇastuti.prasaGge kaścid vadati sambhavati brāhmaṇe vidyā.caraṇasampad iti /
ṇ: tat.chalavādī brāhmaṇatvasya hettvam āropya nirākurvann abhiyuGkte /
ṇ: vrātyenānaikāntikam etat, yadi hi brāhmaṇe vidyā.caraṇasampad bhavati, vrātye.api sā bhavet / vrātyo 'pi brāhmaṇa eva.iti //2//

ṇ80: aupacārike prayoge mukhyārtha.kalpanayā pratiṣedha upacāra.chalam /
ṇ: yathā mañcāḥ krośanti.ity ukte chalavādy āha, mañcasthāḥ puruṣāḥ krośanti, na mañcās teṣām acetanatvād iti //3//
ṇ: atha granthakṛt.chalam vyācikhyāsur ādyasya vāk.chalasyodāharaṇam āha, 'kūpo navodaka' ity atra nūtanārthanava.śabdasya prayoge kṛte chalavādī dūṣayati /
ṇ: kuta eka eva kūpo navasamkhyā.udaka iti /
ṇ: anena śeṣa.chala.dvayodāharaṇe api sūcite draṣṭavye iti /

ṇ81: 'jātaya' ityādi, dūṣaṇābhāsā jātayaḥ /
ṇ: adūṣaṇāny apy dūṣaṇavad ābhāsanta iti dūṣaṇābhāsāḥ /
ṇ: yaiḥ pakṣādiḥ pakṣa.hetv.ādir na dūṣyata ābhāsamātratvān na dūṣayitum śakyate, kevalam samyag.hetau hetv.ābhāse vā vādinā prayukte jhag iti tad.doṣa.tattvāpratibhāse (tad.doṣatvāpratibhāse) hetupratibimbanaprāyam kim.api pratyavasthānam jātiḥ /
ṇ: sā ca catur.vimśati.bhedā sādhyādi.pratyavasthāna.bhedena /
ṇ: yathā -- sādharmyavaidharmyotkarṣāpakarṣavarṇyāvarṇyavikakpasādhyaprāpti.aprāpti.prasaGgapratidṛṣṭāntānutpatti.samśayaprakaraṇāhetu.arthāpatti.aviśeṣopalabdhi.anupalabdhinityānitya.kāryasamāḥ /

ṇ82: tatra sādharmyeṇa pratyavasthānam sādharmyasamā jātir bhavati /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabdaḥ kṛtakatvāt ghaṭavad iti prayoge kṛte sādharmyaprayogeṇaiva pratyavasthānam /
ṇ: yady anitya.ghaṭasādharmyāt kṛtakatvād anityaḥ śabda iṣyate, tarhi nityākāśasādharmyād amūrtatvān nityam prāpnoti.iti 1/

ṇ83: vaidharmyeṇa pratyavasthānam vaidharmyasamā jātih, anityaḥ śabdaḥ kṛtakatvāt, ghaṭavad ity atraiva prayoge vaidharmyeṇokte pratyavasthānam /
ṇ: nityaḥ śabdo 'mūrtatvāt, anityam hi mūrtam dṛṣṭam, yathā ghaṭādir iti /
ṇ: yadi hi nityākāśavaidharmyāt kṛtakatvād anitya iṣyate, tarhi ghaṭādy.anityavaidharmyād amūrtatvān nityaḥ prāpnoti, viśeṣābhāvād iti 2/

ṇ84: utkarṣāpakarṣābhyām pratyavasthānam utkarṣāpakarṣasame jātī bhavataḥ / 
ṇ: tatraiva prayoge dṛṣṭāntasādharmyam kimcit sādhya.dharmiṇy āpādayann utkarṣasamām jātim prayuGkte /
ṇ: yadi ghaṭavat kṛtatvād anityaḥ śabdas tarhi ghaṭavad eva mūrto 'pi bhavet /
ṇ: na cen mūrto ghaṭavad anityo 'pi mā bhūd iti śabde dharmāntarotkarṣam āpādayati 3/
ṇ: apakarṣas tu ghaṭaḥ kṛtakaḥ sann aśrāvaṇo dṛṣṭah, evam śabdo 'pi bhavatu /
ṇ: no cet ghaṭavad anityo 'pi mā bhūd iti śabde śrāvaṇatvam apakarṣati 4/

ṇ85: varṇyāvarṇyābhyām pratyavasthānam varṇyāvarṇyasame jātī bhavataḥ /
ṇ: khyāpanīyo varṇyas tad.viparīto 'varṇyas tāv etau varṇyāvarṇyau sādhya.dṛṣṭānta.dharmau viparyasyan varṇyāvarṇyasame jātī prayuGkte /
ṇ: yathā.vidhaḥ śabda.dharmaḥ kṛtakatvādir na tādṛk ghaṭa.dharmo, yādṛk ca ghaṭa.dharmo na tādṛk śabda.dharma iti /
ṇ: sādhya.dharmo dṛṣṭānta.dharmaś ca hi tulyau kartavyau /
ṇ: atra tu viparyāsaḥ / yato yādṛg ghaṭa.dharmaḥ kṛtakatvādir na tādṛk śabda.dharmaḥ / ghaṭasya hy anyādṛśam kumbha.kārādi.janyam kṛtakatvam, śabdasya hi tālv.oṣṭhādi.vyāpārajam iti 5-6/

ṇ86: dharmāntaravikalpena pratyavasthānam vikalpasamā jātiḥ /
ṇ: yathā kṛtakam kimcin mṛdu dṛṣṭam tūla.śayyādi, kimcit tu kaṭhinam kuṭhārādi, evam kṛtakam kimcid anityam bhaviṣyati ghaṭādikam, kimcic ca nityam śabdādi.iti 7/

ṇ87: sādhyasāmyāpādanena pratyavasthānam sādhyasamā jātiḥ /
ṇ: yadi yathā ghaṭaḥ tathā śabdaḥ prāptam tarhi yathā śabdas tathā ghaṭa iti, śabdaś ca sādhya iti ghaṭo 'pi sādhyo bhavet, tataś ca na sādhyaḥ sādhyasya dṛṣṭāntaḥ syāt /
ṇ: na ced evam tathāpi vailakṣaṇyāt sutarām na dṛṣṭānta iti 8/

ṇ88: prāpty.aprāpti.vikalpābhyām pratyavasthānam prāpty.aprāpti.same jātī /
ṇ: yad etat kṛtakatvam sādhanam upanyastam tat kim prāpya sādhyam sādhayaty aprāpya vā /
ṇ: prāpya cet, tarhi dvayor vidyamānayor eva prāptir bhavati na sad.asator iti/ 
ṇ: dvayoś ca sattvāt kim kasya sādhyam sādhanam vā /
ṇ: aprāpya tu sādhanatvam ayuktam atiprasamgād iti 9-10/

ṇ89: prasamgāpādanena pratyavasthānam prasamgasamā jātiḥ /
ṇ: yady anityatve kṛtakatvam sādhanam, tadā kṛtakatve kim sādhanam, tat.sādhane.api kim sādhanam iti 11/

ṇ90: pratidṛṣṭāntena pratyavasthānam pratidṛṣṭāntasamā jātiḥ /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabdaḥ prayatnānantarīyakatvāt, ghaṭavad ity ukte jāti.vādy āha /
ṇ: yathā ghaṭaḥ prayatnānantarīyako 'nityo dṛṣṭah, evam pratidṛṣṭānta ākāśam nityam api prayatnānantarīyakam dṛṣṭam, kūpa.khananaprayatnānantaram tad.upalambhād iti /
ṇ: na ca.idam anaikāntikatvodbhāvanam bhaGgy.antareṇa pratyavasthānāt 12/
ṇ91: anutpattyā pratyavasthānam anutpatti.samā jātiḥ /
ṇ: anutpanne śabdākhye dharmiṇi kṛtakatvam dharmaḥ kva vartate /
ṇ: tad evam hetv.ābhāṣād (ābhāvād) asiddhir anityatvasya.iti 13/
ṇ92: sādharmyasamā vaidharmyasamā vā yā jātiḥ pūrvam udāhāri sā.eva samśayenopasamhriyamāṇā samśayasamā jātir bhavati /
ṇ: kim ghaṭasādharmyāt kṛtakatvād anityaḥ śabda uta tad.vaidharmyād ākāśasādharmyād amūrtatvān nitya iti 14/

ṇ93: dvitīyapakṣotthāpana.buddhyā prayujyamānā sā.eva sādharmyasamā vaidharmyasamā ca jātiḥ prakaraṇasamā bhavati /
ṇ: tatraivānityaḥ śabdaḥ kṛtakatvād ghaṭavad iti prayoge nityaḥ śabdaḥ śrāvaṇatvāt śabdavad iti /
ṇ: udbhāvanaprakāra.bhedamātreṇa ca jātinānātvam draṣṭavyam 15/

ṇ94: traikālyānupapattyā hetoḥ pratyavasthāna.hetu.samā jātiḥ /
ṇ: hetuḥ sādhanam tat.sādhyāt pūrvam paścāt saha vā bhavet /
ṇ: yadi pūrvam asati sādhye tat kasya sādhanam /
ṇ: atha paścāt sādhanam tarhi pūrvam sādhhyam tasmimś ca pūrvasiddhe kim sādhanena /
ṇ: atha yugapat.sādhyasādhane tarhi tayoḥ savya.itara.go.viṣāṇayor iva sādhyasādhana.bhāva eva na bhavet iti 16/

ṇ95: arthāpattyā pratyavasthānam arthāpatti.samā jātiḥ /
ṇ: yady anityasādharmyāt kṛtakatvād anityaḥ śabdo 'rthād āpadyate, tadā nityasādharmyān nitya iti /
ṇ: asti cāsya nityenākāśādinā sādharmyam amūrtatvam ity udbhāvanaprakāra.bheda evāyam iti 17/

ṇ96: aviśeṣāpādanena pratyavasthānam aviśeṣasamā jātiḥ / 
ṇ: yadi śabda.ghaṭayor eko dharmaḥ kṛtakatvam iṣyate, tarhi samāna.dharma.yogāt tayor aviśeṣe tadvad eva sarvapadārthānām aviśeṣaḥ prasajyata iti 18/

ṇ97: upapattyā pratyavasthānam upapatti.samā jātiḥ /
ṇ: kṛtakatvopapattyā śabdasyānityatvam, tarhy amūrtatvopapattyā nityatvam api kasmān na bhavati.iti pakṣa.dvayopapattyānadhyavasāyaparyavasānatvam vivakṣitam ity udbhāvanaprakāra.bheda evāyam 19/

ṇ98: upalabdhyā pratyavasthānam upalabdhi.samā jātiḥ / anityaḥ śabdaḥ prayatnānantarīyakatvād ity ukte pratyavatiṣṭhate /
ṇ: na khalu prayatnānantarīyakatvam anityatve sādhanam /
ṇ: sādhanam hi tad ucyate yena vinā na sādhyam upalabhyate /
ṇ: upalabhyate ca prayatnānantarīyakatvena vināpi vidyud.ādāv anityatvam, śabde.api kvacid vāyu.vega.bhajyamānavanaspatyādi.janye tathā.eva.iti 20/

ṇ99: anupalabdhyā pratyavasthānam anupalabdhi.samā jātiḥ /
ṇ: tathā.eva prayatnānantarīyakatve hetāv upanyaste saty āha jāti.vādī /
ṇ: na prayatna.kāryaḥ śabdaḥ prāgu.uccāraṇād asty evāsau, āvaraṇa.yogāt tu nopalabhyate /
ṇ: āvaraṇānupalambhe.apy anupalambhān nāsty evoccāraṇāt prāk.śabda iti cen na / 
ṇ: atra hi yānupalabdhiḥ sā svātmani vartate na vā /
ṇ: vartate cet tadā yatrāvaraṇe.anupalabdhir vartate, tasyāvaraṇasya yathānupalambhas tathā.āvaraṇānupalabdher apy anupalambhaḥ syāt /
ṇ: āvaraṇānupalabdheś cānupalambhāad abhāvo bhavet /
ṇ: tad.abhāve cāvaraṇopalabdher bhāvo bhavati / tataś ca mṛd.antaritamūla.kīlādivad āvaraṇopalabdhi.kṛtam eva śabdasya prāg.uccāraṇād agrahaṇam /
ṇ: athānupalabdhiḥ svātmani na vartate cet, tarhy anupalabdhiḥ svarūpeṇāpi nāsti /
ṇ: tathāpy anupalabdher abhāva upalabdhi.rūpas tato 'pi śabdasya prāg.uccāraṇād apy astitvam syād iti /
ṇ: dvedhāpi prayatnakāryatvābhāvān nityaḥ śabda iti 21/

ṇ100: sādhyadharmanityānityavikalpena śabdasya nityatvāpādanam nityasamā jātiḥ /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabda iti pratijñāte jātivādī vikalpayati /
ṇ: yeyam anityatā śabdasyocyate sā kim anityā nityā veti /
ṇ: yady anityā tad iyam avaśyam apāyinī.ity anityatāyā apāyān nityaḥ śabdaḥ /
ṇ: athānityatā nityaiva tathāpi dharmasya nityatvāt tasya ca nirāśrayasyānupapattes tad.āśraya.bhūtaḥ śabdo 'pi nitya eva syāt, tasyānityatve tad.dharmasya nityatvāyogāt /
ṇ: ity ubhayathāpi nityaḥ śabda iti 22/

ṇ101: evam sarva.bhāvānām anityatvopapādanena pratyavasthānam anityasamā jātiḥ /
ṇ: ghaṭasādharmyam anityatvena śabdasyāsti.iti tasyānityatvam yadi pratipādyate, tadā ghaṭena sarvapadārthānām asty eva kim.api sādharmyam iti teṣām apy anityatvam syāt /
ṇ: atha padārthāntarāṇām tathā.bhāve.api nānityatvam tarhi śabdasyāpi tan mā bhūd iti, anityatvamātropapādanapūrvakaviśeṣodbhāvanād aviśeṣasāto bhinnā.iyam jātiḥ 23/

ṇ102 qqprayatna.kāryanānātvopanyāsena pratyavasthānam kāryam.samā jātiḥ /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabdaḥ prayatnāntarīyakatvād ity ukte jāti.vādy āha /
ṇ: prayatnasya dvairūpyam dṛṣṭam / kimcid asad eva tena janyate yathā ghaṭādikam /
ṇ: kimcic ca sad evāvaraṇavyudāsādinābhivyajyate yathā mṛd.antaritamūla.kīlakādi(kīlādi) garbha.gataputrādi vā /
ṇ: evam prayatna.kāryanānātvād eṣa  śabdaḥ prayatnena vyajyate janyate vā.iti samśaya iti /
ṇ: samśayāpādanaprakāra.bhedāc ca samśayasamātaḥ kāryasamā jātir bhidhyate 24 (28 ṛrong)/

ṇ103: tad evam udbhāvanaviṣayavikalpa.bhedena jātīnām ānantye.apy asamkīrṇodāharaṇavivakṣayā catur.vimśatir jāti.bhedā ete pradarśitāḥ /
