ṇ104: pratisamādhānam tu sarva.jātīnām pakṣa.dharmatvādy.anumāna.lakṣaṇaparīkṣā.lakṣaṇam eva / (?)
ṇ: na hy avipluta.lakṣaṇe hetvāv evam.prāyāḥ pāmśupātāḥ prabhavanti /
ṇ: kṛtakatvaprayatnānantarīyakatvayoś ca dṛḍha.kṛtapratibandhāt nāvarakādi.kṛtam śabdānupalambhanam api tv anityatva.kṛtam eva /
ṇ: jāti.prayoge ca pareṇa kṛte samyag uttaram eva vaktavyam, na tu pratīpam jāty.uttarair eva pratyavastheyam āsamañjasyaprasamgād iti //31//

ṇ105: atha nigrahasthānam āha /
ṇ: nigrahasthānam ākhyātam paro yena nigṛhyate / 
ṇ: pratijñā.hāni.samnyāsavirodhādi.vibhedataḥ //32//

ṇ106: vyākhyā -- yena kenacit pratijñā.hānyādy.uparodhena paro vipakṣo nigṛhyate, paravādī, vacananigrahe pātyate tan.nigrahasthānam /
ṇ: parājayas tasya sthānam āśrayaḥ kāraṇam ity arthaḥ /
ṇ: ākhyātam kathitam / kuto nāma bhedata ity āha -- pratijñā.hāni.iti.ādi' /
ṇ: hānis tyāgah, samnyāso 'pahnavanam virodho hetor viruddhatā, teṣām dvandve hāni.samnyāsavirodhāḥ /
ṇ: tataḥ pratijñā.śabdena.ittham sambandhāh, pratijñāyāḥ pakṣasya hāni.samnyāsavirodhāḥ pratijñā.hāni.samnyāsavirodhās te ādir yeṣām te pratijñā.hāni.samnyāsavirodhādayah, ādi.śabdena śeṣān api bhedān parāmṛśati, teṣām vibhedato viśiṣṭa.bhedatah, yena pratijñā.hānyādi.dūṣaṇa.jālena paro nigṛhyate, tan.nigrahasthānam ity arthaḥ /

ṇ107: nigrahasthānam ca sāmānyato dvividham, vipratipattir apratipattiś ca /
ṇ: tatra vipratipattiḥ sādhanābhāse sādhana.buddhiḥ dūṣaṇābhāse ca dūṣaṇa.buddhiḥ /
ṇ: apratipattis tu sādhanasya dūṣaṇam dūṣaṇasya cānuddharaṇam 
ṇ: dvidhā hi vādī parājīyate / yathā -- kartavyam apratipadyamāno viparītam vā pratipadyamāna iti vipratipatty.apratipatti.bhedāc ca dvāvimśatir nigrahasthānāni bhavanti /
ṇ: tad.yathā -- pratijñā.hāniḥ pratijñāntaram pratijñā.virodhaḥ pratijñā.samnyāsaḥ hetv.antaram arthāntaram nirarthakam avijñātārtham apārthakam aprāpta.kālam nyūnam adhikam punar.uktam ananubhāṣaṇam ajñānam apratibhā vikṣepaḥ matānujñā paryanuyojyā.upekṣaṇam niranuyojyānuyogo 'pasiddhānto hetv.ābhāsāś ca /
ṇ: atrāpy ananubhāṣaṇam ajñānam apratibhā vikṣepaḥ paryanuyojyopekṣaṇam ity apratipatti.prakārāh, śeṣāś ca vipratipatti.bhedāḥ /

ṇ108: tatra hetāv anaikāntikī.kṛte pratidṛṣṭānta.dharmam sva.dṛṣṭānte.abhyupagacchataḥ pratijñā.hānir nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabda aindriyakatvād ghaṭavad iti sādhanam vādī vadan pareṇa sāmānyam aindriyakam api nityam dṛṣṭam iti hetāv anaikāntikī.kṛte yady evam brūyāt sāmānyavad ghaṭo 'pi nityo bhavatv iti sa evam bruvāṇaḥ śabdānityatvapratijñām jahyāt /
ṇ: śabdo 'pi nitya eva syāt / tataḥ pratijñā.hānyā parājīyate 1/

ṇ109: pratijñātārthapratiṣedhe pareṇa kṛte tatraiva dharmiṇi dharmāntaram sādhanīyam abhidadhataḥ pratijñāntaram nām nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabda aindriyakatvād ity ukte tathā.eva sāmānyena vyabhicāre nodite yadi brūyād yuktam yat sāmānyam aindriyakam nityam tadd hi sarva.gatam asarva.gatas tu śabda iti /
ṇ: so 'yam anityaḥ śabda iti pūrvapratijñātaḥ pratijñāntaram asarva.gataḥ śabda iti pratijānānaḥ pratijñāntareṇa nigṛhīto bhavati 2/

ṇ110: pratijñā.hetvor virodhaḥ pratijñā.virodho nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: guṇavyatiriktam dravyam rūpādibhyo 'rthāntarasyānupalabdher iti so 'yam pratijñā.hetvor virodhaḥ / 
ṇ: yadi hi guṇavyatiriktam dravyam na tarhi rūpādibhyo 'rthāntarasyānupalabdhiḥ /
ṇ: atha rūpādibhyo 'rthāntarasyānupalabdhih, katham guṇavyatiriktam dravyam iti / tad ayam pratijñā.viruddhābhidhānāt parājīyate 3/

ṇ111: pakṣasādhane pareṇa dūṣite tad.uddharaṇāśaktyā pratijñām eva nihnuvānasya pratijñā.samnyāso nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabda aindriyakatvād ity ukte tathā.eva sāmānyenānaikāntikatāyām udbhāvitāyām yadi brūyāt ka evam āha anityaḥ śabda iti pratijñā.samnyāsāt parājito bhavati 4/
ṇ112: aviśeṣābhihite hetau pratiṣiddhe tad.viśeṣaṇam abhidadhato hetv.antaram nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: tasminn eva prayoge tathā.eva sāmānyasya vyabhicāreṇa dūṣite jātimattve sati.ityādi viśeṣaṇam upādadāno (upādāno) hetv.antareṇa nigṛhīto bhavati 5/

ṇ113: prakṛtād arthād anyo 'rtho 'rthāntaram tad anaupāyikam abhidadhato 'rthāntaram nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabdaḥ kṛtakatvād iti hetuḥ /
ṇ: hetur iti ca hinoter dhātos tupratyaye kṛd.antam padam /
ṇ: padam ca nāmākhyātopasarganipāta.bhedāc catur.vidham iti prastutya nāmādīni vyācakṣāṇaḥ prakṛtānupayoginārthāntareṇa nigṛhyata iti 6/

ṇ114: abhidheyarahitavarṇānupūrvī.prayogamātram nirarthakam nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: anityaḥ śabdaḥ ka.ca.ṭa.tapānām ga.ja.ḍa.da.batvāt gha.jha.ḍha.dha.bhavad ity etad api sarvathārtha.śūnyatvāni nigrahāya bhavati sādhyānupayogād vā 7/

ṇ115: yat sādhanavākyam dūṣaṇam vā kimcit trir abhihitam api parṣat.prativādibhyām boddhum na śakyate, tat kliṣṭa.śabdam apratisiddhaprayogam atihrasvoccāritam ity evam.prakāram avijñātārtham nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: asāmarthyasamvaraṇaprakāro hy ayam iti nigṛhyate 8/

ṇ116: pūrvāparāsamgatapadasamūhaprayogād aprtiṣṭhitavākyārtham apārthakam nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: yathā daśa dāḍimāni ṣaḍ.pūpāḥ kuṇḍam ajājinam palalapiṇḍa ity ādi 9/
  
ṇ117: pratijñā.hetu.udāharaṇopanayanigamanavacana.kramam ullamghyāvayavaviparyāsena prayujyamānam anumānavākyam aprāpta.kālam nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati svapratipattivat parapratipatter janane parārthānumāna.kramasyāpagamāt 10/

ṇ118: pañcāvayave vākye prayoktavye tād.anyatamenāpy avayavena hīnam prayuñjānasya nyūnam nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: pratijñā.ādīnām pañcānām api parapratipatti.janmany upayogād iti 11/

ṇ119: ekenaiva hetunā.udāharaṇena vā pratipādite.arthe hetv.antaram udāharaṇāntaram vā vadato 'dhikam nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati, niṣprayojanābhidhānāt 12/

ṇ120: śabdārthayoḥ punar.vacanam punar.uktam nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati, anyatrānuvādāt /
ṇ: śabdapunar.uktam nāma, yatra sa eva śabdaḥ punar uccāryate, yathānityaḥ śabdo 'nityaḥ śabda iti /
ṇ: arthapunar.uktam tu, yatra so 'rthaḥ prathamam anyena śabdenoccāryate punaś ca paryāyāntareṇocyate, yathānityaḥ śabdo vināśī dhvanir iti /
ṇ: anuvāde tu paunaruktyam na doṣah, yathā hetu.upadeśena pratijñāyāḥ punar.vacanam nigamanam iti 13/

ṇ121: parṣadā viditasya vādinā trir abhihitasyāpi yad apratyuccāraṇam, tad ananubhāṣaṇam nāma prativādino nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: apratyuccārayan kim.āśrayam dūṣaṇam abhidadhīta 14/

ṇ122: parṣadā vijñātasyāpi vādi.vākyārthasya prativādinau yad ajñānam tad ajñānam nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati, aviditottaraviṣayo hi kim uttaram brūyāt, na cānanubhāṣaṇam eva.idam jñāte.api vastuny anubhāṣaṇāsāmarthya.darśanāt 15/

ṇ123: parapakṣe gṛhīte.apy anubhāṣite.api tasminn uttarāpratipattir apratibhā nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati 16/

ṇ124: kāryavyāsaGgāt kathā.vicchedo vikṣepo nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati, sisādhayiṣitasyārthasyāśakyasādhanatām avasāya kathām vicchinatti, idam me karaṇīyam parihīyate pīnasena kaṇṭha uparuddha ityādy abhidhāya kathām vicchindan vikṣepeṇa parājīyate 17/

ṇ125: svapakṣe parāpādita.doṣam anuddhṛtya tam eva parapakṣe pratīpam āpādayato matānujñā nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati /
ṇ: cauro bhavān puruṣatvāt prasiddha.cauravad ity ukte bhavān api cauraḥ puruṣatvād iti pratibruvann ātmanaḥ parāpāditam cauratva.doṣam abhyupagatavān bhavati.iti matānujñayā nigṛhyate 18/

ṇ126: nigrahaprāptasyānigrahaḥ paryanuyojyopekṣaṇam nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati, paryanuyojyo nāma nigrahopapattyāvaśyam nodanīya (?) idam te nigrahasthānam upanatam ato nigṛhīto 'si.iti vacanīyah, tam upekṣya na nigṛhṇāti yaḥ sa paryanuyojyopekṣaṇena nigṛhyate 19/

ṇ127: anigrahasthāne nigrahasthānānuyogo niranuyojyānuyogo nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati, upapannavādinam apramādinam anigrahārham api nigṛhīto 'si.iti yo brūyāt, sa evam sad.bhūta.doṣodbhāvanayā nigṛhyate 20/

ṇ128: siddhāntam abhyupetyāniyamāt kathā.prasaGgo 'pasiddhānto nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati, yaḥ prathamam kimcit siddhāntam abhyupagamya kathām upakramate tatra ca sisādhayiṣitārthasādanāya vā paropalambhāya vā siddhāntaviruddham abhidhatte, so 'pasiddhāantena nigṛhyate, yathā mīmāmsām abhyupagamya kaścid agni.hotram svargasādhanam ity āha katham punar agni.hotra.kriyā dhvastā satī svargasya sādhikā bhavati.ity anuyuktaḥ prāha anayā kriyayā.ārādhito mahā.īśvaraḥ phalam dadāti rājādivat iti, tasya mīmāmsānabhimata.īśvarasvīkārād apaviddho nāma nigrahasthānam bhavati 21/

ṇ129: hetv.ābhāsāś ca yathā.uktā asiddhaviruddhādayo nigrahasthānam 22/
ṇ: iti bhedāntarānantye.api nigrahasthānānām dvāvimśatir mūla.bhedā niveditā iti /

ṇ130: tad evam chala.jātinigrahasthānasvarūpa.bhedābhijñaḥ svavākye tāni varjayan paraprayuktāni samādadhad yathābhitamasādhyasiddhim labhata iti / (these ślokas are absent in ṣuali ed.)
ṇ: jātinigrahasthānām samgraha.ślokā yathā --
ṇ: sādharmyam atha vaidharmyam utkarṣaś cāpakarṣakaḥ /
ṇ: varṇyāvarṇyavikalpāś ca sādhyaprāpty.anavāptayaḥ //1//
ṇ: prasaGgaḥ pratidṛṣṭānto 'nutpattiḥ samśayas tathā /
ṇ: tataḥ prakaraṇāhetū arthāpatty.aviśeṣakau //2//
ṇ: upapattiś copalabdhy.anupalabdhī tathā kramāt /
ṇ: nityānitye kāryasamā jātayaḥ samudīritāḥ //3/
ṇ: pratijñā.hāni.samnyāsavirodhāś ca tad.anantaram /
ṇ: hetv.arthāntaranirarthāvijñātārtham apārthakam //4//
ṇ: aprāpta.kāla.yug nyūnam adhikam punar.ukta.yuk /
ṇ: syān nānubhāṣaṇa.(ananubhāṣaṇa).ajñānāprajñā.(apratimā?).vikṣepasamjñakam //5//
ṇ: matānujñā.pariniranuyojyo bhavatas tataḥ /
ṇ: upekṣaṇānuyogau cāpasiddhāntāpasādhane //6// iti
ṇ: jātinigrahasthānasamgraha.ślokāḥ /

ṇ131: atrānuktam api kimcin nigadyate / arthopalabdhi.hetuḥ pramāṇam /
ṇ: ekātmasamavāyi.jñānāntaravedyam jñānam, pramāṇād bhinnam phalam, pūrvam pramāṇam uttaram tu phalam /
ṇ: smṛter aprāmāṇyam, parasparavibhaktau sāmānyaviśeṣau nityānityatve sad.asad.amśau ca, pramāṇasya viṣayaḥ pāramārthikah, tamaś.chāye adravye, ākāśa.guṇaḥ śabdo 'paudgalikah, samketavaśād eva śabdād arthapratītir na punas tat.pratipādanasāmarthyāt, dharma.dharmiṇor bhedah, sāmānyam anekavṛtti, ātmaviśeṣa.guṇa.lakṣaṇam karma, vapur.viṣaya.indriya.buddhi.sukha.duhkhānām ucchedād ātmasamsthānam muktir iti nyāyasāre punar evam 'nityasamvedyamānena sukhena viśiṣṭā.ātyantikī duhkhanivṛttiḥ puruṣasya mokṣa iti //

ṇ132: eṣām tarka.granthā nyāyasūtra.bhāṣyanyāyavārttika.tātparya.ṭīkā.tātparyapariśuddhinyāyālamkāravṛttayaḥ / krameṇākṣapādavātsyāyanodyotakaravācaspati.śrī.udayana.śrīkaṇṭhābhaya.tilakopadhyāyaviracitāḥ 54000 pramitāḥ (pramitāḥ absent in ṣuali) / 
ṇ: bhā.sarvajñapraṇīte nyāyasāre.aṣṭādaśa ṭīkāḥ // tāsu mukhyā ṭīkā nyāya.bhūṣaṇākhyā tenaiva racitā nyāya.kalikā jayantaracitā, nyāya.kusumāñjali.tarkaś ca //32//

ṇ133: atha tan.matam upasamharann uttaram ca matam upakṣipann āha /
ṇ: naiyāyikamatasyaiṣa samāsaḥ kathito 'ñjasā /
ṇ: sāmkhyābhimata.bhavānām idānīm ayam ucyate //33//

ṇ134: vyākhyā -- eṣo 'nantarodito naiyāyikamatasya samāsaḥ samkṣepaḥ kathita ukto 'ñjasā drāg sāmkhyābhimata.bhāvānām sāmkhyāḥ kāpilās teṣām abhimatā abhīṣṭā bhāvā ye pañcavimśati.tattvādayaḥ padārthās teṣām ayam samāsa idānīm ucyate // (naiyāyikamatasvarūpaprakaṭano nāma dvitīyo 'dhikārah) [Eṇḍ]


ṣḍṣ ṣāmkhya

atha tṛtīyo 'dhikāraḥ /

ṣ1: athādau sāmkhyamataprapannānām parijñānāya liGgādikam nigadyate /
ṣ: tri.daṇḍā eka.daṇḍā vā kaupīnavasanā dhātu.raktāmbarāḥ śikhāvanto jaṭinaḥ kṣuramuṇḍā mṛga.carmāsanā dvija.gṛhāśanāḥ pañca.grāsī.parā vā dvādaśākṣara.jāpinaḥ parivrājakādayaḥ /
ṣ:tad.bhaktā vandamānā om namo nārāyaṇāya.iti vadanti, te tu nārāyaṇāya nama prāhuḥ /
ṣ: teṣām ca mahā.bhārate bīṭā.iti khyātā dāravī mukhavastrikā mukhanihśvāsanirodhikā bhūtānām dayā.nimittam bhavati / yad āhus te --
ṣ: ''ghrāṇāadito 'nuyātena śvāsenaikena jantavaḥ / (ghrāṇād ito 'nuyātena?)
ṣ: hanyante śataśo brahmann aṇu.mātrākṣaravādinām //1//''

ṣ2: te ca jala.jīva.dayārtham svayam galanakam dhārayanti, bhaktānām copadiśanti /
ṣ: ''ṣaṭtrimśad.aGgulāyāmam vimśaty.angulavistṛtam /
ṣ: dṛḍham galanakam kuryād bhūyo jīvān viśodhayet //1//
ṣ: mriyante miṣṭa.toyena pūtarāḥ kṣārasambhavāḥ /
ṣ: kṣāra.toyena tu pare na kuryāt samkaram tataḥ //2//
ṣ: lūtāsya.tantu.galite ye bindau santi jantavaḥ /
ṣ: sūkṣmā bhramaramānās te naiva mānti triviṣṭape //3//'' iti galanakavicāro mīmāmsāyām /

ṣ3: sāmkhyā kecid īśvara.devāh, apare ca nirīśvarāḥ /
ṣ: ye ca nirīśvarās (?) teṣām nārāyaṇo devaḥ /
ṣ: teṣām ācāryā viṣṇupratiṣṭhā.kārakāś caitanyaprabhṛti.śabdair abhidhīyate /
ṣ: teṣām matavaktāraḥ kapilāsuri.pañcaśikha.bhārgavolūkādayah, tataḥ sāmkhyāḥ kāpitā ityādināmabhir abhidhīyante /
ṣ: tathā kapilasya paramarṣir iti dvitīyam nāma, tena teṣām pāramarṣā ity api nāma jñātavyam /

ṣ4: vārāṇasyām teṣām prācuryam / bahavo māsopavāsikā brāhmaṇā arcir.mārgaviruddha.dhūmamārgānugāminaḥ /
ṣ: sāmkhyās tv arcir.mārgānugāḥ /
ṣ: tata eva brāhmaṇā vedapriyā yajñamārgānugāḥ /
ṣ: sāmkhyās tu himsā.āḍhyavedaviratā adhyātmavādinaḥ /
ṣ: te ca svamatasya mahimānam evam āmananti / tad uktam māṭharaprānte --
ṣ: ''hasa piba lala khāda moda nityam bhuGkṣva ca bhogān yathābhikāmam /
ṣ: yadi viditam te kapilamatam tat prāpsyasi mokṣasaukhyam acireṇa //1//''
ṣ: śāstrāntare.apy uktam --
ṣ: ''pañcavimśati.tattvajño yatra tatrāśrame rataḥ /
ṣ: śikhī muṇḍī jaṭī vāpi mucyate nātrasamśayaḥ //2//''//33//

ṣ5: atha śāstra.kāraḥ sāmkhyamatam upadarśayati /
ṣ: sāmkhyā nirīśvarāḥ kecit kecid īśvara.devatāḥ /
ṣ: sarveṣām api teṣām syāt tattvānām pañcavimśatiḥ //34//

ṣ6: vyākhyā -- kecit samkhyā nirgata īśvaro yebhyas te nirīśvarāh, kevalādhyātmaikamāninah, kecid īśvara.devatāḥ -- īśvaro devatā yeṣām te tathā /
ṣ: teṣām sarveṣām api nirīśvarāṇām sa.īśvarāṇām cobhayeṣām api tattvānām pañcavimśatiḥ syāt /
ṣ: sāmkhyamate kila duhkha.trayābhihatasya puruṣasya tad.upaghāta.hetus tattva.jijñāsā.utpadyate /
ṣ: ādhyātmikam ādhidaivikam ādhibhautikam ca.iti duhkha.trayam /
ṣ: atrādhyātmikam dvividham, śārīram mānasam ca /
ṣ: tatra vātapitta.śleṣmaṇām vaiṣamyanimittam yad duhkham ātmānam deham adhikṛtya jvarātīsārādi samutpadyate tat śārīram, mānasam ca kāma.krodha.lobhamoha.īrṣyā.viṣayādarśananibandhanam (viṣayādarśananibandhanam) /
ṣ: sarvam caitad āntaropāyasādhyatvād ādhyātmikam duhkham /
ṣ: bāhyopāyasādhyam duhkham dvedhā -- ādhibhautikam ādhidaivikam ca.iti /
ṣ: tatrādhibhautikam mānuṣapaśupakṣi.mṛgasarīsṛpasthāvaranimittam, ādhidaivikam yakṣarākṣasa.grahādyāveśa.hetukam /
ṣ: anena duhkha.trayeṇa rajah.pariṇāma.bhedena buddhi.vartinā(?).abhihatasya prāṇinas tattvānām jijñāsā bhavati duhkhavighātāya / tattvāni ca pañcavimśatir bhavati //34//

ṣ7: atha tattvapañcavimśatim eva vivakṣur ādau sattvādi.guṇasvarūpam āha /
ṣ: sattvam rajas tamaś ca.iti jñeyam tāvad guṇa.trayam /
ṣ: prasāda.tāpa.dainyādi.kārya.liGgam krameṇa tat //35//

ṣ8: tāvat śabdaḥ prakrame, tac caivam jñātavyam (not in ṣuali), teṣu pañcavimśatau tattveṣu sattvam sukha.lakṣaṇam, rajo duhkha.lakṣaṇam, tamaś ca moha.lakṣaṇam ity evam prathamam tāvad guṇa.trayam jñeyam /
ṣ: tasya guṇa.trayasya kāni liGgāni.ity āha -- 'prasāda' ityādi /
ṣ: tat sattvādi.guṇa.trayam krameṇa prasāda.tāpa.dainyādi.kārya.liGgam /
ṣ: prasādaḥ prasannatā, tāpaḥ samtāpah, dainyam dīnavacanādi.hetur viṣaṇṇatā, dvandve prasāda.tāpa.dainyāni, tāni ādiḥ prakāro yeṣām kāryāṇām tāni prasāda.tāpa.dainyādīni, prasāda.tāpa.dainyādīni kāryāṇi liGgam gamakam cihnam yasya tat prasāda.tāpa.dainyādi.kārya.liGgam /
ṣ: ayam bhāvaḥ -- prasāda.buddhi.pāṭava.lāghavaprasavānabhiṣvaGgādveṣaprītyādayaḥ kāryam sattvasya liGgam /
ṣ: tāpa.śoṣa.bheda.cala.cittatā.stambhodvegāḥ kāryam rajaso liGgam /
ṣ: dainyamohamaraṇasādana.bībhatsājñānāgauravādīni kāryam tamaso liGgam /
ṣ: ebhiḥ kāryaiḥ sattvādīni jñāyante / tathā hi -- loke yaḥ kaścit sukham upalabhate sa ārjavamārdavasatya.śauca.hrī.buddhi.kṣamānukampā.prasādādi.sthānam bhavati, tat sattvam / (grammatically strange)
ṣ: yaḥ kaścid duhkham upalabhate sa tadā dveṣa.drohamatsaranindā.vañcana.bandhana.tāpādi.sthānam bhavatiṇ tad rajaḥ /
ṣ: yaḥ kamcit kadāpi moham labhate so 'jñānamadālasya.bhaya.dainyākarmaṇyatā.nāstikatā.viṣādonmādasvapnādi.sthānam bhavatiṇ tat tama (tamas) iti /

ṣ9: sattvādibhiś ca parasparopakāribhis tribhir api guṇaiḥ sarvam jagad vyāptam vidyate, param ūrdhva.loke prāyo deveṣu sattvasya bahulatā, adho.loke tiryakṣu ca tamo.bahulatā, madhya.loke manuṣyeṣu rajo.bahulatā, yad duhkhaprāyā (yad.duhkhaprāyā) manuṣyā bhavanti / tad uktam (yad uktam in ṣuali) --
ṣ: ''ūrdhvam sattvaviśālas tamo.viśālaś ca mūlataḥ sargaḥ /
ṣ: madhye rajo.viśālo brahmādi.stambaparyantaḥ //1// [ṣK 54]
ṣ: atra brahmādi.stambaparyanta iti brahmādi.piśācānto 'ṣṭavidhaḥ sarga iti //35//
ṣ: eteṣām yā samāvasthā sā prakṛtiḥ kilocyate /
ṣ: pradhānāvyakta.śabdābhyām vācyā nityasvarūpikā //36//

ṣ10: vyākhyā -- eteṣām sattvādi.guṇānām, yā samā tulyapramāṇā, avasthā avasthānam, sā sattvādīnām samāvasthā.evaprakṛtir ucyate /
ṣ: kila.iti pūrvavārttāyām / sattvarajas.tamasām guṇānām kvacid devādau kasyacid ādhikye.api miśraḥ pramāṇāpekṣayā trayāṇām api samānāvasthā prakṛtiḥ kīrtyata ity.arthaḥ /
ṣ: pradhānāvyakta.śabdābhyām vācyā / sā ca prakṛtiḥ pradhānam avyaktam cocyate nāmāntarābhyām /
ṣ: nityam apracyutānuptannasthiraikasvabhāvam kūṭastham svarūpam yasyāḥ sā nityasvarūpikāvicalitasvarūpā.ity.arthaḥ /
ṣ: ata eva sānavayavā sādhāraṇy.aśabdāsparśārasārūpāgandhāvyayā cocyate /
ṣ: maulikyasāmkhyā hy ātmānam ātmānam prati pṛthak pṛthak pradhānam vadanti, uttare tu sāmkhyāḥ sarvātmasv apy ekam nityam pradhānam iti pratipannāḥ //36//

ṣ11: prakṛtyātmasamyogāt sṛṣṭir jāyate / ataḥ sṛṣṭi.kramam evāha --
ṣ: tataḥ samjāyate buddhir mahān iti yakā.ucyate /
ṣ: ahamkāras tato 'pi syāt tasmāt ṣoḍaśako gaṇaḥ //37//

ṣ12: vyākhyā -- tataḥ prakṛter buddhiḥ samjāyata utpadyate / sā ca gavādau puro dṛśyamāne gaur evāyam nāśvah, sthānur.evāyam na puruṣa iti viṣayaniścayādhyavasāyarūpā mahān iti yakā procyate mahad.ākhyayā yābhidhīyate /
ṣ: buddheś ca tasyā aṣṭau rūpāṇi / dharma.jñānavairāgyāiśvaryarūpāṇi catvāri sāttvikāni, adharmādīni tu tat.pratipakṣa.bhūtāni catvāri tāmasāni.iti /
ṣ: tato 'pi buddher apy ahamkāraḥ syād utpadyate /
ṣ: sa ca 'aham subhagah, aham darśanīyah' ityādy.abhimānarūpaḥ /
ṣ: tasmād ahaGkārāt ṣoḍaśako gaṇaḥ samudāyaḥ //37//

ṣ13: atha ṣoḍaśasamkhyam gaṇam śloka.dvayenāha --
ṣ: sparśanam rasanam ghrāṇam cakṣuḥ śrotram ca pañcamam /
ṣ: pañca buddhi.indriyāṇy atra tathā karma.indriyāṇi ca //38//
ṣ: pāyu.upasthavacah.pāṇi.pādākhyāni manas tathā /
ṣ: anyāni pañca rūpādi.tanmātrāṇi.iti ṣoḍaśa //39// yugmam //

ṣ14: vyākhyā -- sparśanam tvak, rasanam jihvā, ghrāṇam nāsikā, cakṣur locanam, śrotram ca śravaṇam pañcamam, etāni pañca buddhi.indriyāṇy atra ṣoḍaśake gaṇe bhavanti /
ṣ: svam svam viṣayam budhyanta iti kṛtvā.indriyāṇy eva buddhi.indriyāṇi procyante /
ṣ: tathā.hi -- sparśanam sparśaviṣayam budhyate, evam rasanam rasam, ghrāṇam gandham, cakṣū rūpam, śrotram ca śabdam iti /
ṣ: tathā.śabdaḥ pañca.iti.padasyānukarṣaṇārthaḥ /
ṣ: pañcasamkhyāni karma.kāraṇatvāt karma.indriyāṇi ca /
ṣ: kāni tāni.ityāha -- pāyu.upasthavacah.pāṇi.pādākhyāni /
ṣ: tatra pāyur gudam, upasthaḥ strī.pumś.cihna.dvayam, vacaś ca.ihocyate.anena.iti (?) vacah, urah.kaṇṭhādi.sthānāṣṭatayā vacanam uccārayati, pāṇī pādau ca prasiddhau, etair malotsargasambhogavacanādāna.calanādīni karmāṇi sidhyanti.iti karma.indriyāṇy ucyante /
ṣ: tathā.śabdaḥ samuccaye / ekādaśam manaś ca, mano hi buddhi.indriyamadhye buddhi.indriyam bhavati, karma.indriyamadhye karma.indriyam, tac ca tattvārtham antareṇāpi samkalpavṛtti /
ṣ: tad.yathā -- kaścid baṭuḥ śṛṇoti ''grāmāntare bhojanam asti'' iti, tatra tasya samkalpaḥ syāt ''tatra yāsyāmi tatra cāham guḍa.dadhi.rūpam bhojanam lapsya utasvid (utaśvid) dadhi kim vā kim api na'' ity evam.rūpam mana (manas) iti /
ṣ: tathāhamkārāad anyāny aparāṇi rūpādi tanmātrāṇi sūkṣmasamjnāni pañcotpadyante /
ṣ: tatra rūpa.tanmātram śukla.kṛṣṇādi.rūpaviśeṣah, rasa.tanmātram tiktādi.rasaviśeṣah, gandha.tanmātram surabhyādi.gandhaviśeṣah, śabda.tanmātram madhurādi.śabdaviśeṣah, sparśa.tanmātram mṛdu.kaṭhinādi.sparśaviśeṣa iti ṣoḍaśa / ayam ṣoḍaśako gaṇa ity.arthaḥ //38-39//

ṣ15: atha tanmātrebhyaḥ pañca.bhūtāny utpadyanta ity āha --
ṣ: rūpāt tejo rasād āpo gandhād bhūmiḥ svarān nabhaḥ /
ṣ: sparśād vāyus tathā.evam ca pañcabhyo bhūtapañcakam //40//

ṣ16: vyākhyā -- rūpa.tanmātrāt sūkṣmasamjñāt tejo 'gnir.utpadyate, rasatanmātrād āpo jalāni jāyante, gandha.tanmātrāt pṛthivī samutpadyate, svarāt śabda.tanmātrād ākāśam udbhavati, tathā sparśa.tanmātrād vāyuḥ prādurbhavati, evam ca pañcabhyas tanmātrebhyo bhūtapañcakam bhavati.iti //40//
ṣ: ''evam catur.vimśati.tattvarūpam niveditam sāmkhyamate pradhānam /
ṣ: anyas tv akartā viguṇaś ca bhoktā tattvam pumān nitya.cid.abhyupetaḥ //41// (nitya.cid?)

ṣ17: vyākhyā -- evam amunā.uktaprakāreṇa sāmkhyamate catur.vimśati.tattvarūpam pradhānam / 
ṣ: prakṛtir mahān ahamkāraś ca.iti trayam pañca buddhi.indriyāṇi, pañca karma.indriyāṇi, manaś caikam, pañca tanmātrāṇi, pañca bhūtāni ca.iti catur.vimśati.tattvāni rūpam svarūpam yasya tac catur.vimśati.tattvarūpam pradhānam prakṛtir niveditam /
ṣ: tathā coktam ''prakṛter mahāms tato 'hamkāras tasmād gaṇaś ca ṣoḍaśakaḥ / tasmād api ṣoḍaśakāt pañcabhya pañca bhūtāni //'' [ṣK33] iti /

ṣ18: atra prakṛtir na vikārah, anutpannatvāt / buddhyādayaś ca sapta pareṣām kāraṇatayā prakṛtayaḥ kāryatayā ca vikṛtaya ucyante /
ṣ: ṣoḍaśakaś ca gaṇo vikṛtir eva kāryatvāt / 
ṣ: puruṣas tu na prakṛtir na vikṛtih, anutpādakatvād anutpannatvāc ca /
ṣ: tathā ca.īśvara.kṛṣṇaḥ sāmkhyasaptatau --
ṣ: ''mūlaprakṛtir avikṛtir mahad.ādyāḥ prakṛti.vikṛtayaḥ sapta /
ṣ: ṣoḍaśakas tu vikāro na prakṛtir na vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ //1// [ṣK 3] iti /

ṣ19: tathā mahad.ādayaḥ prakṛter vikārās te ca vyaktāḥ santaḥ punar avyaktā api bhavanti.iti svarūpād bhraśyanty anityatvāt /
ṣ: prakṛtis tv avikṛtā nityābhyupagamyate / 
ṣ: tato na kadācid api sā svasvarūpād bhraśyati / tathā ca mahad.ādikasya prakṛteś ca svarūpam sāmkhyair ittham ūce /
ṣ: ''hetumad anityam avyāpi sakriyam anekam āśritam liGgam /
ṣ: sāvayavam para.tantram vyaktam viparītam avyaktam //2//'' [ṣK 20] iti /
ṣ: 
ṣ20: tatra hetumat kāraṇavan mahad.ādikam, anityam ity utpatti.dharmakatvād buddhyādeh, avyāpi.iti pratiniyatam na sarvagam, sakriyam iti saha kriyābhir adhyavasāyādibhir vartata iti sakriyam -- savyāpāram samcaraṇa.kriyāvad iti yāvat, anekam iti trayo.vimśati.bhedātmakam, āśritam ity ātmopakārakatvena pradhānam avalambya sthitam, liGgam iti yad yasmād utpannam tat tasminn eva layam kṣayam gacchati.iti liGgam /
ṣ: tatra bhūtāni tanmātreṣu līyante, tanmātrāṇi.indriyāṇi manaś cāhamkāre, sa ca buddhauṇ sā cāvyakte, tac cānutpādyatvān na kvacit pralīyate /
ṣ: sāvayavam iti śabdasparśarūparasa.gandhātmakair avayavair yuktatvāt, para.tantram iti kāraṇāyattatvād ity evam.rūpam vyaktam mahad.ādikam /
ṣ: avyaktam tu prakṛtyākhyam, etad.viparītam iti / tatra viparītatā suyojyā.eva /
ṣ: navaram pradhānam divi bhuvy antarikṣe ca sarvatra vyāpitayā vartata iti vyāpitvam tasya, tathāvyaktasya vyāpakatvena samcaraṇarūpāyāḥ kriyāyā abhāvān niṣkriyatvam ca draṣṭavyam iti diG.mātram(?!) darśitam /
ṣ: viśeṣavyākhyānam tu sāmkhyasaptatyādes tat.śāstrād avaseyam iti /

ṣ21: atha pañcavimśatitamam puruṣa.tattvam āha -- 'anyas tv akartā'' ityādi /
ṣ: prakṛteś catur.vimśati.tattvarūpāyā anyas tu pṛthag.bhūtah, punar akartā viguṇo bhoktā nitya.cid.abhyupetaś ca pumān puruṣas tattvam /
ṣ: tatrātmā viṣayasukhādikam tat.kāraṇam puṇyādi.karma ca na karoti.ity akartā, ātmanas tṛṇamātra.kubjī.karaṇe.apy asamarthatvāt /
ṣ: kartrī tu prakṛtir evaṇ tasyāḥ pravṛtti.svabhāvatvāt / tathā viguṇaḥ sattvādi.guṇarahitah, sattvādīnām prakṛti.dharmatvād ātmanaś ca tad.abhāvāt /

ṣ22: tathā bhoktā anubhavitā / bhokāpi sākṣān na bhoktā, kim.tu prakṛti.vikāra.bhūtāyām apy ubhayamukha.darpaṇākārāyām buddhau samkrāntānām sukha.duhkhādīnām puruṣaḥ svātmani nirmale pratibimbodayamātreṇa bhoktā vyapadiśyateṇ ''buddhy.adhyavasitam artham puruṣaś cetayate'' [  ] iti vacanāt /
ṣ: yathā japā.kusumādi.samnidhānavaśāt sphaṭike raktatā.ādi vyapadiśyate, tathā prakṛty.upadhānavattvāt sukha.duhkhādyātmakānām arthānām puruṣasya bhojakatvam yuktam eva vyapadiśyate /
ṣ: vādamahārṇavo 'py āha -- ''buddhi.darpaṇasamkrāntam arthapratibimbakam dvitīya.darpaṇa.kalpe pumsy adhyārohatiṇ tad eva bhoktṛtvam asyaṇ na tv ātmano vikārāpattiḥ /'' [   ] iti / tathā cāsuriḥ --
ṣ: ``vivikte dṛk.pariṇatau (?) buddhau bhogo 'sya kathyate /
ṣ: pratibimbodayaḥ svacche yathā candramaso 'mbhasi //1//
ṣ: vindhyavāsī tv evam bhogam ācaṣṭe --
ṣ: ''puruṣo 'vikṛtātmā.eva svanirbhā.sama.cetanam (svanirbhāsamacetanam) /
ṣ: manaḥ karoti sāmnidhyād upādhiḥ (upādheh) sphaṭikam yathā //2//'' iti /

ṣ23: tathā nityā yā cic cetanā tayābhyypetah, etena puruṣasya caitanyam eva svarūpam, na tu jñānam, jñānasya buddhi.dharmatvād ity āveditam draṣṭavyam /
ṣ: kevalam ātmā svam buddher avyatiriktam abhimanyate, sukha.duhkhādayaś ca viṣayā indriya.dvāreṇa buddhau samkrāmanti, buddhiś cobhayamukha.darpaṇākārāṇ tatas tasyām caitanya.śaktiḥ pratibimbateṇ tataḥ sukhy aham duhkhy aham jñātāham ity upacaryate / āha ca patañjaliḥ --
ṣ: ''śuddho 'pi puruṣaḥ pratyayam baudham anupaśyati, tam anupaśyann atad.ātmāpi tad.ātmaka iva pratibhāsate'' [YBhs 2.20] iti /
ṣ: ''buddhiś cācetanāpi cit.śakti.(cicchakti).samnidhānāc cetanāvatī.ivāvabhāsate'' iti /

ṣ24: pumān ity atra jāty.apekṣayā.ekavacanam, tenātmāneko 'bhyupagantavyah, janmamaraṇa.karaṇānām niyama.darśanād dharmādi.pravṛttinānātvāc ca /
ṣ: te ca sarve.apy ātmānaḥ sarva.gatā nityāś cāvaseyāḥ / uktam ca --
ṣ: ''amūrtaś cetano bhogī nityaḥ sarva.gato 'kriyaḥ /
ṣ: akartā nirguṇaḥ sūkṣma ātmā kāpila.darśane //1//'' //41//

ṣ25: tattvāny upasamharann āha --
ṣ: pañcavimśati.tattvāni samkhyayā.evam bhavanti ca /
ṣ: pradhānanarayoś cātra vṛttiḥ paGgv.andhayor iva //42//

ṣ26: vyākhyā -- ca.kāaro bhinna.kramah, evam ca samkhyayā pañcavimśati.tattvāni bhavanti /
ṣ: nanu prakṛti.puruṣāv ubhāv api sarva.gatau mithah.samyuktau katham vartete ity āśaGkyāha 'pradhāna.ityādi' /
ṣ: pradhānapuruṣayoś cātra viśve paGgv.andhayor iva vṛttir vartanam /
ṣ: yathā kaścid andhaḥ sārthena samam pāṭaliputrangaram prasthitah, sa sārthaś caurair abhihataḥ /
ṣ: andhas tatraiva rahita itaś ca.itaś ca dhāvan vanāntarapaGgunā dṛṣṭo 'bhihitaś ca 'bho bho andha mā bhaiṣīh, aham paGgur gamanādi.kriyā.vikalatvenākriyaś cakṣurbhyām sarvam paśyann asmi, tvam tu gamanādi.kriyāvān na paśyasi' /
ṣ: tato andhena.ūce -- 'ruciram idamṇ aham bhavantam skandhe kariṣyāmi, evam āvayor vartanam astu' iti /
ṣ: tato 'ndhena paGgur draṣṭṛtva.guṇena svam skandham adhiropito nagaram prāpya nāṭakādikam paśyan gītādikam ca.indriyaviṣayam anyam apy upalabhyamāno yathā modate, tathā paGgu.kalpaḥ śuddha.caitanyasvarūpaḥ puruṣo 'py andha.kalpām jaḍām prakṛtim sakriyām āśrito buddhy.adhyavasitam śabdādikam svātmani pratibimbitam cetayamāno modate, modamānaś ca prakṛtim sukhasvabhāvām mohān manyamānaḥ samsāram adhivasati //42//

ṣ27: tarhi tasya katham muktiḥ syād ity āha --
ṣ: prakṛti.viyogo mokṣaḥ puruṣasya bataitad.antara.jñānāt /
ṣ: māna.tritayam cātra pratyakṣam lainGikam śābdam //43//

ṣ28: vyākhyā -- bata.iti pṛcchakā.nāmāmantraṇe, etayoḥ prakṛti.puruṣayor yad antaram vivekas tasya jñānāt puruṣasya yaḥ prakṛter viyogo bhavati sa mokṣaḥ / tathā.hi --
ṣ: ''śuddha.caitanyarūpo 'yam puruṣaḥ paramārthataḥ /
ṣ: prakṛty.antaram ajñātvā mohāt samsāram āśritaḥ //1//''
ṣ: tata prakṛteḥ sukha.duhkhamohasvabhāvāyā yāvan na vivekena grahaṇam tāvan na mokṣah, prakṛter viveka.darśane tu pravṛtter uparatāyām prakṛtau puruṣasya svarūpeṇāvasthānam mokṣa iti /
ṣ: mokṣaś ca bandhavicchedād bhavati, bandhaś ca prākṛtikavaikārika.dākṣiṇa.bhedāt trividhaḥ /
ṣ: tathā.hi -- prakṛtāv ātma.jñānād ye prakṛtim upāsate, teṣām prākṛtiko bandhaḥ /
ṣ: ye vikārān eva bhūta.indriyāhamkāra.buddhīḥ puruṣa.buddhyā.upāsate, teṣām vaikārikaḥ /
ṣ: iṣṭā.pūrte dākṣiṇah, puruṣa.tattvānabhijño hi.iṣṭā.pūrta.kārī kāmopahatamanā badhyata iti /
ṣ: ''iṣṭā.pūrvam manyamānā variṣṭham nānyat śreyo ye.abhinandanti mūḍhāḥ /
ṣ: nākasya pṛṣṭhe sukṛtena bhūtvā, imam lokam hīnataram vā viśanti //1//'' [Muṇḍaka 1.2.10] iti /
ṣ: bandhāc ca pretyasamsaraṇarūpaḥ samsāraḥ pravartate /

ṣ29: sāmkhyamate ca puruṣasya prakṛti.vikṛty.anātmakasya na bandhamokṣasamsārāh, kim.tu prakṛter eva / tathā ca kāpilāḥ --
ṣ: ''tasmān na badhyate naiva mucyate nāpi samsarati kaścit /
ṣ: samsarati badhyate mucyate ca nānā.āśrayā prakṛtiḥ //1//'' [ṣK 62] iti /
ṣ: navaram amī bandhamokṣasamsārāḥ puruṣe upacaryate /
ṣ: yathā jayaparājayau bhṛtya.gatāv api svāminy upacaryeta tat.phalasya kośa.lābhādeḥ svāmini sambandhāt, tathā bhogāpavargayoḥ prakṛti.gatayor api vivekāgrahāt puruṣe sambandha iti //

ṣ30: atra pramāṇasya sāmānya.lakṣaṇam ucyate -- 'arthopalabdhi.hetuḥ pramāṇam' iti /
ṣ: athottarārdhe māna.tritayam ca - pramāṇa.tritayam ca, atra sāmkhyamate /
ṣ: kim tad ity āha -- pratyakṣam pratītam, laiGgam anumānam, śābdam cāgmaḥ ca.kāro 'trāpi sambandhanīyaḥ /
ṣ: tatra pratyakṣa.lakṣaṇam ākhyāyate - 'śrotrādi.vṛttir avikalpikā pratyakṣam' iti /
ṣ: 'śrotram tvak cakṣuṣī jihvā nāsikā ca.iti pañcamī' iti /
ṣ: śrotrādīni.indriyāṇi, teṣām vṛttir vartanam pariṇāma iti yāvat, indriyāṇy eva viṣayākārapariṇatāni pratyakṣam iti hi teṣām siddhāntaḥ /
ṣ: avikalpikā nāma.jātyādi.kalpanā.rahitā śākyamatādhyakṣavad vyākhyeyā.iti /

ṣ31: īśvarakṛṣṇaś tu 'pratiniyatādhyavasāyaḥ śrotrādi.samuttho 'dhyakṣam' ity āha /

ṣ32: anumānsya tv idam lakṣaṇam -- pūrvavat.śeṣavat.sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam ca.iti trividham anumānam iti /
ṣ: tatra nady.unnati.darśanād upari vṛṣṭo deva ity anumīyate yat tat pūrvavat /
ṣ: tathā samudrodaka.binduprāśanāat śeṣam jalm kṣāram anumānena jñāyateṇ tathā sthālyām sikthaika.campanāt śeṣam annam pakvam apakvam vā jñāyate tat śeṣavat /
ṣ: yat sāmānyato.dṛṣṭam tat liGga.liGgi.pūrvakam, yathā tridaṇḍa.darśanād adṛṣṭo 'pi liGgī parivrājako 'sti.ity avagamyate, iti trividham /
ṣ: athavā tal.liGga.liGgi.pūrvakam ity evānumāna.lakṣaṇam sāmkhyaiḥ samākhyāyate /

ṣ33: śābdam tv āpta.śruti.vacanam, āptā rāga.dveṣādi.rahitā brahmasanatkumārādayah, śrutir vedas teṣām vacanam śābdam /

ṣ34: atrānuktam api kimcid ucyate / cit.śakti.(cicchakti).viṣayapariccheda.śūnyā nārtham jānāti, buddhiś ca jaḍā na cetayateṇ samnidhānāt tayor anyathā.pratibhāsanam, prakṛtyātmasamyogāt sṛṣṭir upajāyate, prakṛti.vikārasvarūpam karma, tathā traiguṇyarūpam sāmānyam, pramāṇaviṣayas tāttvika iti /
ṣ: atra trayo guṇāḥ sattvarasaj.tamāmsi / tataḥ svārthe ''ṇyo'nantādeh' [not in Pṣ] iti ṇyah, yathā trayo lokās trailokyam, ṣaḍ.guṇāḥ ṣāḍguṇyam, tatas traiguṇyam rūpam svabhāvo yasya sāmānyasya tat traiguṇyarūpam iti / pramāṇasya ca phalam ittham / pūrvam pūrvam pramāṇam uttaram tu phalam iti /

ṣ34: tathā kāraṇe kāryam sad evotpadyate.asad akaraṇādibhyo (akāraṇādibhyo) hetubhyaḥ / tad uktam --
ṣ: ''asad akaraṇād (akāraṇād) upādāna.grahaṇāt sarvasambhavābhāvāt /
ṣ: śaktasya śakya.karaṇāt kāraṇa.bhāvāc ca sat kāryam //1//'' [ṣK 9]
ṣ: atra sarvasambhavābhāvād iti, yady asat kāryam syāt tadā sarvam sarvatra bhavet / tataś ca tṛṇādibhyo 'pi suvarṇādīni bhaveyuh, na ca bhavanti, tasmāt kāraṇe kāryam sad eva /
ṣ: tathā dravyāṇy eva kevalāni santi, na punar utpatti.vipatti.dharmāṇaḥ paryāyāḥ ke.api, āvirbhāva.tirobhāvamātratvāt teṣām iti /

ṣ36: sāmkhyānām tarka.granthāḥ ṣaṣṭi.tantroddhārarūpam māṭhara.bhāṣyam sāmkhyasaptatināmakam, tattva.kaumudī, gauḍapādam, ātreya.tantram ca.ityādayaḥ //43//

ṣ: 37 sāmkhyamatam upasamjihīrṣann uttaratra jainamatam abhidhitsann āha --
ṣ: evam sāmkhyamatasyāpi samāso gadito 'dhunā / 
ṣ: jaina.darśanasamkṣepaḥ kathyate suvicāravān //44//

ṣ38: vyākhyā -- evam uktavidhinā sāmkhyamatasyāpi na kevalam baudhanaiyāyikayor ity api śabdārthaḥ / samāsaḥ -- samkṣepo 'dhunā gaditaḥ /
ṣ: jaina.darśanasamkṣepaḥ kathyate / katham.bhūtaḥ suvicāravān suṣṭhu sarvapramāṇair abādhitasvarūpatvena śobhanā vicārāḥ suvicārās te vidyante yasya sa suvicāravān, na punar avicāritaramaṇīyavicāravān iti /
ṣ: anenāpara.darśanāny avicāritaramaṇīyāni.ity āveditam mantavyam / yad uktam parair eva --
ṣ: ''purāṇam mānavo dharmaḥ sāGgo vidaś cikitsitam /
ṣ: ājñā.siddhāni catvāri na hantavyāni hetubhiḥ //1//'' [Manu 12.110]
ṣ: parair hi doṣasambhāvanayā.eva svamatavicāraṇā nādriyate / yata uktam --
ṣ: ''asti vaktavyatā kācit tena.idam na vicāryate /
ṣ: nirdoṣam kāñcanam cet syāt parīkṣāyā bibheti kim //1//'' iti /
ṣ: ata eva jainā jinamatasya nirdūṣaṇatayā parīkṣāto nirbhīkā evam upadiśanti /
ṣ: sarvathā sva.darśanapakṣapātam parityajya mādhyasthyenaiva yukti.śataiḥ sarva.darśanāni punaḥ punar vicāraṇīyāni, teṣu ca yad eva darśanam yukti.yuktatayāvabhāsate, yatra ca pūrvāparavirodha.gandho 'pi na.īkṣyate, tad eva viśāradair ādaraṇīyam nāparam iti / tathā coktam --
ṣ: ''pakṣapāto na me vīre ba dveṣaḥ kapilādiṣu /
ṣ: yuktimad.vacanam yasya tasya kāryam parigrahaḥ //1//'' [Lokatattvani.ślo.38] (this verse occurred in prasthāvanā)
ṣ: (sāmkhyamatarahasyaprakāśano nāma tṛtīyo 'dhikārah) [Eṇḍ]

ṣḍṣ ñaina

atha caturtho 'dhikāraḥ /

ñ1; athādau jainamate liGgaveṣācārādi procyate /
ñ: jainā dvividhāḥ śvetāmbarā dig.ambarāś ca /
ñ: tatra śvetāmbarāṇām rajo.haraṇamukhavastrikā.loca.('bālomkā lumcana').ādi liGgam; colapaṭṭa.(?).kalpādiko veṣah, pañca samitayas tisraś ca guptayas teṣām ācāraḥ /
ñ: ''īryā.bhāṣā.eṣaṇā.ādānanikṣepotsargasamjñikāḥ /
ñ: pañcāhuḥ samitīs tisro guptīs tri.yoganigrahāt //1//'' iti vacanāt /
ñ: ahimsā.satyāsteya.brahmākiñcanyavān krodhādi.vijayī dānta.indriyo nirgrantho guruh, mādhukaryā vṛttyā nava.koṭī.('nine parts'?)ṇviśuddhas teṣām nityam āhārah, samyamanirvāhārtham eva vastrapātrādi.dhāraṇam vandyamānā dharma.lābham ācakṣate / (madhukaravṛtti)(PṣMA dharmarābha - jaina sādhu dvārā diyā jātā āśīr.vāda PṣMA)

ñ2; dig.ambarāḥ punar nāgnya.liGgāḥ pāṇi.pātrāś ca /
ñ: te caturdhā kāṣṭhā.saGghamūlasaGghamāthurasaGgha.gopyasaGgha.bhedāt / 
ñ: kāṣṭhā.saGghe camarī.bālaiḥ picchikā, mūlasaGghe māyūrapicchaiḥ picchikā, māthurasaGghe mūlato 'pi picchikā nādṛtā, gopyā māyūrapicchikā /
ñ: ādyās trayo 'pi saGghā vandyamānā dharmavṛddhim bhaṇanti, strīṇām muktim kevalinām bhuktim sad.vratasyāpi sacīvarasya muktim ca na manvate, gopyās tu vandyamānā dharma.lābham bhaṇanti, strīkām muktim kevalinām bhuktim ca manyante /
ñ: gopyā yāpanīyā ity apy ucyante / (AC drāviḍo yāpanīyakah)
ñ: sarveṣām ca bhikṣāṭane bhojane ca dvātrimśad.antarāyā malāś ca caturdaśa varjanīyāḥ /
ñ: śeṣām ācāre gurau ca deve ca sarvam śvetāmbarais tulyam, nāsti teṣām mithaḥ śāstreṣu tarkeṣv aparo bhedaḥ //44// (ṭhat's all!)

ñ3: atha devasya lakṣaṇam āha --
ñ: jina.indro devatā tatra rāga.dveṣavivarjitaḥ /
ñ: hatamohamahāmallaḥ kevala.jñāna.darśanaḥ //45//
ñ: surāsura.indrasampūjyaḥ sad.bhūtārthaprakāśakaḥ /
ñ: kṛtsna.karma.kṣayam kṛtvā samprāptaḥ paramam padam //46//

ñ4: vyākhyā -- tatra jainamate jayanti rāgādīn iti jināḥ sāmānya.kevalinah, teṣām indras tādṛśāsadṛśa.caturtrimśad.atiśayasanāthaparamāiśvaryasamanvitaḥ svāmī jina.indro devatā devah, kṛtsna.karma.kṣayam kṛtvā paramam padam samprāpta iti sambandhaḥ /
ñ: kīdṛśaḥ sa ity āha -- 'rāga.dveṣavivarjitah' māyā.lobhau rāgah, krodhamāyau dveṣah, rāga.dveṣābhyām viśeṣeṇa punah.punar.bhāvena varjito rahito rāga.dveṣavivarjito vītarāga ity arthaḥ /
ñ: rāga.dveṣau hi durjayau duranta.bhavasampāta.hetutayā ca mukti.pratirodhakau samaye prasiddhau / yad āha --
ñ: ''ko dukham pāvijjā kasya ya sukhehi vimhao hujjā /
ñ: ko ya na labhijja mukkham rāga.ddesā jai na hujjā //1//'' iti /
ñ: (ko duhkham prāpnuyāt kasya ca sukhaiḥ vismayo bhavet /
ñ: kaś ca na labhen mokṣam rāgadveṣau yadi na bhavetām /)
ñ: tatas tayor viccheda uktaḥ /

ñ5: tathā 'hatamohamahāmallah' mohanīya.karmodayād himsā.ādyātmaka.śāstrebhyo 'pi mukti.kāGkṣaṇādi.vyāmoho mohah, sa eva sakala.jagad.durjayatvena mahāmalla.iva mahāmallah, hato mohamahāmallo yena sa tathā /
ñ: etena viśeṣaṇa.dvayena devasya apāyāpagamātiśayo vyañjito draṣṭavyah, tathā rāga.dveṣamahāmoharahito 'rhann eva deva iti jñāpitam ca / yad uktam --
ñ: ''rāgo 'Gganā.saGgamato 'numeyo, dveṣo dviṣad.dāraṇa.heti.gamyaḥ /
ñ: mohaḥ kuvṛttāgama.doṣasādhyo, no yasya devaḥ sa sa caivam arhan //1//'' iti /

ñ6: tathā kevale -- anya.jñānānapekṣatvenāsahāye sampūrṇe vā jñāna.darśane yasya sa tathā kevala.jñāna.kevala.darśanātmako hi bhagavān, kara.tala.kalitāmalakaphalavad dravyaparyāyātmakam nikhilam anavaratam jagat.svarūpam jānāti paśyati ca.iti /
ñ: kevala.jñāna.darśana iti padam sābhiprāyam, chadmasthasya hi prathamam darśanam utpadyate tato jñānam, kevalinas tv asau jñānam tato darśanam iti /
ñ: tatra sāmānyaviśeṣātmake sarvasmin prameye vastuni sāmānyasyopasarjanī.bhāvena viśeṣāṇām ca pradhāna.bhāvena yad grāhakam taj jñānam, viśeṣāṇām upasarjanī.bhāvena sāmānyasya ca prādhānyena yad grāhakam tad darśanam, etena viśeṣeṇa jñānātiśayaḥ sākṣād ukto 'vagantavyaḥ / (difference bet. jñāna and darśana)

ñ7: tathā surāḥ sarve devāh, asurāś ca daityāh, sura.śabdenāsurāṇām samgrahaṇe.api pṛthag.upādānam lokarūḍhyā jñātavyam /
ñ: loko hi devebhyo dānavāms tad.vipakṣatvena pṛghag nirdiśati.iti /
ñ: teṣām indrāḥ svāminas, teṣām tair vā sampūjyo 'bhyarcanīyaḥ /
ñ: tādṛśair api pūjyasya mānava.tiryak.khecara.kinnarādinikarasevyatvam ānuṣaGgikam iti / anena pūjātiśaya uktaḥ /

ñ8: tathā sad.bhūtāḥ -- yathāvasthitā ye.arthāḥ jīvādayaḥ padārthās, teṣām prakāśaka upadeśakaḥ / anena vacanātiśaya ūcānaḥ /

ñ9: tathā kṛtsnāni sampūrṇāni ghāty.aghātīni karmāṇi jñānāvaraṇāni, teṣām kṣayaḥ sarvathā.pralayaḥ /
ñ: tam kṛtvā paramam padam siddhim samprāptaḥ /
ñ: etena kṛtsna.karma.kṣaya.lakṣaṇā siddhāvasthābhidadhe /
ñ: apare sugatādayo mokṣam avāpyāpi tīrthanikārādi.sambhave bhūyo bhavam avataranti (PṣMA tīrtha ñainasaGgha) / yad āhur anye --
ñ: ''jñānino dharma.tīrthasya kartāraḥ paramam padam /
ñ: gatvā gacchanti bhūyo 'pi bhavam tīrthanikārataḥ //1//'' iti /
ñ: na te paramārthato mokṣa.gati.bhājaḥ karma.kṣayābhāvāt /
ñ: na hi tattvataḥ karma.kṣaye punar.bhavāvatāraḥ / yad uktam --
ñ: ''dagdhe bīje yathātyantam prādur.bhavati nāGkuraḥ /
ñ: karma.bīje tathā dagdhe na rohati bhavāGkuraḥ //1// [ṭAAṣ.Bh.10.7]
ñ: uktam ca śrī.siddhasena.divākarapādair api bhavābhigāmukānām prabalamohavijṛmbhitam --
ñ: ''dagdha.indhanaḥ punar upaiti bhavam pramathya, nirvāṇam apy anavadhārita.bhīr aniṣṭam /
ñ: muktaḥ svayam kṛta.tanuś ca parārtha.śūras, tvat.śāsanapratihateṣv iha moharājyam //1//'' [ṣiddha.ḍvā.] ity alam vistareṇa /
ñ: tad evam ebhiś caturbhir atiśayaiḥ sanātho doṣamuktaś ca yo devo bhavati, sa eva devatvenāśrayaṇīyah, sa eva ca parām siddhim prāpayati, na punar itaraḥ sarāgo bhave.avatāravāmś ca deva ity āveditam gantavyam /

ñ10: nanu mā bhūt sugatādiko devah, jagat.sraṣṭā tv īśvaraḥ kim.iti nāGgī.kriyate /
ñ: tat.sādhanapramāṇābhāvād iti brūmaḥ /
ñ: athāsty eva sādhakam pramāṇam -- kṣityādikam buddhimat.kartṛkam kāryatvāt, ghaṭādivat /
ñ: na cāyam asiddho hetuḥ kṣityādeḥ sāvayavatvena kāryatvasiddheḥ /
ñ: tathā.hi -- urvī.parvata.tarv.ādikam sarvam kāryam, sāvayavatvāt, ghaṭavat /
ñ: nāpi viruddhah, niścita.kartṛke ghaṭādau kāryatva.darśanāt /
ñ: nāpy naikāntikah, niścitākartṛkebhyo vyomādibhyo vyāvartamānatvāt /
ñ: nāpi kālātyayāpadiṣṭah, pratyakṣāgama.bādhitaviṣayatvāt /

ñ11: na ca vācyam ghaṭa.kartr.ādi.dṛṣṭānta.dṛṣṭāsarvajñatvāsarva.gatatva.kartṛtvādi.dharmānurodhena sarvajñādi.viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭasādhyaviparyayasādhanād viruddho hetur dṛṣṭāntaś ca sādhyavikalo ghaṭādau tathā.bhūta.buddhimato 'bhāvād iti /
ñ: yataḥ sādhyasādhanayor viśeṣeṇa vyāptau gṛhyamāṇāyām sakalānumānocchedaprasaktih, kim.tu sāmānyenānvayavyatirekābhyām hi vyāptir avadhāryate /
ñ: tau cānantyād vyabhicārāc ca viśeṣeṣu gṛhītum na śakyau /
ñ: tena buddhimat.pūrvakatvamātreṇa kāryatvasya vyāptiḥ pratyetavyā, na śarīritvādinā /
ñ: na khalu kartṛtvasāmagryām śarīram upayujyate, tad.vyatirekeṇāpi jñāna.icchā.prayatnāśrayatvena sva.śarīra.karaṇe kartṛtvopalambhāt /
ñ: akimcit.karasyāpi sahacaratvamātreṇa kāraṇatve vahni.paiGgalyasyāpi dhūmam prati kāraṇatvaprasaGgaḥ syāt /
ñ: vidyamāne.api hi śarīre jñānādīnām samastānām vyastānām vābhāve kulālādāv api kartṛtvam nopalabhyate /
ñ: prathamam hi kāryotpādaka.kāraṇa.kalāpa.jñānam, tataḥ karaṇa.icchā, tataḥ prayatnah, tataḥ phalaniṣpattir ity amīṣām trayāṇām samuditānām eva kārya.kartṛtve sarvatrāvyabhicāraḥ /

ñ12: sarvajñatā cāsyākhila.kārya.kartṛtvāt siddhā /
ñ: prayogo 'tra -- īśvaraḥ sarvajño 'khila.kṣityādi.kārya.kartṛtvāt /
ñ: yo hi yasya kartā sa tad.upādānādy.abhijñah, yathā ghaṭotpādakaḥ kulālo mṛt.piṇḍādy.abhijñah, jagataḥ kartā cāyam, tasmāt sarvajña iti /
ñ: upādānam hi jagataḥ pārthivāpya.taijasavāyavīya.lakṣaṇāś caturvidhāḥ paramāṇavah, nimitta.kāraṇam adṛṣṭādi, bhoktā.ātmā, bhogyam tanv.ādi /
ñ: na caitad.anabhijñasya kṣityādau kartṛtvam sambhavaty asmad.ādivat /

ñ13: te ca tadīya.jñānādayo nityāh, kulālādi.jñānādibhyo vilakṣaṇatvāt /

ñ14: ekatvam ca kṣityādi.kartur aneka.kartṛṛṇām ekādhiṣṭhātṛ.niyamitānām pravṛtty.upapatteḥ siddham /
ñ: prasiddhā hi sthapatyādīnām ekasūtra.dhāraparatantrāṇām mahā.prāsādādi.kārya.karaṇe pravṛttiḥ /

ñ15: na ca īśvarasyaikarūpatve nityatve ca kāryāṇām kādācitkatvam vaicitryam ca virudhyata ity vācyam /
ñ: kādācitkavicitrasahakāri.lābhena kāryāṇām kādācitkatvavaicitryasiddhau virodhāsambhavāt /

ñ16: nanu kṣityāder buddhimadd.hetukatve.akriyā.darśito 'pi jīrṇa.kūpādiṣv iva kṛta.buddhir utpadyate (utpadyeta), na cātrasā.utpadyamānā dṛṣṭā, ato dṛṣṭānta.dṛṣṭasya hetor dharmiṇy.abhāvād asiddhatvam /

ñ: tad apy ayuktam, yataḥ prāmāṇikam itaram vāpeṣkya.idam ucyate (ucyeta) /
ñ: yadi.itaram tarhi dhūmādāv apy asiddhatvānuṣaGgaḥ /
ñ: prāmāṇikasya tu nāsiddhatvam, kāryatvasya buddhimat.kartṛ.pūrvakatvena pratipannāvinā.bhāvasya kṣityādau prasiddheh, parvatādau dhūmādivat /
ñ: na ca yāvantaḥ padārthāḥ kṛtakāḥ tāvantaḥ kṛta.buddhim ātmany āvirbhāvayanti.iti niyamo 'sti, khātaprapūritāyām bhuvy akriyā.darśinaḥ kṛta.buddhy.utpādābhāvāt /

ñ17: kim.ca, buddhimat.kāraṇa.bhāvo 'trānupaladbhito bhavatā prasādhyate /
ñ: etac.cāyuktam, dṛśyānupalabdher evābhāvasādhakatvopapatteḥ /
ñ: na ca.iyam atra sambhavati, jagat.kartur adṛśyatvāt /
ñ: anupalabdhasya cābhāvasādhyatve piśācāder api tat.prasaktiḥ syād iti /

ñ18: atra pratividhīyate, tatra yat tāvat kṣityāder buddhimadd.hetukatvasiddhaye kāryatvasādhanam uktam, tat kim sāvayavatvam 1, prāg.asataḥ sva.kāraṇasattā.samavāyaḥ 2, kṛtam.ity.pratyayaviṣayatvam 3, vikāritvam 4 vā syāt /
ñ: yadi sāvayavatvam, tadā.idam api kim avayaveṣu vartamānatvam 1, avayavair ārabhyamāṇatvam 2, pradeśavattvam 3, sāvayavam ity.buddhi.viṣayatvam 4 vā /
ñ: tatrādyapakṣe.avayavasāmānyenānaikāntiko 'yam hetuh, tadd.hy avayaveṣu vartamānam api niravayavam akāryam ca procyate /
ñ: dvitīyapakṣe tu sādhyasamo hetuh, yathā.eva hi.kṣityādeḥ kāryatvam sādhyam, evam paramāṇv.ādy.avayavārabhyatvam api /
ñ: tṛtīyo 'py ākāśenānaikāntikah, tasya pradeśavattve.apy akāryatvāt /
ñ: prasādhayiṣyate cāgrato 'sya pradeśavattvam / caturtha.kakṣāyām api tenaivānekānto na cāsya niravayavatvam, vyāpitvavirodhāt paramāṇuvat 1/

ñ19: nāpi prāg.asataḥ sva.kāraṇasattā.samavāyaḥ kāryatvam, tasya nityatvena tal.lakṣaṇāyogāt /
ñ: tal.lakṣaṇatve vā kāryasyāpi kṣityādes tadvan nityatvānuṣaGngāt, kasya buddhimadd.hetukatvam sādhyate /

ñ20: kim.ca, yoginām aśeṣa.karma.kṣaye pakṣāntah.pātiny apravṛttatvena bhāgāsiddho 'yam hetuh, tat.prakṣayasya prdhvamsābhāvarūpatvena sattā.sva.kāraṇasamavāyayor abhāvāt 2/

ñ21: kṛtam.iti.pratyayaviṣayatvam api na kartavyam, khananotsecanādinā kṛtam ākāśam ity akārye.apy ākāśe vartamānatvena. anaikāntikatvāt 3/

ñ22: vikāritvasyāpi kāryatve mahā.īśvarasyāpi kāryatvānuṣaGgah, sato vastuno 'nyathā.bhāvo hi vikāritvam /
ñ: tac ca.īśvarasyāpy asti.ity asyāpara.buddhimadd.hetukatvaprasaGgād anavasthā syāt, avikāritve cāsya kārya.kāritvam atidurghaṭam iti 4/
ñ: kāryasvarūpasya vicāryamāṇasyānupapadyamānatvād asiddhiḥ kāryatvād ity ayam hetuḥ /

ñ23: kim.ca, kādācitkam vastu loke kāryatvena prasiddham / jagatas tu mahā.īśvaravat sadā.sattvāt katham kāryam / 
ñ: tad.antar.gata.taruṇa.tṛṇādīnām kāryatvāt tasyāpi kāryatve mahā.īśvarāntar.gatānām buddhyādīnām paramāṇv.ādy.antar.gatānām pākajarūpānām ca kāryatvāt, mahā.īśvarāder api kāryatvānuṣaGgaḥ /
ñ: tathā cāsyāpy apara.buddhimadd.hetukatva.kalpanāyām anavasthāpasiddhāntaś cānuṣajyate /

ñ24: astu vā yathā.kathamcij jagataḥ kāryatvam, tathāpi kāryatvamātram iha hetutvena vivakṣitam, tad.viśeṣo vā /
ñ: yady ādyah, tarhi na tato buddhimat.kartṛ.viśeṣasiddhih, tena samam vyāpty.asiddheh, kim.tu kartṛ.sāmānyasya, tathā ca hetor akimcit.karatvam sādhyaviruddhasādhanād viruddhatvam vā /
ñ: tataḥ kāryatvam kṛta.buddhy.utpādakam, buddhimat.kartur.gamakam na sarvam /
ñ: sārūpyamātreṇa ca gamakatve bāṣpāder apy agnim prati gamakatvaprasaGgah, mahā.īśvaram pratyātmatvādeḥ sādṛśyāt samsāritva.kimcijjñatvākhila.jagad.akartṛtvānumāpakānuṣaGgah, tulyākṣepasamādhānatvāt /
ñ: tato bāṣpa.dhūmayoḥ kenacid amśena sāmye.api tathā kutaścid viśeṣād dhūmo 'gnim gamayati na bāṣpādih, tathā kṣityādi.itara.kāryatvayor api kaścid.viśeṣo 'bhyupagamyaḥ /

ñ25: atha dvitīyah, tarhi hetor asiddhatvam kāryaviśeṣasyābhāvāt, bhāve vā jīrṇa.kūpaprāsādādivad akriyā.darśino 'pi kṛta.buddhy.utpādakatvaprasaGgaḥ /
ñ: samāropān na.iti cet, so 'py ubhayatrāviśeṣataḥ kim na syād ubhayatra kartur atīndriyatvāviśeṣāt /
ñ: atha prāmāṇikasya asty evātra kṛta.buddhiḥ /
ñ: nanu katham tasya tatra kṛtatvāvagamo 'nenānumānāntareṇa vā /
ñ: ādhye.anyonyāśrayaḥ / tathā.hi -- siddhaviśeṣaṇādd hetor asyotthānam, tad.utthāne ca hetor viśeṣaṇasiddhir it /
ñ: dvitīyapakṣe.anumānāntarasyāpi saviśeṣa.hetor evotthānam, tatrāpy anumānāntarāt tat.siddhāv anavasthā /
ñ: tan na kṛta.buddhy.utpādakatvarūpaviśeṣaṇasiddhiḥ / tathā ca viśeṣaṇāsiddhatvam hetoḥ /

ñ26: yad ucyate -- 'khātapratipūrita.bhūmi.darśanena kṛtakānām ātmani kṛta.buddhy.utpādakatvaniyamābhāvah' ity tad apy asat, tatrākṛtrima.bhū.bhāgādi.sārūpyasya tad.anutpādakasya sad.bhāvāt tad.anutpādasyopapatteḥ /
ñ: na ca kṣityādāv apy akṛtrimasamsthānasārūpyam asti, yenākṛtrimatva.buddhir utpadyate tasyaivānabhyupagamāt, abhyupagame cāpasiddhāntaprasaktiḥ syād iti /
ñ: kṛta.buddhy.utpādakatvarūpaviśeṣaṇāsiddher viśeṣaṇāsiddham hetorḥ /

ñ27: sidhyatu vā, tathāpy asau viruddhah, ghaṭādāv iva śarīrādi.viśiṣṭasyaiva buddhimat.kartur atra prasādhanāt / [ddd]

ñ28: nanv evam dṛṣṭānta.dārṣṭāntikasāmānyānveṣaṇe sarvatra hetūnām anupapattir iti cen na / dhūmādy.anumāne mahānasasetarasādhāraṇasyāgneḥ pratipatteḥ /
ñ: atrāpy evam buddhimat.sāmānyaprasiddher na viruddhatvam ity apy ayuktam, dṛśyaviśeṣādhārasyaiva tat.sāmānyasya kāryatva.hetoḥ prasiddher nādṛśyaviśeṣādhārasya, tasya svapne.apy apratīteh, kharaviṣāṇādhāra.tat.sāmānyavat /
ñ: tato yādṛśāt kāraṇād yādṛśam kāryam upalabdham tādṛśād eva tādṛśam anumātavyam, yathā yāvad.dharmātmakād vahner yāvad.dharmātmakasya dhūmasyotpattiḥ sudṛḍhapramāṇāt pratipannā tādṛśād eva dhūmāt tādṛśasyaivāgner anumānam iti /

ñ29: etena 'sādhyasādhanayor viśeṣeṇa vyāptau gṛhyamāṇāyām sarvānumānocchedaprasakti' ity ādi.prāstam draṣṭavyam iti /

ñ30: tathākṛṣṭaprabhavais taru.tṛṇādibhir vyabhicāry ayam hetuḥ /

ñ31: īśvara.buddhyādibhiś ca vyabhicārah, teṣām kāryatve saty api samavāyi.kāranād īśvarād vibhinna.buddhimat.pūrvakatvābhāvāt / tad.abhyupagame cānavasthā /
ñ33: tathā kālātyayāpadiṣṭaś cāyam, akṛṣṭaprabhavāGkurādau kartr.bhāvasyādhyakṣeṇādhyavasāyāt / agner.anuṣṇatve sādhye dravyatvavat /

ñ33: astu vā tatra tat.sad.bhāvah, tathāpy asyādṛṣṭatve śarīrābhāvaḥ kāraṇam, vidyā.ādi.prabhāvah, jāti.viśeṣo vā /

ñ: nanu śarīrābhāve.api jñāna.icchā.prayatnāśrayatvena sva.śarīra.karaṇe kartṛtvam upapadyata ity apy asamīkṣitābhidhānam, śarīrasambandhenaiva tat.preraṇopapatteh, śarīrābhāve muktātmavat tad.asambhavāt /
ñ: śarīrābhāve ca jñānādyāśrayatvam apy asambhāvyam, tad.utpattav asya nimitta.kāraṇatvāt, anyathā muktātmano 'pi tad.utpatti.prasakteḥ /
ñ: vidyā.ādi.prabhāvasya cādṛśyatva.hetutve kadācid asau dṛśyeta / na khalu vidyā.bhṛtām śāśvatikam adśyatvam dṛśyate, piśācādivat /

ñ34: astu vā dṛśyo 'dṛśyo vāsau, tathāpi him sattāmātreṇa 1, jñānavattvena 2, jñāna.icchā.prayatnavattena 3, tat.pūrvakavyāpāreṇa 4, aiśvaryeṇa 5, vā kṣityādeḥ kāraṇam syāt /

ñ35: aiśvaryam api jñātṛtvam kartṛtvam anyad vā / jñātṛtvam cet tat kim jñātṛtvamātram sarva.jñātṛtvam vā /

ñ40: kim.ca kṣityāder buddhimat.pūrvakatve sādhye pradīyamānāḥ sarve.api hi hetavo viruddhāh, dṛṣṭāntānugraheṇa saśarīrāsarvajñāsarvakartṛ.pūrvakatvasādhanāt /

ñ41: vyabhicāriṇaś cāmī, buddhimantam antareṇāpi vidyud.ādīnām prādur.bhāvavibhāvanāt, svapnādy.avasthāyām abuddhimat.pūrvasyāpi kāryasya darśanāc ca.iti /

ñ42: kālātyayāpadiṣṭāś caite, pratyakṣāgama.bādhitapakṣānantaram prayuktatvāt / tad.bādhā ca pūrvam eva darśitā /

ñ43: prakaraṇasamāś.cāmī, prakaraṇa.cintā.pravartakānām hetv.antarāṇām sadbhāvāt /

ñ44: nityatvādīni tu viśeṣaṇāni tad.vyavasthāpanāyānīyamānāni śaṇḍham prati kāminyā rūpasampannarūpaṇaprāyāṇy akarṇanīyāny eva / (good eṣample)

ñ: tathā.hi -- na.īśvaro nityah, svabhāva.bhedenaiva kṣityādi.kārya.kartṛtvāt, apracyutānutpannasthiraikasvabhāvam kūṭastham nityam iti hi nityatva.lakṣaṇāny.upagamāt /

ñ76: atha ki.paṭāḥ prakaṭayanti -- nanu bhavatu suniścitāsambhavad.bādhakapramāṇatvāt sarvajñasiddhiḥ / kim tv asya kavalāhāra iti na mṛṣyāmahe /
ñ: tathā hi -- kevalinaḥ kavalāhāro na bhavati tat.kāraṇābhāvāt; na ca kāraṇābhāve kāryasyotpattir atiprasakteḥ /
ñ: na ca tat.kāraṇābhāve.asiddhih; āhārādānanidāna.bhūte vedanā.ādi.ṣaṭke ekasyāpi tasya kevaliny abhāvāt /
ñ: tathā hi -- na tāvat tasya vedanā.utpadyate; tad.vedanīyasya dagdharajju.sthānikatvāt /
ñ: satyām api vedanāyām na tasya tat.kṛtā pīḍā; anantavīryatvāt /
ñ: vaiyāvṛtty.akaraṇam tu bhagavati trailokyapūjye na sambhavaty eva.iti / īryā.patham punaḥ kevala.jñānāvaraṇa.kṣayāt samyag avalokayaty asau /
ñ: samyamas tu tasya yathā.ākhyāta.cāritriṇo niṣṭhitārthatvād anantavīryatvāc ca nāhāra.kāraṇī.bhavati /
ñ: prāṇavṛttir api tasyānapavartyāyuṣṭvād anantavīryatvāc cānyathāsiddhā.eva /
ñ: dharma.cintāvasaras tv apagatah; niṣṭhitārthatvāt / tad evam kevalinaḥ kāvalikāhāro bahu.doṣa.duṣṭatvān na ghaṭata iti /

ñ79: atrocyate -- tatra yat tāvad ūcānam 'tat.kāraṇābhāvād' iti sādhanam; tad asiddham; āhāra.kāraṇasya vedanīyasya kevalini tathā.eva sad.bhāvāt /
ñ: tathā ca kim.iti sā śārīrī sthitiḥ prāktanī na syāt / prayogo 'tra syāt -- kevalino bhuktiḥ samagrasāmagrīkatvāt pūrva.bhuktivat /
ñ: sāmagrī ca.iyam paryāptatvam vedanīyādaya āhārapaktinimittam taijasa.śarīram dīrghāyuṣṭvam ca.iti / sā ca samagrāpi kevalini samasti /

ñ80: yad api dagdharajju.sthānikatvam vedanīyasyocyate; tad apy anāgamikam ayukti.yuktam ca; āgame.atyantasātodayasya kevalini pratipādanāt /
ñ: yuktir api; yadi ghāti.karma.kṣayāj jñānānādayas tasya bhaveyur vedanīyodbhavāyāḥ kṣudhaḥ kim āyātam yenāsau na bhavati /

ñ81: na tayoś chāyā.tapayor iva sahānavasthāna.lakṣaṇo bhāvābhāvayor iva parasparaparihāra.lakṣaṇo vā kaścid virodho 'sti sātāsātayor antar.muhūrtaparivartamānatayā sātodayavad asātodayo 'py asti.ity anantavīryatve saty api śarīra.balāpacayaḥ kṣud.udbhavapīḍā ca bhavaty eva /
ñ: na cāhāra.grahaṇe tasya kimcit kṣūyate kevalam āho.puruṣikāmātram eva.iti /

ñ82: yad ucyate -- 'vedanīyasyodīraṇābhāvāt prabhūtatarapudgalodayābhāvah; tad.abhāvāc cātyantam pīḍābhāvah' iti; tad ayuktam; turyādi.guṇasthānakeṣu vedanīyasya guṇa.śreṇī.sadbhāvāt; pracurapudgalodaye saty api tat.kṛtapīḍālpatvasyaiva darśanāt; jine sātodayavat pracurapudgalodayābhāve.api tīvratvapradarśanāc ca.iti /

ñ83: yad apy ucyate 'āhārākāGkṣā kṣut, sā ca parigraha.buddhih, sā ca mohanīyavikārah; tasya cāpagatatvāt kevalino na bhuktih' iti; tad asamyak; yato mohanīyavipākāt kṣun na bhavati; tad.vipākasya pratipakṣa.bhāvanā.nivṛtyamānatvāt; krodhādīnām tathā.uparamopalabdheḥ /
ñ: yad uktam -- ''uvasameṇa haṇe koham'' [daśa vai.mā.8.39] ity ādi /
ñ: na ca kṣud.vedanīyam tadvad vipakṣa.bhāvanayā nivartyamānam dṛṣṭam; ato mohavipākasvabhāvā kṣud iti / 

ñ84: etena yad ucyate --
ñ: ''apavartyate kṛtārtham nāyur.jñānādayo na hīyante /
ñ: jagad.upakṛtāv anantam vīryam kim gata.tṛṣo bhuktiḥ //1// [kevali.bhukti.ślo.16] ityāadi nirastam /
ñ: evam.vidhāudārikatvādi.sāmagrī.sadbhāvena chadmasthāvasthāyām api kevalino 'bhukti.prasakteḥ /
ñ: samastavīryāntarāya.kṣayābhāvāt chadmasthasya bhuktir iti cet; tad ayuktam /
ñ: yataḥ kim tatrāyuṣkasyāpavartanam syāt kim vā caturṇām jñānānām kācidd hāniḥ syāt; yena bhuktiḥ /
ñ: tena yatha dīrgha.kālasthiter āyuṣkam kāraṇam evam āhāro 'pi; yathāsiddhagater vyuparata.kriyā.dhyāna.carama.kṣaṇaḥ kāraṇam evam samyak.vādikam api.ity anantavīryatāpi tasyāhāra.grahaṇe na virudhyate /
ñ: tathā tasya deva.chandādīni viśrāma.kāraṇāni gamananiṣīdanāni ca bhavanty evam āhāra.kriyāpi virodhābhāvāt / na ca balavattarasya vīryavato 'lpīyasī kṣud; vyabhicārāt /

ñ85: kim cāgamo 'pi kevalino bhuktim pratipādayati / tathā hi -- tattvārthasūtram ''ekādaśa jine'' [tasū.9.18] iti /
ñ: vyākhyā -- ekādaśa parīsahāḥ kṣut.pipāsā.ṣītoṣṇa.damśamaśaka.caryā.śayyā.vadharoga.tṛṇasparśamalākhyā jine kevalini bhavanti; tat.kāraṇasya vedanīyasyādyāpi vidyamānatvāt /
ñ: na ca kāraṇānucchede kāryasyocchedaḥ sambhāvyate; atiprasakteḥ /
ñ: ata eva kevalini kṣud.vedanīyapīḍā sambhāvyate; kim tv asāv anantavīryatvān na vihvalī.bhavati; na cāsau niṣṭhitārtho niḥprayojanam eva pīḍām sahate /

ñ86: na ca śakyate vaktum 'evam.bhūtam eva bhagavataḥ śarīram; yad uta kṣut.pīḍayā na bādhyate' iti; anumānena tasyās tatra siddhatvāt /
ñ: tathā hi -- kevali.śarīram kṣud.ādinā pīḍyate śarīratvāt; asmad.ādy.adhiṣṭhita.śarīravat /
ñ: tathā 'yathā tat.śarīram svabhāvena prasvedādi.rahitam evam prakṣepāhārarahitam api' ity apakarṇanīyam (apakarṣaṇīyam?) eva; apramāṇakatvāt /
ñ: tad evam deśo na pūrva.koṭi.kālasya kevali.sthiteḥ sambhavād audārika.śarīrasthiteś ca yathā.āyuṣkam kāraṇam evam prakṣepāhāro 'pi /
ñ: tathā hi -- taijasa.śarīreṇa mṛdū.kṛtasyābhyavahṛtasya svaparyāptyā pariṇāmitasyottarottarapariṇāma.krameṇāudārika.śarīriṇām anena prakāreṇa kṣud.udbhavo bhavanti /
ñ: tataḥ kena hetunāsau na bhuGta iti / na ca ghāti.catuṣṭayasya kṣud.vedanīyam prati sahakāri.kāraṇa.bhāvo 'sti; yena (tena?) tad.abhāvāt tad.abhāva ity ucyate.
ñ: iti siddhā kevali.bhuktiḥ / tathā prayogaś cātra -- kevalinaḥ prakṣepāhāro bhavati kavalāhāra.kevalitvayor avirodhāt; sāta.devanīyavat iti / iti kevali.bhukti.vyavasthāpanasthalam iti //

ñ87: atha tattvāny āha --
ñ: jīvājīvau tathā puṇyam pāpam āsravasamvarau /
ñ: bandho vinirjarāmokṣau nava tattvāni tan.mete //47//

ñ88: vyākhyā -- cetanā.lakṣaṇo jīvaḥ 1; tad.viparīta.lakṣaṇas tv ajīvaḥ 2/ dharmādharmākāśa.kālapudgala.bhedena tv asau pañcadhā vyavasthitaḥ /
ñ: anayor eva dvayour jagad.vartinaḥ sarve.api bhāvā antar.bhavanti /
ñ: na hi jñānādayo rūparasādayaś ca dravya.guṇā utkṣepaṇādīni ca karmāṇi sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyāś ca jīvājīvavyatirekeṇātmasthitim labhante; tad.bhedenaikāntatas teṣām anupalambhāt; teṣām tad.ātmakatvena pratipatteh; anyathā tad.asattvaprasaGgāt /

ñ89: baudhādi.parikalpita.duhkhādi.tattvāni jīvājīvābhyām pṛthag jāty.antaratayā na vaktavyāni; jīvājīvarāśi.dvayena sarvasya jagato vyāptatvāt; tad.avyāptasya śaśa.śṛGga.tulyatvāt /

ñ90: tarḥ puṇyapāpāsravādīnām api tataḥ pṛthag.upādānam na yukti.pradhānam syāt; rāśi.dvayena sarvasya vyāptatvād iti cen na; puṇyādīnām vipratipattinirāsārthatvāt; āsravādīnām sakāraṇasamsāramukti.pratipādanapara pṛthag.upādānasyāduṣṭatā /
ñ: yathā ca samvaranirjarayor mokṣa.hetutā; āsravasya bandhanibandhanatvam; puṇyapuṇya.dvi.bheda.bandhasya ca samsāra.hetutvam tathā.āgamāt pratipattavyam /

ñ91: tatra puṇyam śubhāḥ karmapudgalāḥ 3/ ta eva tv aśubhāḥ pāpam 4/ āsravati karma yataḥ sa āsravaḥ kāyavāG.mano.vyāpārah; puṇyāpuṇya.hetutayā cāsau dvividhaḥ 5/ āsravanirodhaḥ samvaro gupti.samiti.dharmānuprekṣā.ādīnām cāsravapratibandhakatvāt; sa ca dvividhaḥ sarva.deśa.bhedād 6/

ñ92: yoganimittaḥ sakaṣāyasyātmanaḥ karmavargaṇā.pudgalaiḥ samśleśaviśeṣo bandhah; sa ca sāmānyenaikavidho 'pi prakṛti.sthity.anubhāgapradeśa.bhedena caturdhā; punar ekaiko jñānāvaraṇādi.mūlaprakṛti.bhedād aṣṭadhā; punar api matyāvaraṇādi.tad.uttaraprakṛti.bhedād anekavidhaḥ /
ñ: ayam ca kaścit tīrthakaratvādi.phalanirvartakatvāt praśastah; aparaś ca nārakādi.phalanirvartakatvād apraśastah; praśastāpraśastātmapariṇāmodbhūtasya karmaṇaḥ sukha.duhkhasamvedanīyaphalanirvartakatvāt 7/
ñ: ātmasampṛkta.karmanirjaraṇa.kāraṇam nirjarā dvādaśavidha.tapo.rūpā /
ñ: sā ca .utkṛṣṭā śukla.dhyānarūpā ''tapasā nirjarā ca'' [tasū.9.3] iti vacanāt; dhyānasya cāntara.tapo.rūpatvāt 8/ 
ñ: vinirmuktāśeṣa.bandhanasya prāpta.jinasvarūpasyātmano lokānte.avasthānam mokṣah; ''bandhaviprayogo mokṣah'' iti vacanāt 9/ etāni navasamkhyāni tattvāni tan.mete jainamate jñātavyāni /

ñ93: atha śāstra.kāra eva tattvān krameṇa vyākhyāti; tatra yathā.uddeśam nirdeśa iti nyāyāt prathamam jīva.tattvam āha --
ñ: tatra jñānādi.dharmebhyo bhinnābhinnno vivṛttimān /
ñ: śubhāśubha.karmakartā mokṣā karmaphalasya ca //85//
ñ: caitanya.lakṣaṇo jīvo yaś caitad.viparītavān /
ñ: ajīvaḥ sa samākhyātaḥ puṇyam sat.karmapudgalāḥ //49// yugmam /

ñ94: vyākhyā -- tatra.iti nirdhāraṇārthaḥ / ye jñāna.darśana.cāritrasukha.duhkhavīrya.bhavyatvasattvaprameyatva.dravyatvaprāṇa.dhāritva.krodhādi.pariṇatatvasamsāritvasiddhatvaparavastu.vyāvṛttatvādayaḥ svaparaparyāyā jīvasya bhavanti; te jñānādayo dharmā ucyante /
ñ: tebhyo jīvo na bhinno nāpy abhinnaḥ kim.tu jāty.antaratayā bhinnābhinnaḥ /
ñ: yadi hi jñānādi.dharmebhyo jīvo bhinnaḥ syāt; tadā 'aham jānāmi; aham paśyāmi; aham jñātā; aham draṣṭā; aham sukhitah; aham bhavyaś ca' ityādy.abhedapratibhāso na syāt; asti ca sarvaprāṇinām so 'bhedapratibhāsaḥ /
ñ: tathā yady abhinnaḥ syāt tadā ''ayam dharmī; ete dharmāh'' iti bheda.buddhir nasyāt; asti ca sā /
ñ: athavābhinnatāyām jñānādi.sarva.dharmāṇām aikyam syāt; eka.jīvābhinnatvāt /
ñ: tathā ca 'mama jñānam mama darśanam cāsti' ityādi.jñānādi.dharmāṇām mitho.bhedapratītir na syāt /
ñ: asti ca sā / tato jñānādir dharmebhyo bhinnābhinna evābhyupagantavyaḥ /
ñ: anena dharmādharmiṇor vaiśeṣikādy.abhimatam bhedaikāntam saugatasvīkṛtam cābhedaikāntam pratikṣipati; saugatenāpi buddhi.kṣaṇaparamparā.rūpasyātmano dharmitvena svī.kārāt /

ñ95: 'tathā vividham vartanam vivṛttir narāmarādi.paryāyāntarānusaraṇam tadvān vivṛttimān /
ñ: anena bhavāntara.gāminam ātmānam prati vipratipannāmś cārvākān kūṭasthanityātmavādino naiyāyikādīn nirasyati /

ñ96: tathā śubhāśubhāni karmāṇi karoti.iti śubhāśubha.karma.kartā / tathā sva.kṛtasya karmaṇo yat phalam sukhādikam tasya sākṣād.bhoktā ca /
ñ: ca.kāro viśeṣaṇānām samuccaye / etena viśeṣaṇa.dvayenākartāram upacaritavṛttyā bhoktāram cātmānam manyamānānām sāmkhyānām nirāsaḥ /

ñ97: tathā caitanyam sākāranirākāropayogātmakam lakṣaṇam svarūpam yasya sa caitanya.lakṣaṇaḥ / 
ñ: etena jaḍasvarūpo naiyāyikādi.sammata ātmā vyavacchidyate /evam.viśeṣaṇo jīvaḥ samākhyāta ity atrāpi sambandhanīyam iti //

ñ98: cārvākāś carcayanti yathā -- iha kāyākārapariṇatāni cetanā.kāraṇa.bhūtāni bhūtāny evopalabhyante; na punas tebhyo vyatirikto bhavāntara.yāyī yathā.ukta.lakṣaṇaḥ kaścanāpy ātmā, tat.sadbhāve pramāṇābhāvāt /
ñ: tathā hi -- bhūtavyatiriktātmasadbhāve kim pratyakṣam pramāṇam pravartetotānumānam /
ñ: na ca 'ghaṭam aham vedmi' ity aham.pratyaye jñāna.kartṛtayā.ātmā bhūtavyatiriktaḥ pratibhāti.ity.abhidhātavyam; tasya 'sthūlo 'ham kṛśo 'ham' ityādivac śarīraviṣayatvasyaivopapatteḥ /
ñ: na khalu tat.pratyayasyātmālambanatvam asti; ātmani sthailyādi.dharmāsambhavāt / 
ñ: tathā 'ghaṭam aham vedmi' ity.asyāpi pratyayasya na śarīrād anyo bhavat.parikalpitaḥ kaścanāpy ātmā.ālambanatvena svapne.api pratīyate /
ñ: apratītasyāpi kalpane kalpanā.gauravam pratiniyatavastu.vyavasthāyā abhāvaś ca syāt /
ñ: na ca jaḍarūpasya śarīrasya ghaṭāder ivāham.pratyayo 'nupapanna iti vācyam; cetanā.yogena tasya sacetanatvāt /
ñ: na ca sa cetanā jīva.kartṛkā.iti vācyam; tasyāpratītatvāt tat.kartṛtvam ayuktam; khapuṣpāder api tat.prasaGgāt /
ñ: tataḥ prasiddhatvāt śarīrasyaiva caitanyam prati kartṛtvam yuktam / tad.anvayavyatirekānuvidhāyitvāc ca /
ñ: prayogaś cātra -- yat khalu yasyānvayavyatirekāv anukaroti tat tasya kāryam yathā ghaṭo mṛt.piṇḍasya; śarīrasyānvayavyatirekāv anukaroti ca caitanyam; tasmāt tat.kartṛtvam /
ñ: anvayavyatirekasamadhigamyo hi sarvatra kārya.kāraṇa.bhāvaḥ /
ñ: tau cātra vidyete; sati śarīre caitanyopalabdheh; asati cānupalabdheḥ /
ñ: na ca mṛta.śarīre caitanyānupalabdhes tad.anvayavyatirekānuvidhāyitvam asiddham iti vācyam; mṛtāvasthāyām vāyu.tejasor abhāvena śarīrasyaivābhāvāt; viśiṣṭa.bhūtasamyogasyaiva śarīratvapratipādanāt /
ñ: na ca śarīrākāramātre caitanyotpattir yuktāh; citra.likhita.turaGngamādiṣv api caitanyotpatti.prasaGgāat /
ñ: tataḥ siddham śarīra.kāryam eva caitanyam / tataś ca caitanyaprasahite śarīra evāham.pratyayotpattiḥ siddhā /
ñ: iti na pratyakṣaprameya ātmā; tataś cāvidyamāna eva / prayogaś cātra -- nāsty ātmā, atyantāpratyakṣatvāt; yad atyantāpratyakṣam tan nāsti; tatha (yathā) khapuṣpam /
ñ: yac cāsti tat pratyakṣeṇa gṛhyata eva; yathā ghaṭaḥ / aṇavo 'pi hy apratyakṣāh; kim.tu ghaṭādi.kāryatayā pariṇatās te pratyakṣatvam upayānti; na punar evam ātmā kadācid api pratyakṣa.bhāvam upagacchati; ato 'trātyanta.iti viśeṣaṇam iti na paramāṇubhir vyabhicāra iti /

ñ99: tathā nāpy anumānam bhūtavyati.riktātmasadbhāve pravartate; tasyāpramāṇatvāt; pramāṇatve vā pratyakṣa.bādhitapakṣaprayogānantaram prayuktatvena hetoḥ kālātyayāpadiṣṭatvāt / śarīravyatiriktātmapakṣo hi pratyakṣeṇaiva bādhyate /

ñ100: kim ca liGga.liGgi.sambandhasmaraṇapūrvakam hy anumānam / yathā -- pūrvam mahānasādāv agni.dhūmayor liGgi.liGgayor anvayavyatirekavantam avinābhāvam adhyakṣeṇa gṛhītvā tata uttara.kālam kvacit kāntāraparvatanitambādau gaganāvalambinīm dhūma.lekhām avalokya prāg.gṛhītasambandham anusmarati /
ñ: tad yathā -- yatra yatra dhūmas tatra tatra vahnim adrākṣam yathā mahānasādau; dhūmaś cātra dṛśyate tasmād vahnināpi.iha bhavitavyam ity evam liGga.grahaṇasambandhasmaraṇābhyām tatra pramātā huta.bhujam avagacchati /
ñ: na caivam ātmanā liGginā sārdham kasyāpi liGgasya pratyakṣeṇa sambandhaḥ siddho 'sti; yatas tat.sambandham asusmarataḥ punas tal.liGga.darśanāj jīve sa pratyayaḥ syāt /
ñ: yadi punar jīva.liGgayoḥ pratyakṣataḥ sambandhas.siddhiḥ syāt; tadā jīvasyāpi pratyakṣatvāpattyānumānavaiyarthyam syāt; tat eva jīvasiddher iti /

ñ101: na ca vaktavyam sāmānyato.dṛṣṭānumānād āditya.gativaj jīvaḥ sidhyati; yathā gatimān ādityo deśāntaraprāpti.darśanāt; devadattavad iti /
ñ: yato hanta devadatte dṛṣṭānta.dharmiṇi sāmānyena deśāntaraprāptir pūrvikā pratyakṣeṇaiva niścitā sūryo 'pi tām tathā.eva pramātā sādhayati.iti yuktam /
ñ: na caivam atra kvacid api dṛṣṭānte jīvasattvenāvinābhūtaḥ ko 'pi hetur adhyakṣeṇopalakṣyata ity ato na sāmānyato.dṛṣṭād apy anumānāt tad.gatir iti /
ñ: tathā nāpy āgama.gamya ātmā / avisamvādi.vacanāptapraṇītatvena hy āgamasya prāmāṇyam / na caivam.bhūtam avisamvādi.vacanam kamcanāpy āptam upalabhāmahe yasyātmā pratyakṣa iti /

ñ125: tathā rūpādi.j;nānam kvacid āśritam guṇatvāt; rūpādivat / tathā jñānasukhādikam upādāna.kāraṇapūrvakam kāryatvād ghaṭādivat /

ñ126: tathā sva.śarīre svasamvedanapratyakṣam ātmānam sādhayitvā para.śarīre.api sāmānyato.dṛṣṭānumānena sādhyate /

ñ130: nanu sarvam apy asambaddham idam / tathā hi -- mat.tri.loka.īśvaratvam tāvad asad eva niṣidhyate; pratiṣedhasyāpi pañcasamkhyā.viśiṣṭatvam apy avidyamānam eva nivāryate /

ñ159: athavā.idam anumānam -- samasti.indriyebhyo bhinno jīvo 'nyenopalabhyānyena vikāra.grahaṇāt / iha yo 'nyenopalabhyānyena vikāram pratipadyate sa tasmād bhinno dṛṣṭah; yathā pravaraprasādopari.pūrvavātāyanena ramaṇīyam avalokyāparavātāyanena samāyātāyās tasyāḥ karādinā kucasparśādi.vikāram upadarśayan devadattaḥ /

ñ160: athavā.indriyebhyo vyatirikta ātmānyenopalabhyānyena grahaṇāt / iha yo ghaṭādikam anyenopalabhyānyena gṛhṇāti sa tābhyām bhedavān dṛṣṭah; yathā pūrvavātāyanena ghaṭam upalabhyāparavātāyanena gṛhṇānas tābhyām devadattaḥ /
ñ: gṛhṇāti ca cakṣuṣā.upalabdham ghaṭādikam artham hastādinā jīvah; tatas tābhyām bhinna iti /

ñ161: evam atrānekāny anumānāni naikāś ca yuktayo viśeṣāvaśyaka.ṭīkā.ādibhyaḥ svayam kartavyāni.iti / proktam vistareṇa prathamam jīva.tattvam /

ñ162: ajīva.tattvam vyacikhyāsur āha -- 'yaś caitad.viparītam' ity ādi /
ñ: yaś caitasmād viparītāni viśeṣaṇāni vidyante yasyāsāv etad.viparītavān so 'jīvaḥ samākhyātaḥ /
ñ: 'yaś caitad.vaiparītyavān' iti pāṭhe tu yaḥ punas tasmāj jīvād vaiparītyam anyathātvam tadvān ajivaḥ sa samākhyātaḥ /
ñ: ajñānādi.dharmebhyo rūparasa.gandhasparśādibhyo bhinnābhinno narāmarādi.bhavāntarān anyuyāī jñānāvaraṇādi.karmaṇām akartā tat.phalasya cābhoktā jaḍasvarūpaś cājīva ity arthaḥ /

ñ163: sa ca dharmādharmākāśa.kālapudgala.bhedāt pañcavidho 'bhidhīyate / tatra dharmo lokavyāpī nityo 'vasthito 'rūpī dravyam astikāyo 'samkhyapradeśo gaty.upagraha.kārī ca bhavati /
ñ: atra nitya.śabdena svabhāvād apracyuta ākhyāyate / avasthita.śabdenānyūnādhika āvir.bhāvyate /
ñ: anyūnādhikaś cānādinidhanate yattābhyām (?) na svatantram vyabhicarati / tathārūpi.prahaṇād amūrta ucyate /
ñ: amūrtaś ca rūparasa.gandhasparśapariṇāma.bāhyavarty abhidhīyate / na khalu mūrtim sparśādayo vyabhicaranti; sahacāritvāt /
ñ: yatra hi rūpapariṇāmas tatra sparśarasa.gandhair api bhāvyam ataḥ sahacaram etac catuṣṭayam antataḥ paramāṇāv api vidyate /

ñ164: tathā dravya.grahaṇād guṇaparyāyavān procyate; 'guṇaparyāyavad dravyam' [tasū.5.38] iti vacanāt /

ñ165: tathāstayaḥ (asti-s) -- pradeśāḥ prakṛṣṭā deśāḥ pradeśā nirvibhāgāni khaṇḍāni.ity.arthaḥ / teṣām kāyaḥ samudāyaḥ kathyate /

ñ166: tathā lokavyāpī.iti vacanenāsamkhyapradeśa iti vacanena ca lokākāśapradeśarpamāṇapradeśo nirdiśyate / tathā svata eva gati.pariṇatānām jīvapudgalānām upakāra.karo 'pekṣā.kāraṇam ity arthaḥ /

ñ167: kāraṇam hi trividham ucyate; yathā ghaṭasya mṛt pariṇāmi.kāraṇam; daṇḍādayo grāhakāś ca nimitta.kāraṇam; kumbha.kāro nirvartakam kāraṇam / tad uktam --
ñ: ''nirvartakam nimittam pariṇāmī ca tridhā.iṣyate hetuḥ /
ñ: kumbhasya kumbha.kāro dhartā mṛc ca.iti samasamkhyam (?) //1//''

ñ168: nimitta.kāraṇam ca dvedhā nimitta.kāraṇam apekṣā.kāraṇam ca / yatra daṇḍādiṣu prāyogikī vaisrasikī ca kriyā bhavati tāni daṇḍādīni nimitta.kāraṇam /
ñ: yatra tu dharma.('dharmāstikāya').ādi.dravyeṣu vaisrasiky eva kriyā tāni nimitta.kāraṇāny api viśeṣa.kāraṇatā.jñāpanārtham apekṣā.kāraṇāny ucyante /

ñ169: dharmādi.dravya.gata.kriyā.pariṇāmam apekṣamāṇam jīvādikam gatyādi.kriyā.pariṇatim puṣṇāti.iti kṛtvā tato 'tra dharmo 'pekṣā.kāraṇam /
ñ: evam adharmo 'pi lokavyāpitādi.sakalaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭo dharmavan nirviśeṣam mantavyah; navaram sthity.upagraha.kārī svata eva sthiti.pariṇatānām jīvapudgalānām sthiti.viṣaye.apekṣā.kāraṇam vaktavyaḥ /

ñ170: evam ākāśam api lokālokavyāpakam anantapradeśam nityam avasthitam arūpi dravyam asti.kāyo 'vagāhopakārakam ca vaktavyam; navaram lokālokavyāpakam iti /

ñ171: ye kecanācāryāḥ kālam dravyam nābhyupayanti; kim.tu dharmādi.dravyāṇām paryāyam eva; tan.mate dharmādharmākāśapudgala.jīvākhyapañcāstikāyātmako lokaḥ /
ñ: ye tu kālam dravyam icchanti; tan.mete ṣaḍ dravyātmako lokah; pañcānām dharmādi.dravyāṇām kāla.dravyasya ca tatra sadbhāvāt /
ñ: ākāśa.dravyam ekam evāsti yatra so 'lokaḥ lokālokayor vyāpakam avagāhopakārakam iti svata evāgavāhamānānām dravyāṇām avagāha.dāyi bhavati; na punar anavagāhamānam pudgalādi balād avagāhayati /
ñ: ato nimitta.kāraṇam ākāśam ambuvan makarādīnām iti / alokākāśam katham avagāhopakārakam; anavagāhyatvād iti ced ucyat; tadd hi vyāpriyetaivāvakāśa.dānena yadi gati.sthiti.hetū dharmādharmāstikāyau tatra syātām; na ca tau tatra stah; tad.abhāvāc ca vidyamāno 'py avagāhana.guṇo nābhivyajyate kila('hāM').alokākāśasya.iti /

ñ172: kālo 'rdha.tṛtīya.dvīpāntar.vartī paramasūkṣmo nirvibhāga ekaḥ samayaḥ / sa cāstikāyo na bhaṇyate; ekasamayarūpasya tasya niḥpradeśatvāt / āha ca --
ñ: ''tasmān māṇuṣa.lokavyāpī kālo 'sti samaya eka iha /
ñ: ekatvā ca sa kāyo na bhavati kāyo hi samudāyaḥ //1//''
ñ: sa ca sūryādi.grahanakṣatrodayāstādi.kriyābhivyaGgyaikīyamatena dravyam abhidhīyate /
ñ: sa caikasamayo dravyaparyāyobhayātmā.eva; dravyārtharūpeṇa pratiparyāyam utpādavyaya.dharmāpi svarūpānanya.bhūta.kramākrama.bhāvy anādy.aparyavasānānantasamkhyaparimāṇah; ata eva ca sa svaparyāyapravāhavyāpī dravyātmanā nityo 'bhidhīyate /
ñ: atītānāgatavartamānāvasthāsv api kālaḥ kāla ity aviśeṣa.śruteḥ / yathā hy ekaḥ paramāṇuḥ paryāyair anityo 'pi dravyatvena sadā sann eva na kadācid asattvam bhajate; tathā.ekaḥ samayo 'pi.iti /

ñ173: ayam ca kālo na nirvartakam kāraṇam nāpi pariṇāmi kāraṇam; kimtu svayam sambhavatām bhāvānām asmin kāle bhavitavyam nānyadā.ity.apekṣā.kāraṇam /
ñ: kāla.kṛtā vartanā.ādyā vastūnām upakārāḥ / athavā vartanā.ādyā upakārāḥ kālasya liGgāni; tatas tān āha ''vartanā pariṇāmaḥ kriyā paratvāparatve ca'' [tasū.5.22] tatra vartante svayam padārthāh; teṣām vartamānānām prayojikā kālāśrayā vṛttir vartanā; prathamasamayāśrayā sthitir ity arthaḥ 1/ 
ñ: pariṇāmo dravyasya sva.jāty.aparityāgena parispanda.itaraprayogajasvabhāvaḥ pariṇāmaḥ /
ñ: tad yathā -- vṛṣkasyāGkuramūlādy.avasthāḥ pariṇāmah; āsīd aGkurah; samprati skandhavān aiṣamaḥ puṣṭiṣyati.iti / puruṣa.dravyasya bāla.kumāra.yuvādy.avasthāḥ pariṇāmaḥ / evam anyatrāpi /

ñ174: pariṇāmo divvidhah; anādiru amūrteṣu dharmādiṣu; mūrteṣu tu sādir abhra.indra.dhanur.ādiṣu stambha.kumbhāmbhoruhādiṣu ca /
ñ: ṛtu.vibhāga.kṛto velā.vibhāga.kṛtaś ca pariṇāmas tulya.jātīyānām vanaspatyādīnām ekasmin kāle vicitro bhavati 2/

ñ175: prayogavisrasabhyām(visrasā 'decay') janito jīvānām pariṇamanavyāpāraḥ karaṇam kriyā tasyā anugrāhakaḥ kālaḥ / tad.yathā -- naṣṭo ghaṭah; sūryam paśyāmi; bhaviṣyati vṛṣṭir ityādikā atītādi.vyapadeśāḥ parasparāsamkīrṇā yad.apekṣayā pravartante sa kālaḥ 3/

ñ176: idam param idam aparam iti.pratyayābhidhāne kālanimitte /

ñ177: tad evam vartanā.ādy.upakārānumeyaḥ kālo dravyam māṇuṣa.kṣetre / manuṣya.lokād bahiḥ kāla.dravyam nāsti /
ñ: santo hi bhāvās tatra svayam evotpadyate vyayanty avatiṣṭhante ca / astitvam ca bhāvānām svata eva; na tu kālāpekṣam /
ñ: na ca tatratyāḥ prāṇāpānanimeṣonmeṣāyuh.pramāṇādi.vṛttayaḥ kālāpekṣāh; tulya.jātīyānām sarveṣām yugapad.abhavanāt /
ñ: kālāpekṣā hy arthās tulya.jātīyānām ekasmin kāle bhavanti; na vijātīyānām /
ñ: tāś ca prāṇādi.vṛttayas tadvatām naikasmin kāle bhavanty uparamanti ca.iti /
ñ: tasmān na kālāpekṣās tāḥ / paratvāparatve api tatra cirācirasthity.apekṣe; sthitiś cāstitvāpekṣā; astitvam ca svata eva.iti /

ñ178: ye tu kālam dravyam na manyante, tan.mate sarveṣām dravyāṇām vartanā.ādayaḥ paryāyā eva santi; na tv apekṣā.kāraṇam kaścana kāla iti 4/

ñ179: atha pudgalāḥ / ''sparśarasa.gandhavarṇavantaḥ pudgalāh'' [tasū.5.23] / atra sparśa.grahaṇam ādau sparśe sati rasādi.sadbhāva.jñāpanārtham /
ñ: tato 'b.ādīni catur.guṇāni sparśitvāt; pṛthivīvat / tathā manaḥ sparśādimat; asarva.gata.dravyatvāt; pārthivāṇuvad iti prayogau siddhau /

ñ180: tatra sparśo hi mṛḍu.kaṭhina.guru.laghu.śītoṣṇasnigdharūkṣāḥ / atra ca snigdharūkṣa.śītoṣṇāś catvāra evāṇuṣu sambhavanti /
ñ: skandheṣv aṣṭāv api yathā.sambhavam abhidhānīyāḥ / rasās tikta.kaṭu.kaṣāyāmlamadhurāḥ /
ñ: lavaṇo madhurāntar.gata ity eke, samsargaja ity apare / gandhau surabhy.asurabhī / kṛṣṇādayo varṇāḥ / tadvantaḥ pudlagā iti /
ñ: na kevalam pudgalānām sparśādayo dharmāh; śabdādayaś ca.iti darśyate / ''śabda.bandhasaukṣmyasthaulyasamsthāna.bheda.tamaś.chāyā.tapo.dyotavantaś ca'' [tasū.5.24] pudgalāḥ /
ñ: atra pudgalapariṇāmāviṣkārī matupratyayo nitya.yogārtham vihitaḥ / tatra śabdo dhvaniḥ / bandhaḥ parasparāśleṣa.lakṣaṇaḥ prayogavisrasā.ādi.janita audārikādi.śarīreṣu jatu.kāṣṭhādi.śleṣavat paramāṇu.samyogajavad vā.iti /
ñ: saukṣmyam sūkṣmatā / sthaulyam sthūlatā / samsthānam ākṛtiḥ / bhedaḥ khaṇḍaśo bhavanam /tamaś.chāyā.ādayaḥ pratītāḥ / sarva evaite sparśādayaḥ śabdādayaś ca pudgaleṣv eva bhavanti.iti /

ñ181: pudgalā dvedhā; paramāṇavaḥ skandhāś ca / tatra paramāṇor lakṣaṇam idam --
ñ: ''kāraṇam eva tad antyam sūkṣmo nityaś ca bhavati paramāṇuḥ /
ñ: ekarasavarṇa.gandho dvi.sparśaḥ kārya.liGgaś ca //1//''

ñ182: vyākhyā -- sakala.bhedaparyantavartitvād anyam tad eva kāraṇam na punar anyad dvy.aṇukādi tad eva kim ity āha sūkṣmaḥ -- āgama.gamyah; asmad.ādī.indriyavyāpārātītatvāt /
ñ: nityaś ca.iti -- dravyārthikanayāpekṣayā tu nīlādibhir ākārair anitya eva.iti /
ñ: na tataḥ param. āṇīyo dravyam asti, tena paramāṇuḥ / tathā pañcānām rasānām dvayor gandhayoḥ pañcavidhasya varṇasyaikena rasādinā yuktaḥ / (five colors)
ñ: tathā caturṇām sparśānām madhye dvāv aviruddhau yau sparṣau snigdhoṣṇau snigdha.śītau rūkṣāśītau rūkṣoṣṇau vā; tābhyām yuktaḥ /
ñ: tathā kāryam dvy.aṇukādy.acitta(?).mahā.skandhaparyantam tasya liGgam iti /
ñ: evam.vidha.lakṣaṇā niravayavāḥ paraspareṇāsamyuktāḥ paramāṇavaḥ / skandhāḥ punr dvy.aṇukādayo 'nantāṇukaparyantāḥ sāvayavāḥ prāyo.grahaṇād ādānādi.vyāpārasamarthāḥ paramāṇu.samghātā iti /

ñ183: ete dharmādharmākāśa.kālapudgalā jīvaiḥ saha ṣaḍ dravyāṇi / eṣv ādyāni catvāry eka.dravyāṇi; jīvāḥ pudgalāś cāneka.dravyāṇi; pudgalarahitāni tāni pañcāmūrtāni; pudgalās tu mūrtā eva.iti /
ñ: nanu jīva.dravyasyārūpiṇo 'py upayogasvabhāvatvena svasamvedanasamvedyatvād astitvam śraddhānapatham avatārayitum śakyam / dharmādharmāstikāyādīnām tu na jātucid api svasamvedanasamvedyatvam samasti; acetanatvāt /
ñ: nāpi parasamvedanavedyatā; nityam arūpitvena / tat katham teṣām dharmāstikāyādīnām satām sattā śraddheyā syād iti ced ucyate; pratyakṣeṇa yo 'rtho nopalabhyate sa sarvathā nāsty eva; yathā śaśaviṣāṇam ity ekāntena na mantavyam /
ñ: yata iha loke dvividhānupalabdhir bhavati; tatraikāsato vastuno 'nupalabdhih; yathā turaGgamottamāGgasamsargānuṣaGgi.śṛGgasya; dvitīyā tu satām apy arthānām anupalabdhir bhavati /
ñ: yā ca satsv abhāvānām api bhāvānām anupalabdhih; sātrāṣṭadhā bhidyate / tathā hi -- atidūrāt; atisāmīpyāt; indriya.ghātāt; manaso 'navasthānāt; saukṣmyāt; āvaraṇāt; abhibhavāt; samānābhihārāc ca.iti /

ñ184: tatrātidūrād deśa.kālasvabhāvaviprakarṣāt trividhānupalabdhiḥ / tatra deśaviprakarṣād yathā kaścid devadatto grāmāntaram gato na dṛśyate; tat katham sa nāsti /
ñ: so 'sty eva; deśaviprakarṣān nopalabdhiḥ / evam samudrasya para.taṭam merv.ādikam vā sad api nopalabhyate /
ñ: tathā kālaviprakarṣād bhūtā nijapūrvajādayo bhaviṣyā vā padmanābhādayo jinā nopalabhyante; abhūvan bhaviṣyanti ca te /
ñ: tathā svabhāvaviprakarṣān nabho.jīvapiśācādayo nopalabhyante, na ca te na santi /

ñ185: tathātisāmīpyād yathā netrekaj jalam nopalabhyate tat katham tan nāsti/ tad asty eva; punar atisāmīpyān nopalabhyate /

ñ186: tathā.indriya.ghātād yathāndha.badhirādayo rūpa.śabdādīn nopalabhyate tat katham rūpādayo na santi / santy eva; te punar indriya.ghātān nopalabhyante /

ñ187: tathā mano 'navasthānād yathā anavasthita.cetā na paśyati / uktam ca --
ñ: ''iṣu.kāranaraḥ kaścid rājānam saparicchadam /
ñ: na jānāti puro yāntam yathā dhyānam samācaret //1//'' (see ṭokunaga's paper ''ṭhe ṣiṣ ṭeachers of Bodhya'' in Festschrift for Prof.Mikogami)
ñ: tat kim rājā na gataḥ / sa gata eva; punar.anavasthita.cetaskatvān na dṛṣṭavān / naṣṭa.cetasām vā sato 'pi bhāvasyānupalabdhiḥ /

ñ188: tathā saukṣmyād yathā jālakāntara.gata.dhūma.ūṣmanīhārādīnām trasareṇavo nopalabhyante; paramāṇv.dvy.aṇukādayo vā sūkṣmanigodādayo nopalabhyante; tat kim na santi / santy eva te; punaḥ saukṣmyān nopalabdhiḥ / (ArdhMāghḍic; nigodaka.jīva 'souls of a ṇigoda, a particular vegetable kingdom')

ñ189: tathā.āvaraṇāt kuḍyādi.vyavadhānāj jñānādyāvaraṇād vānupalabdhih; tatra vyavadhānād; yathā kuḍyāntare vyavasthitam vastu nopalabhyate; tat kim nāsti /
ñ: kim.tu tad asty eva; punar vyavadhānān nopalabdhih; evam sva.karṇa.kandharāmastakapṛṣṭāni nopalabhyante; candramaṇḍalasya ca sann api para.bhāgo na dṛśyate; arvāg.bhāgena vyavahitatvāt /

ñ190: jñānāvaraṇāc cānupalabdhih; yathā mati.māndyāt satām api śāstrasūkṣmya(sūkṣma/saukṣmya?).arthaviśeṣaṇānām anupalabdhih; sato 'pi vā jaladhi.jalapalapramāṇasyānupalabdhih; vismṛter vā pūrvopalabdhasya vastuno 'nupalabdhih; moāt satām api tattvānām jīvādīnām anupalabdhir ityādi /

ñ191: tathābhibhavāt; sūryādi.tejasābhibhūtāni grahanakṣatrāṇi nopalabhyante; tat katham teṣām abhāvah; kim.tu tāni santy eva; punar abhibhhavān na dṛśyante / evam andha.kāre.api ghaṭādayo nopalabhyante /

ñ192: samānābhihārāc ca yathā mudgarāśau mudgamuṣṭis tularāśau tilamuṣṭir vā kṣiptā satī su.upalakṣitāpi nopalabhyate; jale kṣiptāni lavaṇādīni vā nopalabhyante / tat katham teṣām abhāvaḥ / tāni santy eva; punaḥ samānābhihārān nopalabdhiḥ /

ñ193: tathā coktam sāmkhyasaptatau 7/
ñ: ''atidūrāt sāmīpyād indriya.ghātān mano 'navasthānāt /
ñ: saukṣmyād vyavadhānād abhibhāvāt samānābhihārāc ca //1//'' iti /
ñ: evam aṣṭadhā sat.svabhāvānām api bhāvānām yathānupalambho 'bhihito evam dharmāstikāyādayo 'pi vidyamānā api svabhāvaviprakarṣān nopalabhyanta iti mantavyam /

ñ194: āha paro ye.atra deśāntara.gata.devadattādayo darśitāh; te.atrāsmākam apratyakṣā api deśāntara.gata.lokānām keṣāmcit pratyakṣā eva santi tena teṣām sattvam pratīyate; dharmāstikāyādayas tu kaiścid api kadāpi nopalabhyante tat katham teṣām sattām niścīyate /
ñ: atrocyate; yathā devadattādayaḥ keṣāmcit pratyakṣatvāt santo niścīyante, tathā dharmāstikāyādayo 'pi kevalinām prtyakṣatvāt kim na santaḥ praīyantām /
ñ: yathā vā paramāṇavo nityam aprtyakṣā api sva.kāryānumeyāḥ syuh; tathā dharmāstikāyādayo 'pi kim na sva.kāryānumeyā bhaveyuḥ /
ñ: dharmāstikāyādīnām kāryāṇi cāmūni / tatra dharmo gaty.upagraha.kāryānumeyah; adharmaḥ sthity.upagra.kāryānumeyah; avagāhopakārānumeyam ākāśam; vartanā.ādy.upakārānumeyaḥ kālah; pratyakṣānumānāvaseyāś ca pudgalāḥ /

ñ195: nanv ākāśādayaḥ sva.kāryānumeyā bhavantu; dharmādharmau tu katham /

ñ: na ca dṛṣṭa eva mātā.pitr.ādikas tasya hetur iti vaktavyam; dṛṣṭa.hetu.sāmye.api; surūpa.itarādi.bhāvena dehādīnām vaicitrya.darśanāt; tasya cādṛṣṭa.śubhāśubha.karamākhya.hetum antareṇābhāvāt /

ñ227: atha samvara.bandhau vivṛṇoti /

ñ229: atha bandha.tattvam āha -- 'bandho jīvasya karmaṇah' ityādi / tatra bandhanam bandhah; parasparāśleṣo jīvapradeśapudgalānām kṣīranīravat; athavā badhyate yenātmā pāratantryam āpadyate jñānāvaraṇādinā sa bandhaḥ pudgalapariṇāmaḥ /

ñ252: atra sāmkhyā bruvate / iha śuddha.caitanyasvarūpo 'yam puruṣah; tṛṇasya kubjī.karaṇe.apy aśaktatvād akartā; sākṣād abhoktā; jaḍām prakṛtim sakriyām āśritaḥ /

ñ272: atrocyate -- strīṇām puruṣebhyo hīnatvam kim cāaritrādy.abhāvena, viśiṣṭasāmarthyāsattvena; puruṣānabhivandyatvena, smāraṇādy.akartṛtvena (kṣāraṇādy-); amaharṛddhikatvena; māyā.ādi.prakarṣavattvena vā /

ñ308: sambhavo 'pi samudāyena samudāyino 'vagama ity evam.lakṣaṇaḥ sambhavati khāryā droṇa ityādiko nānumānāt pṛthak / khārī droṇavatī; khārītvāt pūrvopalabdha.khārīvat / (Hindi khārī=18 droṇa-s)

ñ310: yad api prātibham akṣa.liGga.śabdavyāpārānapekṣam akasmād eva 'adya me mahī.pati.prasādo bhavitā' ity ākāram spaṣṭatayā vedanam udayate tad apy anindriyanibandhanatayā mānasam iti -- pratyakṣa.kukṣinikṣiptam eva /

ñ312: evam yukty.anupalabdhyor ādi.śabdād viśiṣṭopalabdhi.janakasya bodhābhodharūpaviśeṣa.tyāgena sāmānyato ''likhitam sākṣiṇo bhuktiḥ pramāṇam trividham smṛtam'' [Vasiṣṭhaḍhṣ.16.10, Yājn.smṛti.vyavahāra.1.65ab, etc.] ity uktasya pramāṇasyānyeṣām ca keṣāmcit pramāṇāntaratvena paraparikalpitānām yathā.lakṣaṇam pratyakṣaparokṣayor antar.bhāvo nirākaraṇam ca vidheyam /

ñ314: sāmvyavahārikam dvedhā; cakṣur.ādi.indriyanimittam mano.nimittam ca / tad dvividham api caturdhā; avagraha.īhāvāya.dhāraṇā.bhedāt /

ñ315: atra ca pūrvapūrvasya pramāṇatā.uttarottarasya ca phalatā.ity.ekasyāpi mati.jñānasya cāturvidhyam kathamcit pramāṇaphala.bhedaś copapannaḥ /

ñ316: dhāraṇā.svarūpā ca mati.visamvādasvarūpasmṛti.phalasya hetutvāt pramāṇam; smṛtir api tathā.bhūtapratyavamarśasvabhāvasamjñā.phala.janakatvāt; samjñāpi tathā.bhūta.tarkasvabhāva.cintā.phala.janakatvāt; cintāpy anumāna.lakṣaṇābhinibodhaphala.janakatvāt; so 'pi hānādi.buddhi.janakatvāt /

ñ317: atha parokṣam -- aviśadam avisamvādi jñānam parokṣam / smaraṇapratyabhijñāna.tarkānumānāgama.bhedatas tat pañcadhā /

ñ: anubhavasmaraṇa.kāraṇakam saGkalanam pratyabhijñānam; tad eva.idam tat.sadṛśam tad.vilakṣaṇam tat.pratiyogi.ityādi; yathā sa evāyam devadattaḥ go.sadṛśo gavayo go.vilakṣo mahiṣa idam asmād dīrgham hrasvam aṇīyo mahīyo davīyo vā dūrād ayam tīvro vahniḥ surabhi.idam candanam ityādi /
ñ: atrādi.śabdāt sa eva vahnir anumīyate sa evānenāpy arthaḥ kathyata ityādi smaraṇasacivānumānāgamādi.janyam ca samkalanam udāhāryam /

ñ318: anumānam dvividhā; svārtham parārtham ca / hetu.grahaṇasambandhasmaraṇa.hetukam sādhyavijñānam svārtham /

ñ: pakṣa.hetu.vacanātmakam parārtham anumānam upācārāt / mandamatīms tu vyutpādayitum dṛṣṭāntopanayanigamanāny api prayojyāni /
