ñ311: nav atra niścitānyathānupapattir evaikam hetor lakṣaṇam abhyadhāyi kim napakṣa.dharmatvādi.trairūpyam iti ced ucyate; pakṣa.dharmatvādau trairūpye saty api tat.putratvāder hetor gamakatvādarśanāt; asaty api ca trairūpye hetor gamakatva.darśanāt; tathā hi -- jala.candrān nabhaś.candrah; kṛttikā.udayāt śakaṭodayah; puṣpitaika.cūtataḥ puṣpitāḥ śeṣa.cūtāh; śaśāGkodayāt samudravṛddhih; sūryodayāt padmākara.bodhah; vṛkṣāt chāyā caite pakṣa.dharmatā.virahe.api sarva.janair anumīyante /

ñ320: āptavacanāj jātam artha.jñānam āgamah; upacārād āptavacanam ca yathāsty atra nidhih; santi merv.ādayaḥ /

ñ321: tata ekasyaiva jñānasya yatrāvisamvādas tatra pramāṇatā; itaratra ca tad.ābhāsatā; yathā timirādy.upaplutam jñānam candrādāv avisamvādakatvāt pramāṇam tat.samkhyā.ādau ca tad eva visamvādakatvād apramāṇam /

ñ322: athottarārdham vyākhyāyate / 'ananta.dharmakam vastu' ityādi / iha pramāṇādhikāre pramāṇasya pratyakṣasya parokṣasya ca viṣayas tu grāhyam punar ananta.dharmakam vastu; anantās tri.kālaviṣayatvād aparimitā dharmāḥ --

ñ323: nanu katham ekasmin vastuny anantā dharmāḥ pratīyanta iti ced ucyate; pramāṇaprameyarūpasya sakalasya kramākrama.bhāvy.ananta.dharmākrāntasyaikarūpasya vastuno yathā.eva svapara.dravyādy.apekṣayā sarvatra sarvadā sarvapramātṛṛṇām pratītir jāyamānāsti tathā.eva vayam ete sauvarṇa.ghaṭa.dṛṣṭāntena savistaram darśayāmaḥ /

ñ: dravyatas tu yadā paudgaliko ghaṭo vivakṣyate; tadā sa paudgalika.dravyatvenāsti; dharmādharmākāśādi.dravyatvais tu nāsti /

ñ326: bhāvataḥ punaḥ sa pītavarṇenāsti na punar nīlādi.varṇaiḥ / pīto 'pi so 'parapīta.dravyāpekṣayā.eka.guṇapītah; sa eva ca tad.aparāpekṣayā dvi.guṇapītah; sa eva ca tad.anyāpekṣayā tri.guṇapītah; evam tāvad vaktavyam yāvat kasyāpi pīta.dravyasyāpekṣayānanta.guṇapītaḥ /

ñ384: sāmkhyaḥ sattvarajas.tamobhir anyonyam viruddhair guṇair grathitam pradhānam abhidadhāna ekasyāḥ prakṛteḥ samsārāvasthāmokṣasamayayoḥ pravartananivartana.dharmau viruddhau svī.kurvāṇaś ca katham svasyānekāntamatavaimukyam ākhyātum īśaḥ syāt / (sāmkhya)

ñ385: mīmāmsakās tu svayam eva prakārāntareṇaikānekādy.anekāntam pratipadyamānās tat.pratipattaye sarvathā paryanuyogam nārhanti /
ñ: athavā śabdasya tat.sambandhasya ca nityatvaikāntam prati te.apy evam paryanuyojyāḥ / tri.kāla.śūnya.kāryarūpārthaviṣayavijñānotpādikā nodanā.iti mīmāmsakābhyupagamaḥ / 
ñ: atra kāryatāyās tri.kāla.śūnyatve.abhāvapramāṇasya viṣayatā syāt; arthatve tu pratyakṣādi.viṣayatā bhavet; ubhayarūpatāyām punar nodanāyā viṣayatā.iti //

ñ386: atha bauddhādi.sarva.darśanābhīṣṭā dṛṣṭāntāyuktayaś cānekāntasiddhaye samākhyāyante /
ñ: bahddhādi.sarva.darśanāni samśaya.jñānam ekam ullekha.dvayātmakam prtijānāti nānekāntam pratikṣipanti 1/
ñ: tathā svapakṣasādhakam parapakṣocchedakam ca viruddha.dharmādhyastam anumānam manyamānāḥ pare.anekāntam katham parākuryuḥ 2/
ñ: mayūrāṇḍarase nīlādayaḥ sarve.api varṇā naikarūpā nāpy anekarūpāh; kim tv ekānekarūpā yathāvasthitāḥ tathā.ekānekādy anekānto 'pi / tad uktam nāmasthāpanādy.anekāntam āśritya --
ñ: ''mayūrāṇḍarase yadvad varṇā nīlādayaḥ sthitāḥ /
ñ: sarve.apy anyonyasammiśrās tadvan nāmādayo ghaṭe //1//
ñ: nānvayaḥ sa hi bheditvān na bhedo 'nvayavṛttitaḥ /
ñ: mṛd.bheda.dvayasamsargavṛtti jāty.antaram gaṭaḥ /2//
ñ: atra hi.śabdo hetau yasmād arthe; sa ghaṭaḥ /
ñ: bhāge simho naro bhāge yo 'rtho bhāga.dvayātmakaḥ /
ñ: tam abhāgam avibhāgena narasimham pracakṣate //3//
ñ: na naraḥ simharūpatvān na simho nararūpataḥ /
ñ: śabdavijñāna.kāryāṇām bhedāj jāty.antaram hi saḥ //4//
ñ: trairūpyam pāñcarūpyam vā bruvāṇā hetu.lakṣaṇam /
ñ: sad.asattvādi sarve.pi kuttaḥ pare na manvate //5//

ñ389: yathā.ekasyaivanarasya pitṛtvaputratvādy.anekasambandhā bhinnanimittā na virudhyate / tad yathā -- sa naraḥ svapitr.apekṣayā putraḥ svasutāpekṣayā tu tā.ityādi /
ñ: abhinnanimitās tu sambandhā virudhyate / tad yathā -- svapitr.apekṣayā.eva sa pitā putraś ca.ityādi /
ñ: evam anekānto 'pi dravyātmanā.ekam paryāyātmanā tv anekam ityādi bhinnanimittatayā na virudhyate /
ñ: dravyātmanā.evaikam anekam ca.ityādi tva abhinnanimittatayā virudhyate /
ñ: abhinnanimittatvam hi virodhasya mūlam na punar bhinnanimittatvam iti sukha.duhkhanara.devādi.padārthā apy ātmano nityānityatvādy anekāntam antareṇa nopapadyante yathā sarpasya dravyasya (ñain sarpa.dravyasya) sthirasyotphaṇaviphaṇāvasthe mitho viruddhe api dravyāpekṣayā na viruddhe; yathā.ekasyā aGglyāḥ saralatā.vināśo vakratā.utpattiś ca; yathā vā go.rase sthāyini dugdhaparyāyavināśottara.dadhi.paryāyotpādau sambhavantau pratyakṣādi.pramāṇenopalabdhau; evam sarvasya vastuno dravyaparyāyātmakatāpi /

ñ388: kim ca; sarveṣv api darśaneṣu svābhimatasādhyādhanāyābhidhīyamānā hetavo 'py anekāntābhyupagamam antareṇa samīcīnatām añcati /
ñ: tathā hi -- atra svopajñam eva para.hetu.tamo.bhāskaranāmakam vādasthalam likhyate / 
ñ: yathā -- iha hi sakala.tārkika.cakra.cūḍāmaṇitayā.ātmānam manyamānāḥ sarvadāpi prasabham poṣitasvābhimānā guṇavatsu vidvatsu matsaram vidadhānā mugdha.janasamāje'apy ūrjitasphūrjitam abhidadhānāḥ spaṣṭodbhavena svānubhavena samastavastu.stoma.gatam abhrāntam anekāntam anubhavanto 'pi svayam ca yuktyānekāntam eva vadanto 'pi prakaṭam vacanamātreṇaivānekāntam anicchanto yathāvasthitam vastu.svarūpam apaśyanto nijamatānurāgam eva puṣṇanto vidvat.samīpe ca kadāpi samyagg.hetu.svarūpam apṛcchanto nija.buddhyā ca tad anavagacchanto bhavanto yat sādhyasādhanāya sāhanam adhunābhyadhuh; tatrapi sādhyasiddhinibandhanam hetuḥ /
ñ: ato 'nekāntavyavasthāpanārtham yathāvasthitam vastu.svarūpam darśayadbhiḥ sadbhir asmābhiḥ prathamato hetor eva svarūpam samyag anekāntarūpam prakāśyate /
ñ: tāvad dattāvadhānā nirastasvapakṣābhimānāḥ kṣaṇam mādhyasthyam bhajantaḥ śṛṇvantu bhavantaḥ /
ñ: tathā hi -- yuṣmad.upanyastena hetunā kim anvayinā svasādhyam sādhyeta vyatirekiṇā vānvayavyatirekiṇā vā /
ñ: yadi tāvad anvayinā; tadā tat putratvāder api gamakatvam syāt; anvayamātrasya tatrāpi bhāvāt /
ñ: nāpi vyatirekiṇā; tat putratvāder eva gamakatvaprasaGgāt /
ñ: śyāmatvābhāve.anyatra gaurapakṣe vipakṣe tat putrādita eva sādhyasiddhi.prasaktiḥ / 
ñ: anvayavyatirekiṇā cet; tadāpi tat.putratvādita eva sādhyasiddhi.prasaktiḥ / na cāsya trairūpya.lakṣaṇana.yogino hetv.ābhāsatāśaGkanīyā; anityatvasādhane kṛtakatvāder pi tat.prasaGgāt /
ñ: asti ca bhavad.abhiprāyeṇa trairūpyam tat.putrādāv iti / 

ñ389: atha bhavatv ayam doṣo teṣām pakṣa.dharmatvasapakṣasattvavipakṣāsattvarūpe trairūpye.avinābhāvaparisamāptih; nāsmākam pañca.lakṣaṇa.hetu.vādinām; asmābhir asat.pratipakṣatvapratyakṣāgamābādhitaviṣayatvayor api lakṣaṇayor abhyupagamād iti cet /

ñ390: tarhi kevalānvaya.kevalavyatirekānumānayoḥ pañca.lakṣaṇatvāsambhavenāgamakatvaprasaGgaḥ /
ñ: na ca tayor agamakatvam yaugair iṣṭam; tasmāt pratibandhaniścāyakapramāṇāsambhavena; anyathānupapatteḥ /
ñ: aniścaya eva tat.putratvāder agamakatā.nibandhanam astu / nanu trailakṣaṇyādy.abhāvaḥ / 

ñ391: athātra vipakṣe.asattvam niścitam nāsti; tarhi śyāmatvābhāve tat.putratvenāvaśyam nivartanīyam ity atra pramāṇam asti.iti saugataḥ /
ñ: yaugas tu garjati / śākādyāhārapariṇāmaḥ śyāmatvena samavyāptiko na tu tat.putratvena.ity upādhi.sadbhāvān na tat.putratve vipakṣāsattvasambhava iti /

ñ392: tau hy evam niścitānyathānupapattim eva śabdāntareṇa śaraṇī.kuruta iti sā.eva hetor lakṣaṇam astu /
ñ: api ca; asti nabhaś.candro jala.candrāt; udeṣyati śvaḥ savitā; adyatanādityodayād ityādiṣu pakṣa.dharmatvābhāve.api man.mātā.iyam evam.vidhasvarānyathānupapatteḥ /
ñ: sarvam kṣaṇikam akṣaṇikam vā sattvād ityādiṣu ca sapakṣasyābhāve.api hetūnām gamakatva.darśanāt kim trairūpyādinā /

ñ393: niścitānyathānupapattir evaikam liGga.lakṣaṇam akṣūṇam (akṣuṇṇam? Hindi nirdoṣa) tattvam etad eva; prapañcaḥ punar ayam iti cet tarhi saugatenābādhitaviṣayatvam asat.pratipakṣam jñātatvam ca yaugena ca jñātatvam lakṣaṇam ākhyānīyam /

ñ394: atha vipakṣān niścitavyāvṛtti.mātreṇābādhitaviṣayatvam asat.pratipakṣatvam ca jñāpaka.hetv.adhikārāj jñātatvam ca labdham eva.iti cet; tarhi gamaka.hetv.adhikārād aśeṣam api labdham eva.iti kim śeṣeṇāpi prapañcena.iti /
ñ: ata eva nānvayamātrādd hetur gamako 'pi tv ākṣiptavyatirekād anvayaviśeṣāc ca /
ñ: nāpi vyatirekamātrāt kim tv aGgī.kṛtānvayād vyatirekaviśeṣāt / na cāpi parasparānanuviddha.tad.ubhayamātrāt; api tu paraspararūpājahad.vṛttānvayavyatirekatvāt; niścitānyathānupapatty.eka.lakṣaṇasya hi hetor yathā.pradarśitānvayavyatirekarūpatvāt /
ñ: na ca jainānām hetor eka.lakṣaṇatābhidhānam anekāntasya vighātakam iti vaktavyam; prayoganiyama evaika.lakṣaṇo hetur ity abhidhānāt /
ñ: na tu svabhāvaniyame niyataikasvabhāvasya śaśa.śṛGgāder iva nihsvabhāvatvād iti katham na hetor anekāntātmatā /

ñ395: tathā nanu bhoḥ (ñain bhoḥ bhoh) sakarṇāḥ pratipāṇi.prasiddhapramāṇapratiṣṭhitānekāntaviruddha.buddhibhir mahadbhir anyaiś ca kaṇa.bhakṣākṣapāda.buddhādi.śiṣyakair upanyasyamānāḥ sarva eva hetavo vivakṣayāsiddhaviruddhānaikāntikatām svī.kurtanti.ity avagantavyam /
ñ: tathā hi -- pūrvam tāvat teṣām viruddhatābhidhīyate / yadi hy ekasyaiva hetos trīṇi pañca vā rūpāṇi vāstavāny abhyupagamyante; tadā so 'neka.dharmātmakam eva vastu sādhayati.iti katham na viparyayasiddhih; ekasya hetor aneka.dharmātmakasyābhyupagamāt /
ñ: na ca yad eva pakṣa.dharmasya sapakṣa eva sattvam tad eva vipakṣāt sarvato vyāvṛttatvam iti vācyam; anvayavyatirekayor bhāvābhāvarūpayoḥ sarvathā tādātmyāyogāt; tattvena vā kevalānvayī kevalavyatirekī vā sarvo hetuḥ syān na tu tri.rūpaḥ pañcarūpo vā / tathā ca sādhanābhāvo 'pi gamakaḥ syāt / 

ñ396: atha vivakṣāsattvam nābhyupeyate kim tu sādhyasadbhāve.astitvam eva sādhyābhāvenāstitvam abhidhīyate na tu tatas sad.bhinnam cet tad asat /
ñ: evam hi vipakṣāsattvasyābhāvādd hetos trairūpyādi na syāt / atha tatas tad.anyad.dharmāntaram; tarhy ekarūpasyānekātmakasya hetos tathā.bhūtasādhyāvinā.bhūtatvena niścitasyānekāntavastuprasādhanāt katham na paropanyasta.hetūnām sarveṣām viruddhatā; ekāntaviruddhenānekāntena vyāptatvāt /

ñ397: tathāsiddhatāpi sarvasādhana.dharmāṇām unneyā; yato hetuḥ sāmānyam vā bhaved viśeṣo vā tad ubhayam vānubhavam vā / 
ñ: na tāvat sāmānyam hetuḥ / tadd hi sakalavyāpi sakalasvāśrayavyāpi vā hetutvenopādīyamānam pratyakṣasiddham vā syāt tad anumānasiddham vā syāt /
ñ: na tāvat pratyakṣasiddham; pratyakṣam hy akṣānusāritayā pravartate / akṣam ca niyata.deśādinā.eva nikṛṣyate /
ñ: ato 'kṣānusāri jñānam niyata.deśādāv eva pravartitum utsahate, na sakala.kāla.deśavyāpini /

ñ398: atha niyata.deśasvarūpāvyatirekāt tan.niścaye tasyāpi niścaya iti cen na; niyata.deśasvarūpāvyatireke niyata.deśatā.eva syān na vyāpitā /
ñ: tan na vyāpi.sāmānyarūpo hetuḥ pratyakṣasiddhaḥ / anumānasiddhatāyām anavasthā.rākṣasī durnivārā /
ñ: anumānena hi liGga.grahaṇapūrvakam eva pravartamānena sāmānya sādhyate liGgam ca na viśeṣarūpam iṣyate; ananugamāt /
ñ: sāmānyarūpam tu liGgam avagatam vānavagatam vā bhavet / na tāvad anavagatam; aniṣṭatvād atiprasaGgāca ca /
ñ: avagatam cet tadā tasyāvagamaḥ pratyakṣeṇānumānena vā / na pratyakṣeṇa; samnikṛṣṭa.grāhitvāt tasya /
ñ: nāpy anumānena / tasyāpy anumānam antareṇa liGga.grahaṇe punas tad evāvartate /
ñ: tathā cānumānānām ānantyād yugasahasrair apy eka.liGga.grahaṇam na bhavet /
ñ: api ca; aśeṣavyaktyādheyasvarūpam sāmānyam pratyakṣānumānābhyām niścīyamānam svādhāraniścayam utpādayet / svādhāraniścayo 'pi nijādhāraniścayam iti sakalo janaḥ sarvajñaḥ prasajyate /

ñ399: kim ca; svāśraya.indriyasamyogāt prāk sva.jñānam ajanayat sāmānyam paścād api na taj janayet; avicalitarūpatvāt / parair evādheyātiśayatvāc ca vicalitatva ādheyātiśayatve ca kṣaṇikatā.āpattiḥ /

ñ400: anyac ca; tat.sāmānyam vyaktibhyo bhinnam abhinnam; bhinnābhinnam vā hetur bhavet / na tāvad bhinnam; vyaktibhyaḥ pṛthag anupalambhāt /

ñ401: samavāyena vyaktibhiḥ saha sāmānyasya sambandhitatvāt pṛthag.anupalambha iti cen na; samavāyasya.iha.buddhi.hetutvam gīyate; iha.idam iti buddhiś ca bheda.grahaṇam antareṇa na bhavet /
ñ: kim ca; ato 'śvatvādi.sāmānyam svāśrayasarva.gatam vā; sarrvasarva.gatam vā.iṣyate/ 
ñ: yadi svāśrayasarva.gatam; tadā karkādi.vyakti.śūnye deśe prathamataram upajāyamānāyā vyakter aśvatvādi.sāmānyena yogo na bhavati; vyakti.śūnye deśe sāmānyasyānavasthānād vyakty.antarād anāgamanāc ca /
ñ: atha sarvasarva.gatam tat svī.kriyate; tadā karkādibhir eva śābaleyādibhir api tad abhivyajyeta /
ñ: na ca karkādyānām eva tad.abhivyaktau sāmarthyam na śābalebhyādīnām iti vācyam; yataḥ kim.rūpam tat karkādyānām sāmarthyam /
ñ: sādhāraṇarūpatvam iti cen na; svataś cet sādhāraṇarūpā vyaktayah; tadā svata eva tā aśvo 'śva ity anuvṛttam pratyayam janayiṣyanti.iti kim tad.bhinnasāmānyaparikalpanayā /
ñ: yadi ca svato 'sādhāraṇarūpā vyaktayah; tadāparasāmānya.yogād api na sādhārāṇā bhaveyuḥ svato 'sādhāraṇarūpatvāt; iti vyakti.bhinnasya sāmānyasyābhāvād asiddhas tal.lakṣaṇo hetuḥ / katham tataḥ sādhyasiddhir bhavati /

ñ402: atha vyakty.abhinnam sāmānyam hetus tad apy ayuktam; vyakty.abhinnasya vyakti.svarūpavad vyakty.antarānanugamāt sāmānyarūpatānupapatter vyakty.abhinnatvasya sāmānyarūpatāyāś ca mitho.virodham /
ñ: atha bhinnābhinnam iti cen na; virodhāt / atha kenāpy amśena bhinnam kenāpy abhinnam iti / tad api na yuktam; sāmānyasya niramśatvāt/ tan naikāntasāmānyarūpo hetuḥ sākalyena siddhaḥ /

ñ403: nāpi viśeṣarūpah; tasyāsādhāraṇatvena gamakatvāyogāt; sādhāraṇatva evānvayopapatteḥ / nāpi sāmāyaviśeṣobhayam parasparānanuviddham hetur ubhaya.doṣaprasaGgāt /
ñ: nāy anubhayam; anyonyavyavacchedarūpāṇām ekābhāve dvitīyavidhānād anubhayasyāsattvena hetutvāyogāt /
ñ: buddhi.prakalpitam ca sāmānyam avastu.rūpatvāt sādhyenāpratibaddhatvād asiddhatvāc ca na hetuḥ / tad evam sāmānyādīnām asiddhatve tal.lakṣaṇāḥ sarve.api hetavo 'siddhā eva /

ñ404: tathā pratibandhavikalāḥ samastā api paropanyastā hetavo 'naikāntikā avagantavyāḥ / na caikāntasāmānyayor viśeṣayor vā sādhyasādhanayoḥ pratibandha upapadyate /
ñ: tathā hi -- sāmānyayor ekāntena nityayoḥ parasparam anupakāryopakāraka.bhūtayoḥ kaḥ pratibandhah; mithaḥ kārya.kāraṇādi.bhāvenopakāryopakāratve tv anityatvāpatteḥ /
ñ: viśeṣayos tu niyata deśa.kālayoḥ pratibandha.grahe.api tatraiva tayor dhvamsāt sādhya.dharmiṇy agṛhītapratibandha evānyo viśeṣo hetutvenopādīyamānaḥ katham nānaikāntikaḥ /

ñ405: kim ca pratibandhaḥ pakṣa.dharmatvādike liGga.lakṣaṇe sati sambhavī; na ca sādhyasādhanayoḥ parasparato dharmiṇaś caikāntena bhede.abhede vā pakṣa.dharmatvādi.dharma.yogo liGgasyopapattimān; sambandhāsiddheḥ /

ñ406: sambandho hi sādhyasādhanayor dharmiṇaś ca kim samavāyah; samyogah; virodhah; viśeṣaṇaviśeṣya.bhāvah; tādātyam; tad.utpattir vā bhavet /
ñ: na tāvat samavāyah; tasya dharma.dharmi.dvayātiriktasya pramāṇenāpratīyamānatvāt; iha tantuṣu paṭa ityādes tat.sādhakasya pratyayasyālaukikatvāt; pāmsulapādānām api.iha paṭe tantava ity evam pratīti.darśanāt; iha bhū.tale ghaṭābhāva ity atrāpi samavāyaprasaGgāt /
ñ: sattve vā samavāyasya svata eva dharma.dharmyādiṣu vṛtty.abhyupagame tadvat sādhyādi.dharmāṇām api svata eva dharmiṇi vṛttir astu kim vyarthayā samavāya.kalpanayā /
ñ: samavāyasya samavāyāntareṇa vṛtty.abhyupagame tu tatrāpy aparasamavāya.kalpane.anavasthā.nadī dustarā /
ñ: astu samavāyasya svataḥ parato vā vṛttih; tathāpi tasya pratiniyatānām eva sambandhinām sambandhakatvam na syād; api tv anyeṣām api vyāpakatvena; tasya sarvatra tulyatvād ekasvabhāvatvāc ca /

ñ407: nāpi samyogah; sa hi sādhyasādhanādīnām bhavan kim tato bhinno vā syād abhinno vā /
ñ: prāci pakṣe katham vivakṣitānām evaiṣa kim nānyeṣām api / bhedāviśeṣāt; na ca samavāyo 'traniyāmakaḥ tasya sarvatra sadṛśatvāt /
ñ: dvitīye tu sādhyādīny eva syuh; na kaścit samyogo nāma kathamcid bhinnasamyogāGgī.kāre tu paravādāśrayaṇam bhavet /

ñ408: nāpi virodho 'bhidhātavyah; tasyāpy ekāntamate.asambhavāt / sa hi sahānavasthānam parasparaparihāro vā bhavet /
ñ: tatrādye kim kadācid apy ekatrānavasthānam uta kiyat.kālam sthitvā paścād anavasthānam /
ñ: ādye pakṣe.ahinakulādīnām na virodhaḥ syād anyathā trailokye.apy uragādīnām abhāvaḥ /
ñ: dvitīye tu naravanitā.āder api virodhaḥ syāt; tayor api kimcit.kālam ekatra sthitvāpagamāt /
ñ: kim ca vaḍavānala.jaladhi.jalayor vidyud.ambhodāmbhasoś ca cirataram ekatrāvasthātaḥ katham ayam virodhaḥ /
ñ: parasparaparihāras tu sarva.bhāvānām aviśiṣṭaḥ katham asau pratiniyatānām eva bhavet /

ñ409: nāpi viśeṣaṇaviśeṣya.bhāvo ghaṭām iyarti; tasya samyogādy.asambhave.abhāvāt tasya tu prāg eva nirāsāt /

ñ410: nāpi sādhyasādhanayos tādātmyam ghaṭate; sādhyasādhanayor asiddhasiddhayor bhedābhyupagamena tādātmyāyogāt; --
ñ: tādātmye ca sādhyam sādhanam caikataram eva bhaven na dvayam kathamcit tādātmye tu jainamatānupraveśaḥ syāt /

ñ411: tad.utpattis tu kārya.kāraṇa.bhāve sambhavī kārya.kāraṇa.bhāvaś cārtha.kriyā.siddhau sidhyet /
ñ: artha.kriyā tu nityasya kramākramābhyām sahakāriṣu satsv asatsu ca janakājanakasvabhāva.dvayānabhyupagamena nopapadyate /
ñ: anityasya tu sato 'sato vā sā na ghaṭate sataḥ samasamayavartini vyāpārāyogāt; --
ñ: vyāpāre vā svasva.kāraṇa.kāla eva jātānām uttarottarasarva.kṣaṇānām eka.kṣaṇavartitvaprasaGgāt; sakala.bhāvānām mithaḥ kārya.kāraṇa.bhāvaprasakteś ca; atasaś ca sakala.śakti.vikalatvena kārya.kāraṇāsambhavāt; anyathā śaśaviṣāṇāder api tat.prasaGgāt /
ñ: tad ittham sādhyādīnām sambandhānupapatter ekāntamate pakṣa.dharmatvādi hetu.lakṣaṇam asamgatam eva syāt; tathā ca pratibandho durupapāda eva /

ñ412: tathā.ekāntavādinām prtibandha.grahaṇam api na jāghaṭīti; avicalitasvarūpa ātmani jñānapaurvāparyābhāvāt; pratikṣaṇa.dhvamsiny api kārya.kāraṇāady.ubhaya.grahaṇānuvṛttyā.eka.caitanyābhāvāt /

ñ413: na ca kāryādy.anubhavānantara.bhāvinā smaraṇena kārya.kāraṇa.bhāvādiḥ pratibandho 'nusamdhīyata iti vaktavyam anubhuta eva smaraṇaprādur.bhāvāt /
ñ: na ca pratibandhaḥ kencid anubhūtah; tasyobhayaniṣṭhatvāt / ubhayasya pūrvāpara.kāla.bhāvina ekenāgrahanād iti na pratibandhaniścayo 'pi /

ñ414: tad evam ekāntapakṣe parair uccāryamāṇaḥ sarvo 'pi hetuḥ pratibandhasyābhāvād aniścayāc cānaikāntika eva bhavet /

ñ415: evam ca kevalasya sāmānyasya viśeṣasya ca dvayor vā parasparaviviktayos tayor hetutvāghaṭanād anuvṛttavyāvṛttapratyayanibandhanaparasparasamvalitasāmānyaviśeṣātmano hetor anekāntātmani sādhye gamakatvam abhyupagantavyam /

ñ416: na ca yad eva rūpam rūpāntarād vyāvartate tad eva katham anuvṛttim āsādayati; yac cānuvartate tat katham vyāvṛttim āśrayati iti vaktavyam; anuvṛttavyāvṛttarūpatayādhyakṣataḥ pratīyamāne vastu.rūpe virodhāsiddheh; sāmānyaviśeṣavac citra.jñānavac citrapaṭasyaika.citrarūpavad vā /

ñ417: kim ca; ekāntavādy.upanyasta.hetoḥ sādhyam kim sāmānyam āhosvid viśeṣa utobhayam parasparaviviktam utasvid anubhayam iti vikalpāḥ /
ñ: na tāvat sāmānyam; kevalasya tasyāsambhavād artha.kriyā.kāritvavaikalyāc ca /
ñ: nāpi viśeṣah; tasyānanuyāyitvena sādhayitum aśakyatvāt / nāpy ubhayam; ubhaya.doṣānativṛtteḥ /
ñ: nāpy anubhayam; tasyāsato hetv.avyāpakatvena sādhyatvāyogāt / tasmād vivādāspadī.bhūtasāmānyaviśeṣobhayātmakasādhya.dharmasya sādhya.dharmiṇi sādhnāyānyonyānuviddhānvayavyatirekasvabhāva.dvayātmaika.hetoḥ pradarśane leśato 'pi naikāntapakṣokta.doṣāvakāśaḥ sambhavī; ato 'nekāntātmakam hetu.svarūpam cāvaśyam aGgī.kartavyam; --
ñ: anyathā sakalānumāneṣu sādhyasādhanānām uktanyāyata uccheda eva bhavet /
ñ: tasmād bho ekantavādin; nijapakṣābhimāna.tyāgenāviṣādino 'kṣiṇī nimīlya buddhi.dṛśam unmīlya madhyasthavṛttyā yuktyānusāraikapravṛttyā tat.tattvam jijñāsanto bhavanto 'nekāntam kāntam vicārayantu; --
ñ: pramākaikamūlasakala.yukti.yuktam prāg.uktanikhila.doṣavipramukham tat.tattvam cādhigacchantu / 
ñ: iti para.hetu.tamo.bhāskaranāmakam vādasthalam / tataḥ siddham sarva.darśanasammatam anekāntamatam //57// (siddhānta)

ñ418: atha jainamatam samkṣepayann āha --
ñ: jaina.darśanasamkṣepa ity eṣa gadito 'naghaḥ /
ñ: pūrvāparaparāghāto yatra kvāpi na vidyate //58//

ñ419: vyākyā- jaina.darśanasya samkṣepo vistarasyāgādhatvena vaktum aśakyatvād upayogasāraḥ samāsa ity amunā.uktaprakāreṇaiva -- pratyakṣo gadito.ābhihito 'nagho nirdūṣaṇaḥ sarvavaktavyasya sarvajñamūlatve doṣa.kāluṣyānavakāśāt /
ñ: yatra jaina.darśane kvāpi kvacid api jīvājīvādi.rūpavicāraṇā.viṣayasūkṣmamati.caryāyām api pūrvāparayoḥ pūrvapaścād.abhihitayoḥ parāghātaḥ parasparavyāhatatvam na vidyate; --
ñ: ayam bhāvaḥ -- yathāpara.darśanasambandhiṣu mūla.śāstreṣv api kim punaḥ pāścātyavipralambhaka.grathita.grantha.kathāsu prathamapaścād.abhihitayor mitho.virodho 'sti; --
ñ: tathā jaina.darśane kvāpi kevali.praṇīta.dvādaśāGgeṣu pāramparya.grantheṣu ca susambaddhārthatvāt sūkṣma.īkṣikayā nirīkṣito 'pi sa nāsti /
ñ: yat tu para.darśaneṣv api kvacana sahṛdaya.hṛdayam.gamāni vacanāni kānicid ākarṇayāmas tāny api jinoktasūktasudhā.sindhu.samudgatāny eva samgṛhya mudhā svātmānam bahu manyante / yat śrī.siddhasenapādāḥ --
ñ: ''suniścitam naḥ para.tantra.yuktiṣu sphuranti yāḥ kāścana sūkti.sampadaḥ /
ñ: tavaiva tāḥ pūrvamahārṇavotthitā jagat.pramāṇam jinavākyavipruṣaḥ //1//'' [dvātrimśa.] iti /

ñ420: atra pare prāhuḥ -- aho ārhatāh; arhad.abhihita.tattvānurāgibhir yuṣmābhir idam asambaddham evāvirbhāvayam.babhūve yad uta yuṣmad.dharśaneṣv api pūrvāparayor virodho 'sti.iti /
ñ: na hy asman.mate sūkṣma.īkṣaṇair īkṣamāṇo 'pi virodha.leṣo 'pi kvacana nirīkṣyate; amṛta.karanikaleṣv (ñain kara.karanikareṣv) iva kālimā.iti ced ucyate; bhoh, svamatapakṣapātam parihṛtya mādhyasthyam avalambamānair nirabhimānaiḥ pratibhā.ādy.avadhānam vidadhānair (ñain pratibhāvadbhiryadyavadhānam vidadhānair, Hindi pratibhāke kāna khaḍe karake sāvadhānīse) niśamyate; tadā vayam bhavatām sarvam darśayāmaḥ /

ñ421: tathā hi prathamam tāvat tāthāgatasammate mate pūrvāparavirodha udbhāvyate /
ñ: pūrvam sarvam kṣaṇa.bhaGguram abhidhāya paścād evam abhidadhe ''nānanukṛtānvayavyatirekam kāraṇam; nākāraṇam viṣayah'' [  ] iti /
ñ: asyāyam arthaḥ -- jñānam arthe saty evotpadyate na punar asati.ity anukṛtānvayavyatireko 'rtho jñānasya kāraṇam / yataś cārthāj jñānam utpadyate tam eva tad viṣayī.karoti.iti /
ñ: evam cābhidadhānenārthasya kṣaṇa.dvayam sthitir abhihitā / tad yathā -- arthāt kāraṇāj jñānam kāryam jāyamānam dvitīye kṣaṇe jāyate na tu samasamaye kāraṇa.kāryayoḥ samasamayatvāyogāt / 
ñ: tac ca jñānam sva.janakam evārtham gṛhṇāti nāparam ''nākāraṇam viṣayah'' [  ] iti vacanāt / tathā cārthasya.kṣaṇa.dvayam sthitir balād.āyātā sā ca kṣaṇa.kṣayeṇa viruddhā.iti pūrvāparavirodhaḥ /

ñ422: tathā nākāraṇam viṣaya ity uktvā yogi.pratyakṣasyātītānāgatādir apy artho viṣayo 'bhyadhāyi /
ñ: atītānāgataś ca vinaṣṭānutpannatvena tasya kāraṇam na bhavet / akāraṇam api ca tam viṣayatayābhidadhānasya pūrvāparavirodhaḥ syāt /

ñ423: evam sādhyasādhanayor vyāpti.grāhakasya jñānasya kāraṇatvābhāve.api tri.kāla.gatam artham viṣayam vyāharamāṇasya katham na pūrvāparavyāghātah; akāraṇasya pramāṇaviṣayatvānabhyupagamāt /

ñ424: tathā kṣaṇa.kṣayābhyupagame.anvayavyatirekayor bhinna.kālayoḥ pratipattir sambhavati / tataḥ sādhyasādhanayos tri.kālaviṣayam vyāpti.grahaṇam manvānasya katham pūrvāparavyāhatiḥ /

ñ425: tathā kṣaṇa.kṣayam adhāyi --
ñ: ''ita ekanavatau (ṣuali ekanavate) kalpe śaktyā me puruṣā hataḥ /
ñ: tat.karmaṇo vipākena pāde viddho 'smi bhikṣavaḥ //1//'' ity atra śloke janmāntaraviṣaye me.śabdāsmi.śabdayoḥ prayogam kṣaṇa.kṣayaviruddham bruvāṇasya buddhasya katham na pūrvāparavirodhaḥ /

ñ426: tathā niramśam sarvam vastu prāg.procya himsā virati.dāna.cittasvasamvedanam tu sva.gatam sad.dravya.cetanatvasvargaprāpaṇa.śaktyādikam gṛhṇad api sva.gatasya sad.dravyatvāder ekasyāmśasya nirṇayam utpādayati na punaḥ sva.gatasyāpi dvitīyasya svargaprāpaṇa.śaktyāder amśasya.iti sāmśatām paścād vadataḥ saugatasya katham pūrvāparaviruddham vaco na syāt /

ñ427: evam nirvikalpakam adhyakṣam nīlādikasya vastunaḥ sāmastyena grahaṇa kurvāṇam api nīlādy.amśe nirṇayam utpādayati na punar nīlādy.artha.gate kṣaṇa.kṣaye.amśa iti sāmśatām abhidadhataḥ saugatasya pūrvāparavaco.virodhaḥ subodha eva /

ñ428: tathā hetors trairūpyam samśayasya collekha.dvayātmakatām abhidadhāno 'pi sa sāmśam vastu yan na manyate tad api pūrvāparaviruddham /

ñ429: tathā parasparānāśliṣṭā evāṇavaḥ pratyāsatti.bhājaḥ samuditā ghaṭādi.rūpatayā pratibhāsante na punar anyonyam aGgāGgi.bhāvarūpeṇārabdhaskandha.kāryās ta iti hi baudhamatam /
ñ: tatra cāmī doṣāḥ / parasparaparamāṇūnām anāśliṣṭatvād ghaṭaṣyaika.deśe hastena dhāryamāṇe kṛtsnasya ghaṭasya dhāraṇam na syāt; utpṣepāvakṣepāpakarṣāś ca tathā.eva na bhaveyuḥ /
ñ: dhāraṇādīni ca ghaṭasyārtha.kriyā.lakṣaṇam sattvam aGgī.kurvāṇaiḥ saugatair abhyupagatāny eva tāni ca tan.mete.anupapannāni / tato bhavati pūrvāparayor virodhaḥ /

ñ430: atha naiyāyikavaiśeṣikamatayoḥ pūrvāparato vyāhatatvam darśyate / sattā.yogaḥ sattvam ity uktvā sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyānām sattā.yogam antareṇāpi sad.bhāvam bhāṣamāṇānām katham na vyāhatam vaco bhavet /

ñ431: jñānam svātmānam na vetti svātmani kriyā.virodhād ity abhidhāya.īśvara.jñānam svātmani kriyā.virodhābhāvena svasamveditam icchatām katham na svavacanavirodhaḥ / pradīpo 'py ātmānam ātmanā.eva prakāśayan svātmani kriyā.virodham vyapākaroti /

ñ432: parapañcanātmakāny api chala.jātinigrahasthānāni tattvarūpatayā.upadiśato 'kṣapādarṣer vairāgyavyāvarṇanam tamasaḥ prakāśātmakatā.prakhyāpanam iva katham na vyāhanyate /

ñ433: ākāśasya niravayavatvam svī.kṛtya tad.guṇaḥ śabdas tad.ekadeśa eva śrūyate na sarvatra.iti sāvayavatām bruvāṇasya katham na virodhaḥ /

ñ434: sattā.yogaḥ sattvam yogaś ca sarvair vastubhiḥ sāmśatāyām eva bhavati sāmānyam ca niramśam ekam abhyupagamyate; tataḥ katham na pūrvāparato vyāhatiḥ /

ñ435: samavāyo nitya ekasvabhāvaś ca.iṣyate sarvaiḥ samavāyibhiḥ sambandhaś ca naiyatyena jāyamāno 'nekasvabhāvatāyām eva bhavati; tathā ca pūrvaparavirodhaḥ subodhaḥ /

ñ436: arthavat pramāṇam ity atrārthaḥ sahakārī yasya tad arthavat pramāṇam ity abhidhāya yogi.pratyakṣam atītādy.arthaviṣayam abhidadhānasya pūrvāparavirodhaḥ syāt; atītādeḥ sahakāritvāyogāt /

ñ437: tathā smṛtir gṛhīta.grāhitvena na pramāṇa iṣyata anartha.janyatvena vā / gṛhīta.grāhitvena smṛter aprāmāṇye dhārā.vāhi.jñānānām api gṛhīta.grāhitvenāprāmāṇyaprasaGgaḥ /
ñ: na ca dhārā.vāhi.jñānānām aprāmāṇyam naiyāyikavaiśeṣikaiḥ svī.kriyate; anartha.janyatvena tu smṛter aprāmāṇye.atītānāgatādi.viṣayasyānumānasyāpy anartha.janyatvenāprāmāṇyam bhavet; tri.kālaviṣayam te cānumānam śabdavad iṣyate, dhūmena hi vartamāno 'gnir anumīyate meghonnatyā bhaviṣyantī vṛṣṭir nadī.pūreṇa ca sā.eva.bhūtā.iti; tad evam dhārā.vāhi.jñānair anumānena ca smṛteḥ sādṛśye saty api yat smṛter aprāmāṇyam dhārā.vāhi.jñānādīnām ca prāmāṇyam iṣyate sa pūrvāparavirodhaḥ /

ñ438: īśvarasya sarvārthaviṣayam pratyakṣam kim indriyārthasamnikarṣanirapekṣam iṣyata āhosvid indriyārthasamnikarṣotpannam /
ñ: yadi.indriyārthasamnikarṣanirapekṣam tadā.indriyārthasamnikarṣotpannam jñānam avyapadeśyam ity atra sūtre samnikarṣopādānam nirarthakam bhavet; īśvarapratyakṣasya samnikarṣam vināpi bhāvāt /
ñ: atha.īśvarapratyakṣam indriyārthasamnikarṣotpannam evābhipreyata iti ced ucyate --
ñ: na hi.īśvarasambandhi.manaso 'ṇuparimāṇatvād yugapat sarvārthair samyogo bhavet; tataś caikam artham sa yadā vetti tadā nāparān sato 'py arthān tato 'smad.ādivan na tasya kadāpi sarvajñatā; --
ñ: yugapat.samnikarṣāsambhavena sarvārthānām yugapad.avedanāt / atha sarvārthānām krameṇa samvedanāt sa sarvajña iti cen na; bahunā kālena sarvārthasamvedanasya khaṇḍaparaśāv ivāsmad.ādiṣv api sambhavāt te.api sarvajñāḥ prasajeyuḥ /
ñ: api cātītānāgatānām arthānām vinaṣṭānutpannatvād eva manasā samnikarṣo na bhavet; satām eva samyogasambhavāt teṣām ca tadānīm asattvāt; --
ñ: tataḥ katham mahā.īśvarasya jñānam atītānāgatārtha.grāhakam syāt; sarvārtha.grāhakam ca taj.jñānam iṣyate tataḥ pūrvāparo virodhaḥ subodhaḥ /

ñ439: evam yoginām api sarvārthasamvedanam durdharavirodharuddham avaboddhavyam /

ñ440: kārya.dravye prāg.utpanne sati tasya rūpam paścād utpadyate nirāśrayasya rūpasya guṇatvāt prāg.anutpādena.iti pūrvam uktvā paścāc ca kārya.dravye vinaṣṭe sati tad.rūpam vinaśyati.ity.ucyamānam pūrvāparaviruddham bhavet; yato 'tra rūpam kārye vinaṣṭe sati nirāśrayam sthitam sat paścād vinaśyed iti /

ñ441: sāmkhyasya tv evam svavacanavirodhaḥ / prakṛtir nityā.ekā niravayavā niṣkriyāvyaktā ca.iṣyate /
ñ: sā.evānityādibhir mahad.ādi.vikāraiḥ pariṇamata iti cābhidhīyate; tac ca pūrvāparato 'sambaddham /
ñ: arthādhyavasāyasya buddhi.vyāpāratvāc cetanā.viṣayaparicchedarahitārtham na budhyata ity etat sarva.lokapratīti.viruddham /
ñ: buddhir mahd.ākhyā jaḍā na kim.api cetayata ity api svaparapratīti.viruddham / ākāśādi.bhūtapañcakam svarādi.tanmātrebhyaḥ sūkṣmasamjñnebhya utpannam yad ucyate tad api nityaikāntavāde pūrvāparaviruddham katham śraddheyam /
ñ: yathā puruṣasya kūṭasthanityatvān na vikṛtir bhavati nāpi bandhamokṣau tathā prakṛter api na te sambhavanti kūṭasthanityatvād eva; kūṭasthanityam caikasvabhāvam iṣyate tato ye prakṛter vikṛtir bandhamokṣau cābhyupagamyante paraih; te nityatvam ca paraparaviroddhāni /

ñ422: mīmāmsakasya punar evam svamatavirodhaḥ / ''na himsyāt sarva.bhūtāni'' [  ] iti ''na vai himsro bhavet'' [  ] iti cābhidhāya; ''mahā.ukṣam vā mahājam vā śrotriyāya prakalpayet [Yājnṣmṛti AcAra 1.109ab] iti jalpato vedasya katham na pūrvāparavirodhaḥ /
ñ: tathā ''na himsyāt sarva.bhūtāni'' [  ] iti prathamam uktvā paścāt tad.āgeme paṭhitam evam --
ñ: ''ṣaṭ.śatāni niyujyante paśūnām madhyame.ahani /
ñ: aśvamedhasya vacanān nyūnāni paśubhis tribhiḥ //1//'' 
ñ: tathā ''agnīṣomīyam paśum ālabheta'' [AitAr 6.13] ''satpa.daśa prājāpatyān paśūn ālabheta'' [ṭṣ 1.4] ity ādi.vacanāni katham iva na pūrvāparavirodham anurudhyante /

ñ443: tathānṛta.bhāṣaṇam prathamam niṣidhya paścād ūce ''brāhmaṇārthe.anṛtam brūyāt'' [  ] ityādi; tathā --
ñ: ''na narma.yuktam vacanam hinasti na strīṣu rājan na vivāha.kāle /
ñ: prāṇātyaye sarva.dhanāpahāre pañcānṛtāny āhur apātakāni /'' [MBḥ 1.77.16; etc.]

ñ444: tathādattādānam anekadhā nirasya paścād uktam / yady api brāhmaṇo haṭhena parakīyam ādatte balena vā; tathāpi tasya nādattādānam; yataḥ sarvam idam brāhmaṇebhyo dattam brāhmaṇānām na daurbalyād vṛṣalāḥ paribhuñjate; tasmād apaharan brāhmaṇaḥ svam ādatte svam eva brāhmaṇo bhuGkte svam vaste svam dadāti.iti /

ñ445: tathā ''aputrasya gatir nāsti [  ] iti lapitvā.uktam --
ñ: ''anekāni sahasrāṇi kumāra.brahma.cāriṇām /
ñ: divam.gatāni viprāṇām akṛtvā kulasamtatim //1//'' ity ādi / tathā
ñ: ''na māmsa.bhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune /
ñ: pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānām nivṛttis tu mahā.phalā //1//'' [Manu 5.56] iti smṛti.gate śloke / yadi pravṛttir nirdoṣā, tadā katham tato nivṛttis tu mahā.phalā.iti vyāhatam etat /

ñ446: vedavihitā himsā dharma.hetur ity atra prakaṭa eva svavacanavirodhah; tathā hi -- dharma.hetuś cedd himsā katham / himsā ced dharma.hetuḥ katham / na hi bhavati mātā ca vandhyā ca.iti / dharmasya ca lakṣaṇam idam śrūyate /
ñ: ''śrūyatām dharmasarvasvam śrutvā caivāvadhāryatām /
ñ: ātmanaḥ pratikūlāni pareṣām na samācaret //11// [Cāṇakya 1.7] ityādy arcir.mārgaprapannair vedāntavādibhir gahitā ca.imam himsā /
ñ: ''andhe tamasi majjāmaḥ paśubhir ye yajāmahe /
ñ: himsā nāma bhaved dharmo na bhūyo na bhaviṣyati //1//'' iti /

ñ447: tathā bhavāntaram prāptānām tṛptaye ca śrāddhādi.vidhānam tad apy avicāritaramaṇīyam / tathā ca tad.yūthinaḥ paṭhanti --
ñ: ''mṛtānām api jantūnām śrāddham cet tṛpti.kāraṇam /
ñ: tan.nirvāṇapradīpasya snehaḥ samvardhayet śikhām //1//'' iti
ñ: evam anyāny api purāṇoktāni pūrvāparaviruddhāni samdehasamuccaya.śāstrād atrāvatārya vaktavyāni /

ñ448: tathā nityaparokṣa.jñānavādino bhaṭṭāḥ svātmani kriyā.virodhāj jñānam svāprakāśakam abhyupagacchantaḥ pradīpasya param prakāśakam (svaparaprakāśakam?) anaGgī.kurvantaś ca katham sad.bhūta.bhāṣiṇaḥ /

ñ449: tathā brahmādvaitavādino 'vidyā.vivekena san.mātram pratyakṣāt pratiyanto 'pi na niṣedhakam pratyakṣam iti bruvāṇāḥ katham na viruddhavādinah; avidyā.nirāsena san.mātrasya grahaṇāt /

ñ450: tathā pūrvottaramīmāmsā.vādinaḥ katham api devam anaGgī.kurvāṇā api sarve.api brahmaviṣṇu.mahā.īśvarādīn devān pūjayanto dhyāyanto vā dṛśyante / tad api pūrvāparaviruddham ity ādi /

ñ451: athavā ye ye bauddhādi.darśaneṣu syād vādābhyupagamāḥ procānaś lokavyākhyāyām pradarśitāḥ te sarve.api pūrvāparaviruddhatayātrasarva.darśaneṣu yathā.svam darśayitavyāh; yato bauddhādaya uktaprakāreṇa syād.vādam svī.kuvanto 'p tan.nirāsāya ca yuktīḥ sphorayantaḥ pūrvāparaviruddhavādinaḥ katham na bhaveyuḥ /
ñ: kiyanto vā dadhi.māṣa.bhojanāt.kṛṣṇā (Hindi kālimāko ūpara lāvem) vivicyanta ity uparamyate /

ñ452: cārvākas tu varāka ātma.tad.āśrita.dharmādharmānekāntasvargāpavargādikam sarvam kugraha.grahilatayā.evāpratipadyamāno 'vajñā.upahata eva kartavyah; na punas tam pratyanekāntābhyupagamopanyāsena pūrvāparoktavirodhaprakāśanena vā kim api prayojanam; --
ñ: sarvasya tad.uktasya sarva.loka.śāstraiḥ saha viruddhatvāt /
ñ: mūrtebhyo bhūtebhyo 'mūrta.caitanyasyotpādasya viruddhatvād bhūtebhya utpadyamānasyānyata āgacchato vā caitanyasyādarśanāt; ātmavac caitanyasyāpy aindriyakapratyakṣāviṣayatvād ityādi /

ñ453: tad evam baudhādīnām anyeṣām sarveṣām āgamāḥ pratyuta svapraṇetṛṛṇām asarvajñatvam eva sādhayanti na punaḥ sarvajñamūlatām; pūrvāparaviruddhārthavacanopetatvāt /
ñ: jainamatam tu sarvam pūrvāparavirodhābhāvāt svasya sarvajñamūlatām evāvedayati.iti sthitam /

ñ454: athānuktam api kim.api likhyate / prāpya.kārīṇy eva.indriyāṇi.iti kaṇa.bhakṣākṣapādamīmāmsakasamkhyāḥ samākhyānti /
ñ: cakṣuh.śrotra.itarāṇi tathā.iti tāthāgatāḥ / cakṣur.varjāni.iti syād.vādāvadāta.hṛdayāḥ /

ñ455: śvetāmbarāṇām sammatir naya.cakravālaḥ syād.vādaratnākaro ratnākarāvatārikā tattvārthapramāṇavārttikam pramāṇamīmāmsā nyāyāvatāro 'nekānta.jayapatākānekāntapradeśo dharmasamgrahaṇī prameyaratna.kośaś ca.ityy evam.ādayo 'neke tarka.granthāḥ / digambarāṇām tu prameya.kamalamārtaṇḍo nyāya.kumuda.candra āptaparīkṣāṣṭasahasrī siddhāntasāro nyāyaviniścaya.ṭīkā ca.ityādayaḥ //58// (jainamatasvarūpanirṇayo nāma caturtho 'dhikārah) [Eṇḍ]

ṣḍṣ Vaiśeṣika

atha pañcamo 'dhikārah.

V456: atha vaiśeṣikamatavivakṣayā prāha --
V: devatā.viṣayo bhedo nāsti naiyāyikaiḥ samam /
V: vaiśeṣikāṇām tattve tu vidyate.asau nidarśyate //

V457: vyākhyā -- asya liGgaveṣācāra.devādi naiyāyikaprastāve prasaGgena prāg eva procānam /
V: muni.viśeṣasya kāpotīm vṛttim anuṣṭhitavato rathyā.nipatitāms taṇḍula.kaṇān ādāya kṛtāhārasyāhāranimittāt kaṇāda iti samjñājani /
V: tasya kaṇādasya muneḥ puraḥ śivenolūkarūpeṇa matam etat prakāśitam /
V: tata aulūkyam procyate / paśupati.bhaktatvena pāśupatam cocyate / 
V: kaṇādasya śiṣyatvena vaiśeṣikāḥ kāṇādā bhaṇyante / ācāryasya ca 'prāg.abhidhānī parikara' iti nāma samāmnāyate / ('attendants who possess the previous name of their teacher'?) 

V458: atha prastutam prastūyate / deva eva devatā tad.viṣayo bhedo -- viśeṣo vaiśeṣikāṇām naiyāyikaiḥ sama nāsti; etena yādṛg.viśeṣaṇa īśvaro devo naiyāyikair abhipretah; tādṛg.viśeṣaṇaḥ sa eva vaiśeṣikāṇām api deva ity arthaḥ /
V: tattve tu tattvaviṣaye punar vidyate bhedaḥ / asau tattvaviṣayo bhedo nidarśyate pradarśyate //59// tam evāha --
V: dravyam guṇas tathā karma sāmānyam ca caturthakam /
V: viśeṣasamavāyau ca tattvaṣaṭkam tu tan.mate //60//

V459: vyākhyā -- dravyam prathamam tattvam guṇī dvitīyam / tathā.śabdo bhedāntarasūcane / karma tṛtīyam sāmānyam ca caturtham eva /
V: caturthakam svārthe kapratyayaḥ / viśeṣasamavāyau ca pañcama.ṣaṣṭhe tattve / 
V: ubhayatra ca.kārau samuccayārthau / tu.śabdasyāvadhāraṇārthatve tattva.ṣaṭlam eva na nyūnādhikam ṣaḍ eva padārthā ity arthaḥ /
V: tan.mate vaiśeṣikamate / atra padārtha.ṣaṭke dravyāṇi guṇāś ca; kecin nityā eva kecit tv anityāh; karmānityam eva; sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyās tu nityā eva.iti / kecit tv abhāvam saptamam padārtham āhuḥ //60//

V460: atha dravya.bhedān āha --

V: kāla.dig.ātmamanāmsi ca guṇaḥ punaḥ pañcavimśatidhā //61//

V461: vuāljuā -- tatra teṣu ṣaṭsu padārtheṣu dravyam navadhā; vyavacchedaphalam vākyam iti nyāyān navadhā.eva na tu nyūnādhikaprakāram /
V: atra dravyam iti jāty.apekṣam ekavacanam; evam prāg agre ca jñeyam; tato navaiva dravyāṇi.ity arthaḥ /
V: etena chāyā.tamaso ālokābhāvarūpatvān na dravye bhavata ity uktam /
V: bhūḥ pṛthivī; kāṭhinya.lakṣaṇā mṛt.pāṣāṇavanaspati.rūpā /
V: jalam āpaḥ tac ca sarit.samudra.karakādi.gatam /
V: tejo 'gnih; tac ca caturdhā; bhaumam kāṣṭha.indhanaprabhavam; divyam sūryavidyud.ādijam; āhārapariṇāma.hetur audaryam; ākarajam ca suvarṇādi / etāni catvāry anekavidhāni /

V462: antarikṣam ākāśam / tac caikam nityam amūrtam vibhu ca drabyam / vibhu.śabdena viśvavyāpakam /

V463: kālaḥ parāparavyatikara.yaugapadyāyaugapadya.cira.kṣiprapratyaya.liGgo dravyam /
V: tathā hi -- paraḥ pitāparaḥ putro yugapad ayugapad vā ciram kṣipram kṛtam kariṣyate vā.iti yat parāparādi.jñānam tad ādityādi.kriyā.dravyavyatiriktapadārthanibandhanam tat.pratyayavilakṣaṇatvāt; ghaṭādi.pratyayavat /

V464: dig api dravyam ekā nityāmūrtā vibhuś ca (vibhvī ca) / mūrteṣv eva hi dravyeṣu mūrtam dravyam avadhim kṛtvā.idam asmāt pūrveṇa dakṣiṇena paścimenottareṇa pūrva.dakṣiṇena dakṣiṇāpareṇāparottareṇottarapūrveṇādhastād.upariṣṭād ity amī daśapratyayā yaoto bhavanti sā dig iti /

V466: manaś cittam; tac ca nityam dravyam aṇu.mātram anekam āśu.samcāri prati.śarīram ekam ca /
V: yugapaj.jñānānutpattir manaso liGgam; ātmano hi sarva.gatatvād yugapad.aneka.indriyārthasamnidhāne saty api krameṇaiva jñānotpatty.upalambhād anumīyate /

V: tasya ca manaso mṛta.śarīrān nirgatasya mṛta.śarīrapratyāsannam adṛṣṭavaśād upajāta.kriyair aṇubhir dvy.aṇukādi.krameṇārabdham atisūkṣmam anupalabdhi.yogyam śarīram samkramyaiva svargādau gatasya svargādy.upabhogya.śarīreṇa sambandho bhavati /

V471: vyākhyā -- sparṣas tvag.indriya.grāhyaḥ pṛthivy.udaka.jvalanapavanavṛttiḥ 1/

V480: kartṛ.phala.dāyy ātma.guṇa ātmamanah.samyogajaḥ sva.kāryavirodhī dharmādharmarūpatayā bhedavān parokṣo 'dṛṣṭākhyo guṇaḥ /
