V481: adharmo 'py ātma.guṇaḥ kartṛ.rahitaḥ pratyavāya.hetur atīndriyo 'ntya.duhkhasamvijñānavirodhī 18/

V482: prayatna utsāhah; sa ca suptāvasthāyām prāṇāpānaprerakaḥ prabodha.kāle.antah.karaṇasya.indriyāntaraprāpti.hetur hitāhitaprāpti.parihārodyamaḥ śarīravidhārakaś ca 19/

V483: samskāro dvedhā; bhāvanā sthiti.sthāpakaś ca / (Pḍhṣ samskāras trividho; vego bhāvanā sthiti.sthāpakaś ca)
V: bhāvanākhya ātma.guṇo jñānajo jñāna.hetuś ca dṛṣṭānubhūta.śruteṣv artheṣu smṛti.pratyabhij;āna.kāryonnīyamānasadbhāvaḥ / 
V: sthiti.sthāpakas tu mūrtimad.dravya.guṇah; sa ca ghanāvayavasamniveśaviśiṣṭam svam āśrayam kālāntarasthāyinam anyathā.vyavasthitam api prayatnataḥ pūrvavad yathāvasthitam sthāpayati.iti sthiti.sthāpaka ucyate /
V: dṛśyate tālapattrādeḥ prabhūtatara.kālasamveṣṭitasya prasāryamuktasya punas tathaivāvasthānam samskāravaśāt /
V: evam dhanuh.śākhā.śṛṇga.dantādiṣu bhugnāpavartiteṣu ca vastrādiṣu tasya kāryam parisphuṭam upalabhyate 20/ (Pḍhṣ dantāsthi.sūtravastrādiṣu; bhugnasamvartiteṣu)

V484: prajvalanātmako dveṣaḥ yasmin sati prajvalitam ivātmānam manyate / drohaḥ krodho manyur akṣamāmarṣa iti dveṣa.bhedāḥ 21/

V485: sneho 'pām viśeṣa.guṇaḥ samgrahamṛdv.ādi.hetuḥ / asyāpi gurutvavan nityānityatvaniṣpattayaḥ 22/

V486: gurutvam jala.bhūmyoḥ patana.karma.kāraṇam apratyakṣam / tasyāb.ādi.paramāṇu.rūpādivan nityānityatvaniṣpattayaḥ 23/

V487: dravatvam syandana.karma.kāraṇam tri.dravyavṛttiḥ /
V: tad dvedhā -- sahajam naimittikam ca / sahajam apām dravatvam / naimittikam tu pṛthivī.tejasor agni.samyogajam; yathā sarpiṣaḥ suvarṇa.trapv.ādeś cāgni.samyogād dravatvam utpadyate 24/

V488: vegaḥ pṛthivy.ap.tejo.vāyu.manahsu mūrtimad.dravyeṣu prayatnābhighātaviśeṣāpekṣāt karmaṇaḥ samutpadyate; niyata.dik.kriyā.kāryaprabandha.hetuḥ sparśavad.dravyasamyogavirodhī ca /
V: tatra śarīrādi.prayatnāvirbhūta.karmotpannavegavaśād iṣor apāntarāle pātaḥ (pahuMca; ñ apāntarāle.apātahṇ ṣuali -rāle.apapatah); sa ca (no ca in ṣuali) niyata.dik.kriyā.kāryasambandhonnīyamānasadbhāvaḥ /
V: loṣṭādy.abhighātotpanna.karmotpādyas tu śākhā.ādau vegaḥ /

V489: kecit tu samskārasya trividhasya bhedatayā vegam prāhuḥ / tan.mate catur.vimśatir eva guṇāḥ / (e.g.Pḍhṣ)
V: śauryāudārya.kāruṇya.dākṣiṇyāunnatyādīnām ca guṇānām eṣv eva prayatna.buddhyādiṣu guṇeṣv antar.bhāvān nādhikyam /

V490: sparśādīnām guṇānām sarveṣām guṇatvābhisambandho dravyāśritatvam niṣkriyatvam aguṇatvam ca /
V: tathā sparśarasa.gandharūpaparatvāparatva.gurutva.dravatvashenavegā mūrta.guṇāḥ /
V: buddhi.sukha.duhkha.icchā.dharmādharmaprayatna.bhāvanā.dveṣa.śabdā amūrta.guṇāḥ /
V: samkhyā.parimāṇapṛthaktvasamyogavibhāgā ubhaya.guṇā ityādi guṇaviṣayam viśeṣasvarūpam svayam samavaseyam //63//

V491: atha karmavyācikhyāsur āha --
V: utkṣepāvakṣepāv ākuñcanakam prasāraṇam gamanam /
V: pañcavidham karmaitat parāpare dve tu sāmānye //64//

V492: vyākhyā -- utkṣepa ūrdhvam kṣepaṇam; muśśalāder ūrdhvam.nayanam utpkṣepaṇam karma.ity.arthaḥ /
V: tad.viparīto 'vakṣepo 'dho.nayanam ity arthaḥ / ṛjuno 'Ggulyādi.dravyasya kuṭilatva.kāraṇam karmākuñcanam / svārthe kapratyaya ākuñcanakam /
V: yena vakro 'vayavy ṛjuḥ sampadyate tat karma prasāraṇam / yad aniyata.dig.deśaiḥ samyogavibhāga.kāraṇam tad gamanam /
V: aiyata.grahaṇena bhramaṇapatanasyandanarecanādīnām api gamana evāntar.bhāvo vibhāvanīyaḥ / pañcavidham eva karma kriyā.rūpam etad anantaroktam /

V493: atha sāmānyam ucyate / tu.śabdasya vyastasambandhāt sāmānye tu dve parāpare -- param aparam ca dvividham sāmānyam ity arthaḥ //64//

V494: atha parāpare vyākhyāti --
V: tatra param sattā.ākhyam dravyatvādy aparam atha viśeṣas tu /
V: niścayato nitya.dravyavṛttir anityo nirdiṣṭaḥ //65//

V495: vyākhyā -- tatra tayoḥ parāparayor madhye param sāmānyam sattā.ākhyam /
V: idam sad idam sad ity anugatākāra.jñāna.kāraṇam sattā.sāmānyam ity arthaḥ /
V: tac ca triṣu dravya.guṇa.karmasu padārthṣu sat sad ity anuvṛtti.pratyayasyaiva kāraṇatvāt sāmānyam evocyate; na tu viśeṣaḥ /
V: athāparam ucyate 'dravyatvādi' dravyatvam guṇatvam karmatvam cāparam sāmānyam; tatra navasu dravyeṣu dravyam dravyam iti buddhi.hetur dravyam /
V: evam guṇeṣu guṇatva.buddhi.vidhāyi guṇatvamṇ karmasu ca karmatva.buddhi.kāraṇam karmatvam /
V: tac ca dravyatvādikam svāśrayeṣu dravyādiṣv anuvṛtti.pratyaya.hetutvāt sāmānyam apy ucyate; svāśrayasya ca vijātīyebhyo guṇādibhyo vyāvṛtti.pratyaya.hetutayā viśeṣo 'py ucyate /
V: tato 'param sāmānyam ubhayarūpatvāt sāmānyaviśeṣasamjñām labhate /
V: apekṣā.bhedād ekasyāpi sāmānyaviśeṣa.bhāvo na virudhyate /
V: evam pṛthivītvasparśatvotkṣepaṇatva.gotva.ghaṭatvādīnām apy anuvṛtti.vyāpṛtti.hetutvāt sāmānyaviśeṣa.bhāvaḥ siddha iti /
V: atra sattā.yogāt sattvam yad iṣyate tad.dravya.guṇa.karmasv eva na punar ākāśādiṣu; ākāśa.kāla.dikṣu hi vastu.svarūpam evāstitvam svīkriyate vyakty.aikyādi.kāraṇaiḥ / tathā codayanaḥ --
V: ''vyakter abhedas tulyatvam saGkaro 'thānavasthitiḥ /
V: rūpa.hānir asambandho jāti.bādhakasamgrahaḥ //1//'' [praśa.kiraṇā.pṛ.33]
V496: asya vyākhyā -- vyakter abheda ekam anekavarti sāmānyam / ākāśe vyakter abhedān na jātitvam /
V: pṛthivītve jātau yadi bhūmitvam ucyate; tadā tulyatvam / paramāṇuṣu jātitve.aGgī.kṛte pārthivāpya.taijasavāyavīyatvayogāt saGkaraḥ /
V: sāmānye yadi sāmānyam aGgī.kriyate; tadā mūla.kṣati.(ṣuali kṣiti).kāraṇī anavasthitiḥ /
V: viśeṣeṣu yadi sāmānyam svī.kriyate; tadā viśeṣasya rūpa.hāniḥ / yadi samavāye jātitvam aGgī.kriyate; tadā sambandhābhāvaḥ /
V: kena hi sambandhena tatra sattā sambadhyate / samavāyāntarābhāvād iti /

V497: pare punaḥ prāhuḥ -- sāmānyam trividham; mahā.sāmānyam sattā.sāmānyam sāmānyaviśeṣasāmānyam ca /
V: tatra mahā.sāmānyam ṣaṭsv api padārtheṣu padārthatva.buddhi.kāri / sattā.sāmānyam tri.padārthasad.buddhi.vidhāyi / (dravya; guṇa; karma)
V: sāmānyaviśeṣasāmānyam tu dravyatvādi / anye tv ācakṣate tri.padārthasatkārī sattā; sāmānyam dravyatvādi; sāmānyaviśeṣaḥ pṛthivītvādir iti /
V: lakṣaṇa.bhedād eteṣām sattā.ādīnām dravya.guṇa.karmabhyaḥ padārthāntaratvam siddham /

V498: atha ity ānantarye / viśeṣas tu niścayatas tattvavṛttita eva nirdiṣṭah; na punar ghaṭapaṭa.kaṭādir iva vyavahārato viśeṣaḥ /
V: tu.śabdo 'nantaroktasāmānyād asyātyantavyāvṛtti.buddhi.hetutvena bhṛśam vailakṣaṇyam sūcayati /
V: yata eva niścayato viśeṣah; tata eva 'nitya.dravyavṛttir antyah' iti / 
V: tatranitya.dravyeṣu vināśārambharahiteṣv ākāśa.kāla.dig.ātmamanahsu vṛttir vartanam yasya sa nitya.dravyavṛttiḥ /
V: tathā paramāṇūnām jagad.vināśārambha.koṭi.bhūtatvān muktātmanām muktamanasām ca samsāraparyantarūpatvād antatvam; anteṣu bhavo 'ntyo viśeṣo vinirdiṣṭaḥ proktah; anteṣu sthitasya sphuṭataram ālakṣyamāṇatvāt /
V: vṛttis tu tasya sarvasminn eva paramāṇv.ādau nitye dravye vidyata eva /
V: ata eva nitya.dravyavṛttir antya ity ubhayapadopādānam / 
V: viśeṣaś ca dravyam dravyam pratyekaika eva vartate nānekah; ekenaiva viśeṣeṇa svāśrayasya vyāvṛtti.siddher anekaviśeṣa.kalpanā.vaiyarthyāt /
V: sarvanitya.dravyāṇy āśritya punar viśeṣāṇām bahutve.api jātāv atraikavacanam / tathā ca praśastakaraḥ --

V499: ''anteṣu bhavā antyāh; svāśrayasya viśeṣakatvād viśeṣāh; vināśārambharahiteṣu nitya.dravyeṣv aṇv.ākāśa.kāla.dig.ātmamanahsu pratidravyam ekaśo vartamānā atyantavyāvṛtti.buddhi.hetavaḥ /
V: yathāsmad.ādīnām gavādiṣv aśvādibhyas tulyākṛti.guṇa.kriyāvayavasamyoganimittā pratyayavyāvṛttir dṛṣṭā; yathā gauḥ śuklaḥ śīghra.gatiḥ pīna.kakudmān mahā.ghaṇṭa iti /
V: tathāsmad.viśiṣṭānām yoginām nityeṣu tulyākṛti.guṇa.kriyeṣu paramāṇuṣu muktātmamanahsu cānyanimittāsambhavādyebhyo nimittebhyaḥ pratyādhāram vilakṣaṇo 'yam vilakṣaṇo 'yam iti pratyayavyāvṛttih; deśa.kālaviprakṛṣṭe ca paramāṇau sa evāyam iti pratyabhijñānam ca bhavati; te.antyā viśeṣāḥ /'' [praśa.bhā.pṛ.167] iti /

V500: anye tu 'nitya.dravyavṛttayo 'ntyā viśeṣāh'' iti sūtram evam vyācakṣate /
V: nitya.dravyeṣv eva vṛttir eva yeṣām iti sāvadhāraṇam vākyam etat /
V: nitya.dravyavṛttaya iti padam antyapadasya vivaraṇam etat; tathā coktam --
V: ''nitya.dravyāṇy utpatti.vināśayor ante vyavasthitatvād anta.śabdavācyāni teṣu bhavās tad.vṛttayo viśeṣā antyāḥ (cf.vaiśe.upa.1.2.6) ity ākhyāyanta iti /
V: amī cātyantavyāvṛtti.hetavo dravyādibhyo vailakṣaṇyāt padārthāntaram //65//

V501: atha samavāyam svarūpato nirūpayati --
V: ya ihāyutasiddhānām ādhārādheya.bhūta.bhāvānām /
V: sambandha ihapratyaya.hetuḥ sa hi bhavati samavāyaḥ //66//

V502: vyākhyā -- kecid dhātupārāyaṇa.kṛto 'yu amiśraṇe' iti paṭhanti; tata evāyutasiddhānām ityādi vaiśeṣikīyasūtre.ayutasiddhānām apṛthak.siddhānām iti vyākhyātam / 
V: tathā loke.api bhedābhidhāyī yuta.śabdaḥ prayujyamāno dṛśyate; dvāv api bhrātarāv etau yutau jātāv ityādi /
V: tato 'yam atrārthaḥ / 'iha' vaiśeṣika.darśane 'ayutasiddhānām' apṛthak.siddhānām; tantuṣu samavetapaṭavat pṛthag.āśrayānāśritānām iti yāvad ādhārāś cādheyāś ca ādhārādheyā te bhavanti sma /
V: 'ādhārādheya.bhūtāh' te ca te bhāvāś cārthāḥ teṣām yaḥ 'sambandha ihapratyaya.hetuh' iha tantuṣu paṭa ityādeḥ pratyayasyāsādhāraṇam kāraṇam 'sa hi' sa eva 'bhavati samavāyah' sambandhaḥ /
V: yato hi.iha tantuṣu paṭah; iha paṭa.dravye guṇa.karmaṇī; iha dravya.guṇa.karmasu sattā; iha dravye dravyatvam; iha guṇe guṇatvam; iha karmaṇi karmatvam; iha dravyeṣv antyā viśeṣā ityādi viśeṣapratyaya utpadyate; sa pañcabhya padārthebhyo 'rthāntaram samavāya ity arthaḥ /
V: sa caiko vibhur nityaś ca vijñeyaḥ //66/

V503: tad evam ṣaṭ.padārthasvarūpam prarūpitam / samprati pramāṇasya sāmānyato lakṣaṇam ākhyāyate /
V: athopalabdhi.hetuḥ pramāṇam iti/ asyāyam arthaḥ -- avyabhicārādi.viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭārthopalabdhi.janikā sāmagrī tad.ekadeśo vā bodharūpo 'bodharūpo vā jñānapradīpādiḥ sādhakatamatvāt pramāṇam /
V: etat.kārya.bhūtā vā yathā.uktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭārthopalabdhiḥ pramāṇasya sāmānya.lakṣaṇam; tayā sva.kāraṇasya pramāṇābhyāsebhyo vyavacchidyamānatvāt /
V: indriyajatva.liGgajatvādi.viśeṣaṇaviśeṣitā sā.evopalabdhiḥ pramāṇasya viśeṣa.lakṣaṇam iti /

V504: atha pramāṇasamkhyām prāha --
V: pramāṇam ca dvidhāmīṣām pratyakṣam laiGgikam tathā /
V: vaiśeṣikamatasyaiṣa samkṣepaḥ parikīrtitaḥ //67//

V505: vyākhyā -- amīṣām vaiśeṣikāṇām pramāṇam dvidhā dvividham / caḥ punar.arthe /
V: katham ity āha 'prathakṣam' / tathā.iti samuccaye /
V: liGgāj jātam laiGgikam ca tatra pratyakṣam dvedhā; aindriyo yogajam ca /
V: aindriyam ghrāṇarasana.cakṣus.tvak.śrotramanah.samnikarṣajam asmad.ādīnām pratyakṣam /
V: tad dvedhā; nirvikalpakam savikalpakam ca /
V: tatra vastu.svarūpālocanamātram nirvikalpakam / tac ca na sāmānyamātram gṛhṇāti bhedasyāpi pratibhāsanāt; nāpi sva.lakṣaṇamātram sāmānyākārasyāpi samvedanāt; vyakty.antara.darśane pratisamdhānāc ca; kim.tu sāmānyam viśeṣam cobhayam api gṛhṇāti; param idam sāmānyam ayam viśeṣa ity eva vivicya na pratyeti; sāmānyaviśeṣasambandhinor anuvṛtti.vyāvṛtti.dharmayor agrahaṇāt /
V: savikalpakam tu sāmānyaviśeṣarūpatām vivicya pratyety; vastv.antaraiḥ samam anuvṛtti.vyāvṛtti.dharmau (?) pratipadyamānasya ātmana indriya.dvāreṇa tathā.bhūtapratīty.upapatteḥ /

V506: yogajam api pratyakṣam dvedhā; yuktānām pratyakṣam viyuktānām ca /
V: tatra yuktānām samādhim aikāgryam āśritānām yogaja.dharma.balād antahkaraṇe śarīrād bahir nirgatyātīndriyārthaiḥ samam samyukte sai yad atīndriyārtha.darśanam tad yuktānām pratyakṣam /
V: ye cātyanta.yogābhyāsocita.dharmātiśayād asamādhim prāptā apy aīndriyam artham paśyanti; te viyuktāḥ /
V: teṣām ātmamana.indriyārthasamnikarṣād deśa.kālasvabhāvaviprakṛṣṭārtha.grāhakam yat pratyakṣam tad viyuktānām pratyakṣam; etac cotkṛṣṭa.yogino 'vaseyam; yogi.mātrasya tad.asambhāvād iti / vistaras tu nyāya.kandalīto vijñeyaḥ /

V507: laiGgikasya punaḥ svarūpam idam / liGga.darśanād yad avyabhicāritvādi.viśeṣaṇam jñānam tad yataḥ (?) parāmarśa.jñānopalakṣitāt kārakasamūhād bhavati tal laiGgikam anumānam iti yāvat /
V: tac caivam bhavati / ''asya.idam kāryam kāraṇam samyogi samavāyi virodhi ca.iti laiGgikam /'' [vaiśe.sū.9.18] 
V: tatra kāryam kāraṇapūrvakatvenopalambhād upalabhyamānam kāraṇasya gamakam; yathāyam nadī.pūro vṛṣṭi.kāryo viśiṣṭanadī.pūratvāt pūrvopalabdhaviśiṣṭanadī.pūravat /
V: kāraṇam api kārya.janakatvena pūrvam upalabdher upalabhyamānam kāryasya liGgam yathā viśiṣṭameghonnatir varṣa.karmaṇaḥ /
V: tathā dhūmo 'gneḥ samyogī / samavāyī coṣṭasparśo vāristham tejo gamayati.iti /
V: virodhī ca yathāhir visphūrjanaviśiṣṭo nakulāder liGgam; vahnir vā śītābhāvasya.iti /
V: 'asya.idam' iti sūtre ca kāryādīnām upādānam liGganidarśanārtham kṛtam; na punar etāvanty eva liGgāni.ity.avadhāraṇārtham /
V: yataḥ kāryādi.vyatiriktāany api liGgāni santi; yathā candrādayaḥ samudravṛddheḥ kumudavikāśasya ca liGgam; na candrādayaḥ samudravṛddhi.kumudavikāśau ca mithaḥ kāryam kāraṇam vā bhavanti; viśiṣṭa.dig.deśa.kālasamyogāt kallolapattravistāra.lakṣaṇānām udakavṛddhi.vikāśānām svasva.kāraṇebhya evotpatteḥ /
V: śaradi ca jalasya nairmalyam agastyodayasya liGgam ityādi tat sarvam 'asya.idam' iti padena gṛhītam vijñeyam /
V: asya sādhyasya.idam sambandhi.iti kṛtvā yad yasya deśa.kālādy.avinābhūtam tat tasya liGgam ity.arthaḥ /
V: tataḥ 'asya.idam' iti sūtrasya nāvyāpatā.iti /
V: viśeṣārthinā tu nyāya.kandalī vilokanīyā /
V: śabdādīnām tu pramāṇānām anumāna evāntar.bhāvāt kandalī.kārābhiprāyeṇaitat pramāṇa.dvitayam atrāvocad ācāryaḥ /
V: vyomaśivas tu pratyakṣānumāna.śabdāni trīṇi pramāṇāni procivān /
V: upasamharann āha -- 'vaiśeṣikamatasya'' ityādi /vaiśeṣikamatasyaiṣo 'nantarauktaḥ samkṣepaḥ parikīrtitaḥ kathitaḥ /

V508: athātrāpy anuktam kimcid ucyate / vyomādikam nityam / pradīpādi kiyat.kālāvasthāyi /
V: buddhi.sukhādikam ca kṣaṇikam / caitanyādayo rūpādayaś ca dharmāḥ ātmāder ghaṭādeś ca dharmiṇo 'tyantam vyatiriktā api samavāyasambandhena sambaddhāh; sa ca samavāyo nityaḥ sarva.gata ekaś ca /
V: sarva.gata ātmā / buddhi.sukha.duhkha.icchā.dharmādharmaprayatna.bhāvanā.ākhyasamskāra.dveṣāṇām navānām ātmaviśeṣa.guṇānām ucchedo mokṣaḥ /
V: parasparavibhaktau sāmānyaviśeṣau dravyaparyāyau ca pramāṇa.gocaraḥ /
V: dravya.guṇādiṣu ṣaṭsu padārtheṣu svarūpasattvam vastutvanibandhanam vidyate / 
V: dravya.guṇa.karmasu ca sattā.sambandho vartate sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyeṣu ca sa nāsti.iti //67//

V509: ṣaṭ.padārthī kaṇāda.kṛtā tad.bhāṣyam praśasta.kara.kṛtam taṭ.ṭīkā kandalī śrīdharācārīyāṇ kiraṇāvalī tu.udayanasamdṛbdhā; vyomavatir vyomaśivācāryaviracirā; līlāvatī.tarkaḥ śrīvatsācārīyah; ātreya.tantram ca.ityādayo vaiśeṣika.tarkāḥ / (vaiśeṣikamatanirṇayo nāma pañcamo 'dhikārah) [Eṇḍ]

ṣḍṣ Mīmāmsā

atha ṣaṣṭho 'dhikārah

M510, atha mīmāmsakamatam jaiminīyāparāhvayam procyate /
M, jaiminīyā veṣeṇa sāmkhyā ivaika.daṇḍās tri.daṇḍā dhātu.raktavāsaso mṛga.carmopaveśanāḥ kamaṇḍalu.dharā muṇḍa.śirasaḥ samnyāsi.prabhṛtayo dvijāḥ /
M, teṣām veda eva gurur na punar anyo vaktā guruḥ / 
M, ta eva svayam tava samnyastam samnyastam iti bhāṣante / yajñopavītam ca prakṣālya trir jalam pibanti /

M511, te dvidhā, eke yājñikādayaḥ purvamīmāmsā.vādinah, apare tu.uttaramīmāmsā.vādinaḥ /
M, tatra pūrvamīmāmsā.vādinaḥ ku.karmavivarjinah, yajanādi.ṣaṭ.karma.kāriṇah, brahmasūtriṇo gṛhasthāśramasamsthitāḥ śūdrānnādi.varjakā bhavanti / te ca dvedhā bhāṭṭāḥ prābhākarāś ca ṣaṭ pañca prāmāṇaprarūpiṇaḥ /

M, ye tu.uttaramīmāmsā.vādinah, te vedāntino brahmādvaitam eva manyante /
M, ''sarvam etad idam brahma'' [ChUp 3.14.1] iti bhāṣante pramāṇam ca yathā tathā vadanti / eka evātmā sarva.śarīreṣu.upalabhyata iti jalpanti /
M, ''eka eva hi bhūtātmā bhūte bhūte vyavasthitaḥ /
M, ekadhā bahudhā caiva dṛśyate jala.candravat //1//'' [tri.tā.5.12] iti vacanāt /
M, ''puruṣa eva.idam sarvam yat bhūtam yac ca bhāvyam'' [ṛV.10.10.2] iti vacanāc ca /
M, ātmany eva layam muktim ācakṣate, na tv aparām kām api muktim manyante /
M, te ca dvijā eva bhagavan.nāma.dheyāś caturdhābhidhīyante kuṭīcara.bahūdaka.hamsaparama.hamsa.bhedāt /
M, tatra tridaṇḍī saśikho brahmasūtrī gṛha.tyāgī yajamānaparigrahī sakṛt putra.gṛhe.aśnan kuṭyām nivasan kuṭī.cara ucyate /
M, kuṭī.cara.tulyaveṣo vipra.gehanairāśya.bhikṣāśano viṣṇu.jāpaparo nadī.tīrasnāyī bahu.udakaḥ kathyate /
M, brahmasūtra.śikhābhyām rahitaḥ kaṣāyāmbara.daṇḍa.dhārī grāme caikarātram nagare ca tri.rātram nivasan vidhūmeṣu vigatāgniṣu vipra.geheṣu bhikṣām bhuñjānas tapah.śoṣitavigraho deśeṣu bhraman hamsaḥ samucyate /
M, hamsa evotpanna.jñānaś cāturvarṣya.geha.bhojī sva.iccchayā daṇḍa.dhāra īśānīm diśam gacchan śakti.hīnatāyām anaśana.grāhī vedāntaikādhyāyī parama.hamsaḥ samākhyāyate /
M, eteṣu caturṣu paraḥ paro 'dhikaḥ / ete ca catvāro 'pi kevala.brahmādvaitavādasādhanaikavyasaninaḥ śabdārthayor nirāsāya (?) anekā yuktīḥ sphorayanto 'nirvācya.tattve yathā vyavatiṣṭhante tathā khaṇḍana.tarkād abhiyuktair avaseyam / (khaṇḍana.khaṇḍana.khādya)
M, nātra tan.matam vakṣyate, iha tu sāmānyena śāstra.kāraḥ pūrvamīmāmsā.vādinam eva vibhaṇiṣur evam āha --

M513, jaiminīyāḥ punaḥ prāhuḥ sarvajñādi.viśeṣaṇaḥ /
M, devo na vidyate ko 'pi yasya mānam vaco bhavet //68//

M514, vyākhyā -- jaiminīyās tu bruvate / sarvajñādīni viśeṣaṇāni yasya sa sarvajñādi.viśeṣaṇaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarva.darśī vītarāgaḥ sṛṣṭyādi.kartā ca.ityādi.veśeṣaṇavān ko 'pi prāg.ukta.darśanasammata.devānām ekataro 'pi devo daivatam na vidyate, yasya devasya vaco vacanam mānam pramāṇa bhavet /
M, prathamam tāvad deva eva vaktā na vartate, kutas tat.praṇītāni vacanāni sambhaveyur iti bhāvaḥ /
M, tathā.hi -- puruṣo na sarvajñaḥ mānuṣatvād rathyā.puruṣavat /

M515, atha kimkarāyamāṇasurāsurasevyamānatā trailokyasāmrājyasūcaka.chattra.cāmarādi.vibhūty.anyathānupapattir asti sarvajñe viśeṣa iti cet, māyāvibhir api kīrti.pūjā.lipsubhir indra.jālavaśena tat.prakaṭanāt / (kimkarāyamāṇaṇ denom?)
M, yad uktam tvad.yūthyenaiva samanta.bhadreṇa --
M, ''devāgamanabho.yāna.cāmarādi.vibhūtayaḥ /
M, māyāviṣv api dṛśyante nātas tvam asi no mahān //1//'' [āptamī.ślo.1]
M, atha yathānāder api suvarṇamalasya kṣāramṛt.puṭapākādi.prakriyayā viśodhyamānasya nirmalatvam, evam ātmano 'pi nirantara.jñānādy.abhyāsena vigatamalatvāt sarvajñatvam kim sambhaved iti matis tad api na, abhyāsena hi śuddhes tāratamyam eva bhavet, na punaḥ paramaḥ prakarṣaḥ /
M, na hi narasya laGghanam abhyāsatas tāratamyavad apy upalabhyamānam sakala.lokaviṣayam upalabhyate / uktam ca --
M, ''daśa.hastāntaram vyomno yo nāmotplutya gacchati /
M, na yojana.śatam gantum śakto 'bhyāsa.śatair api'' //1// (Guoted earlier)

M516, atha mā bhūn mānuṣasya sarvajñatvam, brahmaviṣṇu.mahā.īśvarādīnām tu tad astu /
M, te hi devāh, sambhavaty api teṣv atiśayasampat / yat kumārilaḥ --
M, ''athāpi divya.dehatvād brahmaviṣṇu.mahā.īśvarāḥ /
M, kāmam bhavantu sarvajñāḥ sārvajñyam mānuṣasya kim //1//''
M, iti tad api na rāga.dveṣamūlanigrahānugraha.grastānām kāmāsevanavihastānām asambhāvyam idam eṣām iti /

M517, na ca pratyakṣam tat sādhanam ''sambaddham vartamānam ca gṛhyate cakṣur.ādinā'' [mī.pratyakṣasū.ślo.84] iti vacanāt /
M, na cānumānam, pratyakṣa.dṛṣṭa evārthe tat.pravṛtteḥ / na cāgamah, sarvajñasyāsiddhatvena tad.āgamasyāpi vivādāspadatvāt /
M, na copamānam, tad.aparasyāpi sarvajñasyābhāvād eva /
M, na cārthāpattir api, sarvajñasādhakasyānyathānupapannapadārthasyādarśanāt /
M, tataḥ pramāṇapañcakāpravṛtter abhāvapramāṇa.gocara eva sarvajñaḥ /
M, prayogaś cātra -- nāsti sarvajñah, pratyakṣādi.gocarātikrāntatvāt śaśa.śṛGgavad iti //68//

M518, yadi devas tad.vacanāni ca na santi, tarhi kuto 'tīndriyārtha.jñānam ity āśaGkyāha --
M, tasmād atīndriyārthānām sākṣād.draṣṭur abhāvataḥ /
M, nityebhyo vedavākyebhyo yathārthatvaniviścayaḥ //69//

M519, vyākhyā -- tasmāt tataḥ kāraṇāt / kuto hetutaḥ / ity āha -- atīndriyārthānām indriyaviṣayātītapadārthānām ātma.dharmādharma.kālasvarganarakaparamāṇuprabhṛtīnām sākṣāt spaṣṭapratyakṣāvabodhena draṣṭuḥ jñātur abhāvato 'sadbhāvādd hetor nityebhyo 'pracyutānutpannasthiraikasvabhāvebhyo 'vadhāraṇasya.iṣṭaviṣayatvād vedavākyebhya eva yathārthatvaviniścayaḥ /
M, arthānām anatikrameṇa yathārtham tasya bhāvo yathārthatvam yathāvasthitapadārthatvam tasya veśeṣeṇa niścayo bhavati /
M, nityatvenāpauruṣeyebhyo vedavacanebhya eva yathāvad atīndriyādy.artha.jñānam bhavati, na punaḥ sarvajñapraṇītāgamādibhyaḥ sarvajñādīnām evābhāvād iti bhāvaḥ / yathā.āhus te --
M, ''atīndriyāṇām arthānām sākṣād.draṣṭā na vidyate /
M, vacanena hi nityena yaḥ paśyati sa paśyati //1//''

M520, nanv apauruṣeyānām vedānām katham arthaparijñānam iti cet / avyavacchinnānādi.sampradāyena.iti //

M521, athaitad eva dṛṣṭhayann āha --
M, ata eva purā kāryo vedapāṭhaḥ prayatnataḥ /
M, tato dharmasya jijñāsā kartavyā dharmasādhanī //70//

M522, vyākhyā -- ata eva sarvajñādy.abhāvād eva purā pūrvam vedapāṭhaḥ ṛg.yajuh.sāmātharvaṇānām pāṭhaḥ prayatnataḥ kāryaḥ /
M, tataḥ kim kartavyam ity āha -- 'tato dharmasya' iti / tato vedapāṭhād anantaram dharmasya jijñāsā kartavyā /
M, dharmo hy atīndriyah, tataḥ sa kīdṛkkena pramāṇena vā jñāsyata ity evam jñatum icchā kāryā / sā kīdṛśī dharmasādhanī -- dharmasādhanasyopāyaḥ //70//

M523, yataś caivam tatas tasya nimittam parīkṣya nimittam ca nodanā / nimittam hi dvividham janakam grāhakam ca / atra tu grāhakam jñeyam / etad eva viśeṣitataram prāha --
M, nodanā.lakṣaṇo dharmo nodanā tu kriyām prati /
M, pravartakam vacaḥ prāhuḥ svah.kāmo 'gnim yathā yajet //71//

M524, vyākhyā -- nodyante preryante śreyah.sādhaka.dravyeādiṣu pravartyante jīvā anayā.iti nodanā -- vedavacana.kṛtā preraṇā.ity.arthaḥ /
M, dharmo nodanayā lakṣyate jñāyata iti nodanā.lakṣaṇaḥ / dharmo hy atīndriyatvena nodanayā.eva lakṣyate nānyena pramāṇena, pratyakṣādīnām vidyamānopalambhakatvāt, dharmasya tu kartavyatā.rūpatvāt, kartavyatāyāś ca tri.kāla.śūnyārtharūpatvāt, tri.kāla.śūnya.kāryarūpārthaviṣayavijñānotpādikā codanā.iti mīmāmsakābhyupagamāt /
M, atha nodanām vyākhyāti 'nodanā tu kriyām prati'' ityādi / nodanā punaḥ kriyām havanasarva.bhūtāhimsanā.dānādi.kriyām prati pravartakam vaco vedavacanam prāhur mīmāmsakā bhāṣante /
M, havanādi.kriyā.viṣaye yad eva prerakam vedasya vacanam sā.eva nodanā.iti bhāvaḥ /
M, pravartakam tad.vacanam eva nidarśanena darśayati 'svah.kāmo 'gnim yathā yajet& iti / yathā.ity upadarśanārthaḥ /
M, svaḥ svargo kāmo yasya sa svah.kāmaḥ pumān svah.kāmaḥ san /
M, agnim vahnim jayet tarpayet / atra.idam śloka.bandhānulomyena.ittham upanyastam /
M, anyathā tv evam bhavati / ''agni.hotram juhuyāt svarga.kāmah'' [maitryup.6.3] iti pravartakavacanasyopalakṣaṇatvāt /
M, nivartakam api vedavacanam nodanā jñeyāṇ yathā ''na himsyāt sarva.bhūtāni'' [common in MBh] iti / 
M, evam na vai himsro bhaved ityādy api / ābhir nodanābhir nodito yadi yathā nodanam yair dravya.guṇa.karmabhir yo havanādau pravartate nivartate vā, tadā teṣām dravyādīnām tasyābhīṣṭasvargādi.phalasādhana.yogyatā.eva dharmam ity abhidhīyate /
M, etena vedavacanaiḥ prerito 'pi yadi na pravarte na nivartate vā viparītam vā pravartate, tadā tasya narakādy.aniṣṭaphalasamsādhana.yogyatā.eva dravyādi.sambandhinī pāpam ity ucyata ity.api jñāpitam draṣṭavyam /
M, iṣṭāniṣṭārthasādhana.yogyatā.lakṣaṇau dharmādharmāv iti hi mīmāmsakāḥ /
M, uktam ca śābare -- ''ya eva śreyas.karaḥ sa eva dharma.śabdenocyate'' [Zābarabhā.1.1.2] anena dravyādīnām iṣṭārthasādhana.yogyatā dharma iti pratipāditam śabarasvāminā / bhaṭṭo 'py etad evāha --
M, ''śreyo hi puruṣaprītiḥ sā dravya.guṇa.karmabhiḥ /
M, nodanā.lakṣaṇaiḥ sādhyā tasmād eṣv eva dharmatā //1//'' [mī.ślo.codanā sū.ślo.191]
M, ''eṣām aindriyakatve.api na tādrūpyeṇa dharmatā /
M, śreyah.sādhanatā hy eṣām nityam vedāt pratīyate //2//
M, tādrūpyeṇa ca dharmatvam tasmān na.indriya.gocaraḥ /'' [mī.ślo.codanā sū.ślo.13-14] iti //71//

M, atha viśeṣa.lakṣaṇam pramāṇasyābhidhānīyam, tac ca sāmānya.lakṣaṇāvinā.bhūtam, tataḥ prathamam pramāṇasya sāmānya.lakṣaṇam abhidhīyate /
M, 'anadhigatārtha.gantṛ pramāṇam' iti / anadhigataḥ agṛhīto yo 'rtho bāhyaḥ stambhādis tasyādhigantṛ ādhikyena samśayādi.vyudāsena paricchedakam /
M, anadhigatārthādhigantṛ prāg.jñātārthapariccchedakamñ samarthaviśeṣaṇopādānāj jñānam viśeṣyam labhyate, agṛhītārtha.grāhakam jñānam pramāṇam ity arthaḥ /
M, atra 'anadhigata' iti padam dhārā.vāhi.jñānānām gṛhīta.grāhiṇām prāmāṇyaparākaraṇārtham / 
M, 'artha' iti grahaṇam samvedanam svasamviditam bhavatiṇ svātmani kriyā.virodhāt, kim.tu, nityam parokṣam eva.iti jñāpanārtham /
M, tac ca parokṣam jñānam bhāṭṭamate.arthaprākatyaphalānumeyam prabhākaramate samvedanākhyaphalānumeyam vā pratipattavyam /

M526, atha pramāṇasya viśeṣa.lakṣaṇam vivakṣuḥ prathamam tan.nāmāni tat.samkhyām cāha --
M, pratyakṣam anumānam ca śābdam copamayā saha /
M, arthāpattir abhāvaś ca ṣaṭ pramāṇāni jaimineḥ //72//

M527, vyākhyā -- pratyakṣānumāna.śābdopamānārthāpatty.abhāva.lakṣaṇāni ṣaṭ pramāṇāni jaimini.muneḥ sammatāni ity adhyāhāraḥ /
M, c.kārāḥ samuccayārthāḥ / tatrādyāni pañcaiva pramāṇāni.iti.prabhākaro 'bhāvasya pratyakṣeṇaiva grāhyatām manyamāno 'bhimanyate / ṣaḍ.api tāni.iti bhaṭṭo bhāṣate //72//

M528, atha pratyakṣasya lakṣaṇam ācaṣṭe --
M, tatra pratyakṣam akṣāṇām samprayoge satām sati /
M, ātmano buddhi.janma.ity anumānam laiGgikam punaḥ //73//

M529, vyākhyā -- 'tatra' iti nirdhāraṇārthaḥ / iyam atrākṣara.ghaṭanā -- satām samprayoge saty ātmano 'kṣāṇām buddhi.janma pratyakṣam iti (janmapratyakṣam in botḥ teṣts) /
M, śloke tu bandhānulomyena vyastanirdeśaḥ / satām vidyamānānām vastūnām sambandhini samprayoge sambandhe sati ātmano jīvasya.indriyāṇām yo buddhy.utpādas tat pratyakṣam iti /
M, satām ity atra sata ity ekavacanenaiva prastutārthasiddhau ṣaṣṭhī.bahu.vacanābhidhānam bahūnām apy arthānām sambandha indriyasya samyogaḥ kvacana bhavati.iti jñāpanārtham /
M, atra jaiminīyam sūtram idam -- ''sat.samprayoge sati puruṣasya.indriyāṇām buddhi.janma tat pratyakṣam /'' [Mṣ.1.1.4] iti /
M, vyākhyā -- satā vidyamānena vastunā.indriyāṇām samprayoge sambandhe sati punar asya yo jñānotpādas tat pratyakṣam /

M530, ayam atra bhāvaḥ -- yad.viṣayam vijñānam tenaivārthena samprayoga indriyāṇām pratyakṣam, pratyakṣābhāsam tv anyasamprayogajam, yathā maru.marīcikā.ādi.samprayogajam jalādi.jñānam iti /
M, athavā sat.samprayogajatvam vidyamānopalambhanatvam ucyate / tatra sati -- vidyamāne samyak.prayogo 'rtheṣv indriyāṇām vyāpāro yogyatā vā, na tu naiyāyikābhyupagata eva samyogādiḥ /
M, tasmin sati śeṣam prāgvat / iti.śabdaḥ pratyakṣa.lakṣaṇasamāpti.sūcakaḥ //

M531, athānumānam lakṣayati punah.śabdasya vyastasambandhāt / anumānam punar laiGgikam liGgāl liGgi.jñānam anumānam ity arthaḥ /
M, tatra.idam anumāna.lakṣaṇasya sūcāmātram uktam / sampūrṇam tv ittham tal.lakṣaṇam '' jñātasambandhasyaika.deśa.darśanād asamnikṛṣṭe.arthe buddhir anumānam'' [śābarabhā.1.1.5] iti śābaram anumāna.lakṣaṇam /
M, vyākhyā -- avagatasādhyasādhanāvinābhāvasambandhasya pumsa eka.deśasya sādhanasya darśanād asamnikṛṣṭe parokṣe.arthe buddhir jñānam anumānam iti //73//

M532, atha śābdam āha --
M, śābdam śāśvatavedottham upamānam tu kīrtitam /
M, prasiddhārthasya sādharmyād aprasiddhasya sādhanam //74//

M533, vyākhyā -- śāśvato 'pauruṣeyatvān nityo yo vedaḥ tasmad utthā utthānam yasya tat śāśvatavedottham / arthād veda.śabda.janitam jñānam śābdam pramāṇam /
M, asyal.idam lakṣaṇam -- ''śabda.jñānād asamnikṛṣṭe.arthe buddhiḥ śābdam'' [śābarabhā.1.1.5] iti /
M, ayam śabdo 'syārthasya vācaka iti yaj jñānam tat śabda.jñānam / tasmād anantaram śabde śrute jñānād asamnikṛṣṭe.artha apratyakṣe.apy arthe ghaṭādau buddhi.jñānam śābdam pramāṇam /
M, śabdād apratyakṣe vastuni yaj jñānam udeti tat śābdam ity.arthaḥ /
M, atra mate śabdasya.idam svarūpam prarūpyate / nityā ākāśavat sarva.gatāś ca varṇāḥ /
M, te ca tālv.oṣṭhādibhir abhivyajyante na punar utpādyante / viśiṣṭānupūrvīkā varṇāḥ śabdaḥ / nityaḥ śabdārthayor vācyavācakasambandha iti /

M534, athopamānam āha 'upamānam tu' ityādi / upamānam punaḥ kīrtitam / tat kim.rūpam ity āha 'prasiddhārthasya' ityādi /
M, prasiddha upalabdho 'rtho gavādir yasya pumsaḥ sa prasiddhārthaḥ jñāta.gavādi.padārtha ity arthaḥ /
M, tasya gavaya.darśane sādharmyād gavaya.gatasādṛśyāt parokṣe gavy aprasiddhasya purānupalabdhasya sādṛśyasādhanam jñānam /
M, asya.idam sūtram ''upamānam api sādṛśyād asamnikṛṣṭe.arthe buddhim utpādayati, yathā gavaya.darśanam go.smaraṇasya'' [śābarabhā.1.1.8] iti /
M, gavayasādṛśyād asamnikṛṣṭe.arthe parokṣasya goḥ sādṛśye go.smaraṇasya.iti /
M, gavi smaraṇam yasya pumsaḥ sa go.smaraṇaḥ tasya go.smaraṇavata ity.arthaḥ /
M, śeṣam spaṣṭam / tatra.idam tātparyam -- yena pratipattrā gaur upalabdho na gavayo na cātideśavākyam 'gaur iva gavaya' iti śrutam, tasyāraṇye paryaṭato gavaya.darśane prathama upajāyate parokṣe gavi sādṛśya.jñānam yad utpadyate 'anena sadṛśo gauh' iti, tad upamānam iti /
M, tasya viṣayaḥ sādṛśyaviśiṣṭaḥ parokṣo gauh, tad.viśiṣṭam vā sādṛśyam iti /
M, asya cānadhigatārthādhigantṛtayā prāmāṇyam upapannam, yato 'tra gavayaviṣayeṇa pratyakṣeṇa gavaya eva viṣayī.kṛto na punar asamnihitasya goḥ sādṛśyam /
M, yad api tasya pūrvam gaur iti pratyakṣam abhūt, tathāpi tasya gavayo 'tyantam apratyakṣa eva.iti katham gavi tad.apekṣam tat.sādṛśya.jñānam /
M, tad evam gavayasādṛśo gaur iti prāg.apratipatter anadhigatārthādhigantṛ.parokṣe gavi gavaya.darśanāt sādṛśya.jñānam //74//

M535, dṛṣṭārthānupapattyā tu kasyāpy arthasya kalpanā /
M, kriyate yad.balenāsau arthāpattir udāhṛtā //75//

M536, vyākhyā -- pratyakṣādibhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ pramāṇair dṛṣṭaḥ prasiddho yārthah, tasyānupapattyānyathāsambhavena tu punaḥ kasyāpy anyasyādṛṣṭasyārthasya kalpanā yad.balena yasya jñānasya balena sāmarthyena kriyate /
M, 'dṛṣṭādy.anupapattyā' iti pāṭhe tu dṛṣṭaḥ pramāṇapañcakenādi.śabdāt śrutaḥ śābdapramāṇena tasya dṛṣṭasya śrutasya cārthasyānupapattyā kasyāpy arthasya kalpanā yad.balena kriyata iti prāgvat / 
M, asāv adṛṣṭārtha.kalpanā.rūpam jñānam evārthāpattir udāhṛtā /
M, atra.idam sūtram -- ''arthāpattir api dṛṣṭaḥ śruto vārtho 'nyathā nopapadyata ity adṛṣṭārtha.kalpanā'' [śābarabhā.1.1.5] iti /
M, atra pramāṇapañcakena dṛṣṭaḥ śabdena śrutaś.ca arthau mitho.vailakṣaṇya.jñāpanārthapṛthak.kṛtyoktau staḥ /
M, śeṣam tulyam / idam uktam bhavati -- prtyakṣādi.pramāṇa.ṣaṭkavijñāto 'rtho yena vinā nopapadyate tasyārthasya kalpanam arthāpatiḥ /

M537, tatra pratyakṣapūrvikārthāpattir yathāgneḥ pratyakṣeṇoṣṇasparśam upalabhya dāhaka.śakti.yogo 'rthāpattyā prakalpyate /
M, na hi śaktir adhyakṣaparicchedyā nāpy anumānādi.samadhigamyā, apratyakṣayā śaktyā saha kasyacid arthasya sambandhāsiddheḥ /
M, anumānapūrvikārthāpatir yathā.ādityasya deśāntaraprāptyā devadattasya.iva gaty.anumāne tato 'numānād.gamana.śakti.yogo 'rthāpattyāvasīyate /
M, upamānapūrvikārthāpattiḥ yathā 'gavayavad gauh' ity.ukter arthād vāha.dohādi.śakti.yogas tasya pratīyate, anyathā gotvasyaivāyogāt /
M, śabdapūrvikārthāpattiḥ śrutārthāpattir iti.itaranāmikā yathā śabdār arthapratītau śabdasyārthena sambandhasiddhiḥ /
M, arthāpatti.pūrvikārthāpattiḥ yathā.uktaprakāreṇa śabdasyārthena sambandhasiddhāv arthān nityatvasiddhiḥ pauruṣeyatve śabdasya sambandhāyogāt /
M, abhāvapūrvikārthāpattir yathā jīvato devadattasya gṛhe.adarśanād arthād bahir.bhāvaḥ /
M, atra ca catasṛbhir arthāpattibhiḥ śaktiḥ sādhyate, pañcamyā nityatā, ṣaṣṭyā gṛhād.bahir.bhūto devadatta eva sādhyata ity evam ṣaṭ.prakārārthāpattiḥ /
M, anye tu śrutārthāpattim anyathā.udāharanti, pīno devadatto divā na bhuGkta iti vākya.śravaṇād rātri.bhojanavākyapratītih, śrutārthāpattiḥ /
M, gavayopamitasya gos taj.jñāna.grāhyatā.śaktir upamānapūrvikārthāpattir iti / 
M, iyam ca ṣaṭ.prakārāpy arthāpattir nādhyakṣam, atīndriya.śaktyādy.arthaviṣayatvāt / ata eva nānumānam api, pratyakṣapūrvakatvāt tasya, tataḥ pramāṇāntaram evārthāpattiḥ siddhā //75//

M538, athābhāvapramāṇam svarūpataḥ prarūpayati --
M, pramāṇapañcakam yatra vastu.rūpe na jāyate /
M, vastu.sattāvabodhārtham tatrābhāvapramāṇatā //76//

M539, vyākhyā -- sad.asad.amśātmake vastuni pratyakṣādīni pañca pramāṇāni sad.amśam gṛhyate na punar asad.amśam /
M, pramāṇābhāva.lakṣaṇas tv abhāvo 'sad.amśam gṛhṇīte na punaḥ sad.amśam /
M, ''abhāvo 'pi pramāṇābhāva.lakṣaṇo nāsti.ity.arthasyāsamnikṛṣṭasya prasiddy.artham pramāmam'' [śā.bhā.1.1] iti vacanāt /

M540, anye punar abhāvākhyam pramāṇam tridhā varṇayanti /
M, pramāṇapañcakābhāva.lakṣaṇo 'nantaro 'bhāvaḥ pratiṣidhyamānād vā tad.anya.jñānam (ñain tad.anyaj.jñnānam), ātmā vā viṣaya.grahaṇarūpeṇānabhinirvṛttasvabhāva iti /
M, tataḥ prastuta.ślokasyāyam arthaḥ -- pramāṇapañcakam pratyakṣādi.pramāṇapañcakam yatra bhu.talādāv ādhāre ghaṭāder ādheyasya grahaṇāya na jāyate na pravartate, tatra ādheyavarjitasyādhārasya grahaṇe.abhāvapramāṇatābhāvasya prāmāṇyam /
M, etena niṣidhyamānāt tad.anya.jñānam (ñain tad.anyaj.jñānam) uktam /
M, tathā 'pramāṇapañcakam yatra' iti padasyātrāpi sambandhād yatra vastu.rūpe ghatāder vastuno rūpe.asad.amśe grāhakatayā na jāyateṇ tatrāsad.amśe.abhāvasya pramāṇatā /
M, etena pramāṇapañcakābhāva uktaḥ / tathā pramāṇapañcakam 'vastu.sattāvabodhārtham' ghaṭādi.vastu.sattāyā avabodhāya na jāyate -- asad.amśe na vyāpriyate tatra sattānavabodhe.abhāvasya pramāṇatā /
M, anenātmā viṣaya.grahaṇarūpeṇāpariṇata uktaḥ / evam ihābhāvapramāṇa tridhā pradarśitam / tad uktam --
M, ''pratyakṣāder anutpattiḥ pramāṇābhāva ucyate /
M, sātmano.(sā.ātmano?).apariṇāmo vā (cf. pariṇati na honā) vijñānam vā anyavastuni //1// [mī.ślokābhāva.ślo.11]

M541, atra sā.śabdo 'nutpatter viśeṣaṇatayā yojya iti sammati.ṭikāyām abhāvapramāṇam yathā tridhā.upadarśitam tathā.ihāpi tad darśitam /

M542, ratnākarāvatāritakāyām tu pratyakṣāder anutpattir ity asyaiva uktasya balena dvidhā tad varṇitam āste /
M, tatra sa.śabdaḥ pullingaḥ pramāṇābhāvasya viśeṣaṇam kārya iti /tattvam tu bahu.śrutā jānate /

M543, atha ye.abhāvapramāṇam ekadhābhiddhati tan.metena prastuta.śloko vyākhyāyate /
M, pramāṇapañcakam pratyakṣādi.pramāṇapañcakam yatra yasmin vastu.rūpe ghaṭādi.vastu.rūpe na jāyate na vyāpiparti /
M, vastu.rūpam dvedhā, sad.asad.rūpa.bhedāt / ato dvayo rūpayor ekataravyaktaye prāha 'vastu.sattā' ityādi /
M, vastuno ghaṭādeḥ sattā sad.rūpatā sad.amśa iti yāvat, tasyā avabodhārtham sad.amśo hi pratyakṣādi.pañcakasya viṣayah, sa cet tena na gṛhyate, tadā tatra vasru.rūpe śeṣasyāsad.amśasya grahaṇābhāvasya pramāṇatā.iti /

M544, 'vastv.asattāvabodhanārtham' iti kvacit pāṭhāntaram / tatrāyam arthaḥ -- pramāṇapañcakam yatra vastuno rūpe na vyāpriyate, tatra vastuno yāsattāsad.amśah, tad.avabodhanārtham abhāvasya pramāṇatā.iti /
M, anena ca trividhenaikavidhena vābhāvapramāṇena pradeśādau ghaṭābhāvo gamyate /
M, na ca pratyakṣeṇaivābhāvo 'vasīyate, tasyābhāvaviṣayatvavirodhāt, bhāvāmśenaiva.indriyāṇām samyogāt /

M545, atha ghaṭānupalabdhyā pradeśe dharmiṇi ghaṭābhāvaḥ sādhyata ity anumāna.grāhyo 'bhāva iti cen na, sādhyasādhanayoḥ kasyacit sambandhasyābhāvāt / tasmād abhāvo 'pi pramāṇāntaram eva /

M546, abhāvaś ca prāg.abhāvādi.bheda.bhinno vastu.rūpo 'bhyupagantavyah, anyathā kāraṇādi.vyavahārasya lokapratītasyābhāvaprasaGgāt / tad uktam --
M, ''na ca syād vyavahāro 'yam kāraṇādi.vibhāgataḥ /
M, prāg.abhāvādi.bhedena nābhāvo yadi bhidyate //1//
M, yad vānuvṛtti.vyāvṛtti.buddhi.grāhyo yatas tv ayam /
M, tasmād gavādivad vastuprameyatvāc ca gṛhyatām //2//
M, na cāvastuna ete syur bhedās tenāsya vastutā /
M, kāryādīnām abhāvaḥ ko bhāvo yaḥ kāraṇādinā //3//
M, vastu.samkarasiddhiś ca tat.prāmāṇyam samāśritā / (vastv.asamkara)
M, kṣīre dadhyādi yan nāsti prāg.abhāvaḥ sa ucyate //4//
M, nāstitā payaso dadhni pradhvamsābhāva.lakṣaṇam /
M, gavi yo 'śvādy.abhāvas tu so 'nyonyābhāva ucyate //5//
M, śiraso 'vayavā nimnā vṛddhi.kāṭhinyavarjitāḥ /
M, śaś.śṛGgādi.rūpeṇa so 'tyantābhāva ucyate //6//'' [mī.ślo 'bhāva.ślo.2-9?]
M, yadi caitad.vyavasthāpakam abhāvākhyam pramāṇam na bhavet tadā pratiniyatavastu.vyapasthā dūrotsāritā.eva syāt /
M, ''kṣīre dadhi bhaved evam (ṣuali eva) dadhni kṣīram ghate paṭaḥ /
M, śaśe śṛGgam pṛthivyādau caitanyam mūrtir ātmani //7//
M, apsu gandho rasaś cāgnau vāyau rūpeṇa tau saha /
M, vyomni samsparśitā te ca na ced asya pramāṇatā //8// [mī.ślo 'bhāva.ślo.5-6?]

M547, atha niramśasad.ekarūpatvād vastuno adhyakṣeṇa sarvātmanā grahaṇe ko 'paro sad.amśo yatrābhāvaḥ pramāṇam bhaved iti cen na, svapararūpābhyām sad.asad.ātmakatvād vastunah, anyathā vastutvāyogāt /
M, na ca sad.amśāsad.amśasyābhinnatvāt tad.grahaṇe tasyāpi graha iti vācyam, sad.asad.amśayor dharmy.abhede.api bhedābhyupagamāt /
M, tad evam (ṣuali eva) pratyakṣādy.agṛhītaprameyābhāva.grāhakatvāt pramāṇābhāvaḥ pramāṇanantaram iti / 

M548, athoktam api kimcid vyaktaye likhyate -- anadhigatārthādhigantṛ pramāṇam /
M, pūrvam pūrvam pramāṇam uttaram tu phalam sāmānyaviśeṣātmakam vastu pramāṇa.gocaraḥ /
M, nityaparokṣam jñānam hi bhāṭṭaprabhākaramatayor arthaprākaṭyākhyasamvedanākhyaphalānumeyam /
M, vedo 'pauruṣeyaḥ / vedoktā himsā dharmāya / śabdo nityaḥ / sarvajño nāsty avidyā.paranāmamāyā.vaśāt pratibhāsamānaḥ sarvaḥ prapañco 'pāramārthikaḥ / para.brahmaiva paramārthasat //76// (short reference to Vedānta)

M549, upasamharann āha --
M, jaiminīyamātasyāpi samkṣepo 'yam niveditaḥ /
M, evam āstikavādānām kṛtam samkṣepa.kīrtitam //77//

M550, vyākhyā -- api.śabdān na kevalam apara.darśanānām samkṣepo nivedito jaiminīyamatasyāpy ayam samkṣepo niveditaḥ /
M, vaktavyasya bāhulyād alpīyasy asmin sūtre samastasya vaktum aśakyatvāt samkṣepa eva proktaḥ /
M, atha prāg.uktamatānām sūtrakṛn nigamanam āha 'evam' ityādi /
M, evam ittham āstikavādānām jīvapara.lokapuṇyapāpādy.astitvavādinām baudhanaiyāyikasāmkhya.jainavaiśeṣika.jaiminīyānām samkṣepeṇa kīrtanam vaktavyābhidhānam samkṣepa.kīrtanam kṛtam ///77//

M551, atraiva viśeṣam āha --
M, naiyāyikamatād anye bhedam vaiśeṣikaiḥ saha /
M, na manyante mate teṣām pañcaivāstikavādinaḥ //78//

M552, vyākhyā -- anye kecanācāryā naiyāyikamatād vaiśeṣikaiḥ saha bhedam pārthakyam na manyante /
M, eka.deva.tattvena tattvānām mitho 'ntar.bhāvane.alpīyasa eva bhedasya bhāvāc ca naiyāyikavaiśeṣikāṇām mitho matāikyam eva.icchanti.ity.arthaḥ / teṣām ācāryāṇām mata āstīkavādinaḥ pa,caiva na punaḥ ṣaṭ //78//

M553, atha darśanānām samkhyā ṣaṭ iti yā jagat.prasiddhā sā katham upapādanīyā.ityāśaGkyāha --
M, ṣaḍ.darśanasamkhyā tu pūryate tan.mate kila /
M, lokāyatamata.kṣepe kathyate tena tan.matam //79//

M554, vyākhyā -- ye naiyāyikavaiśeṣikayor matam ekam ācakṣate tan.mate ṣaḍ.darśanasamkhyā tu ṣaṇṇām darśanānām samkhyā punar lokāyatā nāstikās teṣām yan matam tasya kṣepe mīlana eva /
M, kila.ity āptavāde / pūryate pūrṇī.bhavet / tena kāraṇena tan.matam cārvākamatam kathyate svarūpataḥ prarūpyate / atrādyapāde saptākṣaram chando 'ntaram iti na chandah.śāstravirodhaḥ śaGkanīyaḥ //79//[Eṇḍ]

ṣḍṣ Lokāyata

atha lokāyatamatam

L555: prathamam nāstikasvarūpam ucyate / kāpālikā bhasmoddhūlanaparā yogino brāhmaṇādy.antyajāntāś ca kecana nāstikā bhavanti /
L: te ca jīvapuṇyapāpādikam na manyante / catur.bhūtātmakam jagad ācakṣate /
L: kecit tu cārvākaika.deśīyā ākāśam pañcamam bhūtam abhimanyamānāḥ pañca.bhūtātmakam jagad iti nigadanti /
L: tan.mate bhūtebhyo mada.śaktivac caitanyam utpadyate / jala.budbudavaj jīvāḥ /
L: caitanyaviśiṣṭaḥ kāyaḥ puruṣa iti / te ca madyamāmse bhuñjate mātr.āady.agamyā.gamanam api kurvate /
L: varṣe varṣe kasminn api divase sarve sambhūya yathā.nāmanirgamam (?) sthrībhir abhiramante /
L: dharmam kāmād aparam na manvate / tan.nāmāni cārvākā lokāyatā ityādīni/ 
L: 'gala carva adane' carvanti bhakṣayanti tattvato na manyante puṇpapāpādikam parokṣam vastu.jātam iti cārvākāḥ /
L: ''mayāka.śyāmāka'' [  ] ityādi.siddha.haimoṇādi.daṇḍakena śabdanipātanam / )hemavyākaraṇake auṇādika sūtrase 'cārvāka' śabda nipāta samjñaka siddha hotā hai)
L: lokāḥ nirvicārāḥ sāmānya.lokās tadvad ācaranti sma.iti lokāyatā lokāyatikā ity api / bṛhaspati.praṇītamatatvena bārhaspatyāś ca.iti /

L556: atha tan.matam evāha --
L: lokāyatā vadanty evam nāsti jīvo na nirvṛtiḥ /
L: dharmādharmau na vidyete na phalam puṇyapāpayoḥ //80//

L557: vyākhyā -- lokāyatā nāstikā evam ittham vadanti / katham ity āha / jīvaś cetanā.lakṣaṇaḥ para.loka.yāyī nāsti; pañcamahā.bhhutasamudbhūtasya caitanyasya.ihaiva bhūtanāśe nāśāt para.lokānusaraṇāsambhavāt /
L: jīvasthāne deva iti pāṭhe tu devaḥ sarvajñādir nāsti / tathā na nirvṛtir mokṣo nāsti.ity.arthaḥ /
L: anyac ca dharmaś cādharmaś ca dharmādharmau na vidyete puṇyapāpe sarvathā na sta ity.arthaḥ /
L: na caiva puṇyapāpayoḥ phalam svarganarakādi.rūpam asti; dharmādharmayor abhāve kutastyam tat.phalam iti bhāvaḥ //80//

L558: solluṇṭham yathā te sva.śāstre procire tathā.eva darśayann āha -- tathā ca tan.matam /
L: etāvān eva loko 'yam yāvān indriya.gocaraḥ /
L: bhadre vṛkapadam paśya yadvad antya.bahu.śrutāḥ //81//

L559: 'tathā ca' ity upadarśane / tan.matam prakramān nāstikamatam / tat kīdṛg ity āha; ayam pratyakṣo loko maṇuṣya.lokaḥ / etāvān eva etāvan.mātra eva / yāvān yāvan.mātraḥ / indriya.gocara indriyāṇi sparśanarasana.ghrāṇa.cakṣuh.śrotrāṇi pañca teṣām gocaro viṣayah; pañca.indriyaviṣayī.kṛtam eva vastu vidyate nāparakm kim.api /
L: loka.grahaṇāl lokasthāḥ padārthasārthā grāhyāḥ / tato yat pare (param?) jīvam puṇyapāpe tat.phalam svarganarakādikam ca prāhuh; tan nāsti; apratyakṣatvāt /
L: apratyakṣam apy asti.iti cet / śaśa.śṛGgavandhyā.stanam.dhayādīnām api bhāvo 'stu /
L: na hi pañcavidhena pratyakṣeṇa mṛdu.kaṭhorādi.vastūni tikta.kaṭu.kaṣāyādi.dravyāṇi surabhi.durabhi.bhāvān bhū.bhū.dhara.bhuvana.bhū.ruhastambhāmbhoruhādinarapaśu.śvāpadādi.sthāvara.jaGgamapadārthasārthān vividhaveṇu.vīṇā.ādi.dhvanīmś ca vimucya jātucid apy anyad anubhūyate /
L: yāvatā ca bhūtodbhūta.caitanyavyatiriktaś caitanya.hetutayā parikalpyamānaḥ para.loka.yāyī.jīvaḥ pratyakṣeṇa nānubhūyate; tavatā jīvasya sukha.duhkhanibandhanau dharmādharmau tat.prakṛṣṭaphala.bhoga.bhūmī svarganarakau puṇyapāpa.kṣayotthamokṣasukham copavarṇyamānāny ākāśe vicitra.citraviracanam iva kasya nāma na hāsyāni (ñain hāsyāvahāni) /
L: tato ye.atrāspṛṣṭam anāsvāditam anāghrātam adṛṣṭam aśrutam api jīvādikam ādiyamāṇāḥ svargāpavargādi.sukha.lipsayā vipralabdha.buddhayaḥ śiras.tuṇḍamuṇḍana.duścaratara.tapaś.caraṇācaraṇasukhsaha.tapanātapasahanādi.kleśair yat sauvam janma kṣayanti; tat teṣām mahāmohodrekavilasitam / tad uktam --
L: ''tapāmsi yātanāś citrāḥ samyamo bhogavañcanā /
L: agni.hotrādikam karma bāla.krīḍā.iva lakṣyate //1//
L: yāvaj jīvet sukham jīvet tāvad vaiṣayikam sukham /
L: bhasmī.bhūtasya dehasya punar.āgamanam kutaḥ //2//''
L: ityādi tataḥ susthitam indriya.gocara eva tāttvika iti //

L560: atha ye parokṣe viṣaye.anumānādīnām prāmāṇyena jīvapuṇyapāpādikam vyavasthāpayanti na jātucid viramanti tān prabodhayitum dṛṣṭāntam prāha 'bhadre vṛkapadam paśya' iti /
L: atrāyam sampradāyaḥ -- kaścit puruṣo nāstikamatavāsanā.vāsitāntah.karaṇo nijām jāyām āstikamatanibaddhamatim sva.śāstrokta.yuktibhir abhiyuktaḥ pratyaham pratibodhayati / 
L: sā tu yadā na pratibudhyeta tadā sa iyam anenopāyena pratibotsyata iti sva.cetasi vicintya niśāyāḥ paścime yāme tayā samam nagarān nirgatya tām pratyavādīt /
L: 'priye; ya ime nagaravāsino narāḥ parokṣaviṣaye.anumānādi.prāmāṇyam ācakṣāṇā lokena ca bahu.śrutatayā vyavahriyamāṇā vidyante; paśya teṣām cāru.vicāraṇāyām cāturyam' iti /
L: tataḥ sa nagara.dvārād ārabhya catuṣpatham yāvan mantharataraprasṛmarasamīraṇasamī.bhūtapāmśuprakare rājamārge dvayor api sva.karayor aGguli.trayam mīlayitvā sva.śarīrasyobhayoḥ pakṣayoḥ pāmśuṣu nyāsena vṛkapadāni pracakre /
L: tataḥ prātas tāni padāni nirīkṣyāstoko loko rājamārge.amilat / bahu.śrutā api tatrāgatā janān pratyavocan 'bho bho vṛkapadānām anyathānupapattyā nūnam niśi vṛkaḥ kaścana vanato 'trāgacchad' ityādi /
L: tataḥ sa tāms tathā.bhāṣamāṇān nirīkṣya nijām bhāryām jajalpa /
L: he bhadre priye vṛkapadam 'atra jātāv ekavacanam' paśya nirīkṣasva kim tad ity āha / 
L: yad vṛkapadam vadanti jalpanty abahu.śrutā lokarūḍhyā bahu.śrutā apy ete paramārtham ajñatvā bhāṣamāṇā abahu.śrutā eva.ity.arthaḥ /
L: 'yad vadanti bahu.śrutāh' iti pāṭhe tv evam vyākheyam -- lokaprasiddhā bahu.śrutā iti; tathā hy ete vṛkapadaviṣaye samyag.aviditaparamārthā bahavo 'py ekasadṛśam eva bhāṣamāṇā api bahu.mugdha.jana.dhyā.āndhyam utpādayanto 'pi ca jñāta.tattvānām ādeyavacanā na bhavanti /
L: tathā bahavo 'py amī vādino dhārmika.chadma.dhūrtāḥ paravañcanaikapravaṇā yat.kimcid anumānāgamādibhir dārḍhyam ādarśya jīvādy.astitvam sadṛśam eva bhāṣamāṇā api mudhā.eva mugdha.janān svargādi.prāpti.labhyasukhasamtati.pralobhanayā bhakṣyābhakṣya.gamyāgamya.heyopādeyādi.samkaṭe pātayanto bahu.mugdha.dhārmikavyāmoham utpādayanto 'pi ca satām avadhīraṇīyavacanā eva bhavanti.iti / tataḥ sā patyur vacanam sarvam mānitavatī //81//

L561: tad anu ca tasyāḥ sa patir yad upadiṣṭavān tad eva darśayann āha -- 
L: piba khāda ca cāru.locane yad atītam vara.gātri tann na te /
L: na hi bhīru gatam nivartate samudayamātram idam kalevaram ///82//
L: vyākhyā -- he cāru.locane.śobhanākṣi piba peyāpeyavyavasthā.lopena madirādeḥ pānam kuru /
L: na kevalam piba khāda ca bhakṣyābhakṣyanirapekṣatayā māmsādikam bhakṣaya ca / piba.khādādi.kriyayor upalak.saṇatvād gamyāgamyavibhāga.tyāgena bhogānām upabhogena sva.yauvanam sphalī.kurv ity api vaco 'tra.jñātavyam /
L: yad yauvanādy.atītam atikrāntam he pradhānāGgi tad bhūyas te tava na bhaviṣyati.ity.adhyāhāryam /
L: cāru.locane vara.gātri.iti sambodhana.dvayasya samānārthasyāpy ādarānurāgātiekān na paunaruktya.doṣaḥ / yad uktam --
L: ''anuvādādaravīpsā.bhṛśārtah.viniyoga.hetv.asūyāsu /
L: īṣat.sambhramavismaya.gaṇanā.smaraṇeṣv apunaru.uktam //11/'' [  ]

L563: atha sva.icchā.viracite pāne khādane bhogasevane ca suprāpā para.loke kaṣṭaparamparāṇ sulabham ca sati sukṛtasamcaye bhavāntare bhogasukha.yauvanādikam iti parāśaGkām parākartum prāha /
L: na hi -- naiva he bhīru / paroktamātreṇa narakādi.prāpya.duhkha.bhayākule! gatam -- iha bhavād atikrāntam sukha.yauvanādi nivartate para.loke punar apy upaḍhautake /
L: para.lokasukha.lipsayā tapaś.caraṇādi.kaṣṭa.kriyābhir ihatyasukhopekṣaṇam vyartham ity arthaḥ /

L564: atha śubhāśubha.karmapāratantryeṇa jīvenāmum kāyam adhunādhiṣṭhāya sthitenāvaśyam para.loke.api sva.karma.hetukam sukha.duhkhādi.veditavyam eva ity āśaGkya prāha /
L: samudayamātram samudayo bhūta.catuṣtayasamyogas tan.mātram / mātra.śabdo 'vadhāraṇe / idam pratyakṣam kalevaram śarīram evāsti.ity.adhyāhārah; na punar bhūta.catuṣṭayasamyogamātrād aparo bhavāntara.yāyī śubhāśubha.karmavipāka.bhoktā kāye kaścana jīvo vidyate / 
L: bhūta.catuṣkasamyogāś ca vidyud uddyota iva kṣaṇato dṛṣṭo naṣṭaḥ / tasmāt para.lokānapekṣayā yathā.icchamm piba khāda ca.ity.arthaḥ //82//
 
L535: atha prameyam pramāṇam cāha -- kim ca;
L: pṛthivī jalam tathā tejo vāyur bhūta.catuṣṭayam /
L: ādhāro bhūmir eṣām mānam tv akṣajam eva hi //83//

L566: vyākhyā 'kim ca' ity abhyuccaye / pṛthivī bhūmih; jalam āpah; tejo vahnih; vāyuḥ pavanah; bhūta.catuṣṭayam /
L: etāni bhūtāni catvāry ādhāro bhūmir eteṣām bhūtānām ādhāro 'dhikaraṇam bhūmiḥ pṛthvī /
L: 'caitanya.bhūmir eteṣām' iti pāṭhe tu catuṣṭayam kim.viśiṣṭam caitanya.bhūmiḥ caitanyotpatti.sthānam; bhūtāni sambhūyaikam caitanyam janayanti.ity.arthaḥ /
L: 'eteṣām cārvākāṇām mate 'pramāṇa.bhūmir eteṣām' iti pāṭhāntare tu bhūta.catuṣṭayam pramāṇa.bhūmiḥ pramāṇa.gocaras tāttvika eteṣām mate /
L: mānam tu pramāṇam punar akṣajam eva pratyakṣam evaikam na punar anumānādikam pramāṇam / hi.śabdo 'traviśeṣaṇārtho (viśeṣārtho?) vartate /
L: viśeṣaḥ punaś cārvākair loka.yātrā.nirvāhaṇapravaṇam dhūmādy.anumānam iṣyate; kvacana na punaḥ svargādṛṣṭādi.prasādhakam alaukikam anumānam iti /

L537: atha bhūta.catuṣṭayī.prabhavā dehe caitanyotpattiḥ katham pratīyatām ity āśaGkyāha --
L: pṛthvyādi.bhūtasamhatyā tathā devaparīṇateḥ /
L: mada.śaktiḥ surāGgebhyo yadvat tadvac cid.ātmani //84//

L538: vyākhyā -- pṛthivyādīni pṛthivy.ap.tejo.vāyu.lakṣaṇāni yāni bhūtāni teṣām samhatiḥ samavāyaḥ samyoga iti yāvat; tayā hetu.bhūtayā /
L: tathā tena prakāreṇa yā dehasya parīṇatiḥ pariṇāmas tasyāḥ sakāśāc cid iti yogaḥ /
L: yadvad yathā surāGgebhyo guḍa.dhātakyādibhyo madyāGgebhyo mada.śaktir unmādakatvam bhavati; tadvat tathā cic caitanyam ātmani śarīre /
L: atrātma.śabdenānekārthena śarīram eva jñātavyam; na punar jīvaḥ /
L: ayam bhāvaḥ -- bhūta.catuṣṭayasambandhād dehaparīṇāmah; tataś ca dehe caitanyam iti / atra parīṇati.śabde ghañ.abhāve.api bāhulakād upasargasya dīrghatvam siddham / pāṭhāntaram vā --
L: ''pṛthvyādi.bhūtasamhatyām tathā dehādi.sambhavaḥ /
L: mada.śaktiḥ surāGgebhyo yadvat tadvat sthitā.ātmatā //''
L: pṛthivyādi.bhūtasamhatyā satyām; tathā.śabdaḥ pūrva.ślokāpekṣayā samuccayeṇ dehādi.sambhavaḥ /
L: ādi.śabdād bhū.bhū.dharādayo bhūtasamyogajā jñeyāḥ / surāGgebhyo yadvan mada.śaktir bhavati tadvad bhūtasambandhāt śarīra ātmanā sacetanatā sthitā vyavasthitā.iti /
L: yad uvāca vācaspatiḥ -- ''pṛthivy āpas tejo vāyur iti tattvāni; tat.samudāye śarīraviṣaya.indriyasamjñā; tebhyaś caitanyam'' iti //84//

L569: evam sthite tathā.upadiśanti tathā darśayann āha --
L: tasmād dṛṣṭaparityāgād yad adṛṣṭe pravartanam 
L: lokasya tad vimūḍhatvam cārvākāḥ pratipedire //85//

L570: vyavasthā -- yasmād bhūtebhyaś caitanyotpattis tasmāt kāraṇād dṛṣṭaparityāgād dṛṣṭam pratyakṣānubhūtam aihikam laukikam yad viṣayajam sukham tasya parityāgād dṛṣṭe para.llokasukhādau tapaś.caraṇādi.kaṣṭa.kriyā.sādhye yat pravartanam pravṛttiḥ tat lokasya vimūḍhatvam ajñānam eva.iti cārvākāḥ prapedire pratipannāḥ /
L: yo hi loko vipratārakavacanopanyāsa.trāsitasamjñāno hasta.gatam ihatyam sukham vihāya svargāpavargasukhaprepsayā tapo.japa.dhyāna.homādau yad yatate; tatra tasyājñānatā.eva kāraṇam iti tan matopadeśaḥ //85//

L571: atha ye śāntarasapūritasvāntā nirupamam śamasukham varṇayanti; tān uddiśya yac cārvākā bruvate tad āha --
L: sādyavṛttinivṛttibhyām yā prītir jāyate jane /
L: nirarthā sā mate teṣām dharmaḥ kāmāt paro na hi //86//

L572: vyākyā -- sādhyam dhyānam dvedhā; upādeyam heyamca / upādeye dharma.śukla.dhyāna.yuge heye cārtaraudra.dhyāna.yuge /
L: athavā sādhye sādhanīye kārye; upādeye puṇya.kṛtye tapah.samyamādau; heye ca pāpa.kṛtye viṣamasukhādike krameṇa vṛttinivṛttibhyām pravartananivartanābhyām jane loke yā prītr manah.sukham jāyate samutpadyate sā teṣām cārvākāṇām mate 'nirarthā niḥprayojanā niḥphalātāttvikī.ity.arthaḥ /
L: hir yasmāt; dharmaḥ kāmād viṣayasukhasevanān na paraḥ / kāma eva paramo dharmah; taj.janitam eva ca paramam sukham iti bhāvaḥ /
L: athavā ye dharmaprabhāvād iha loke.api.iṣṭāniṣṭa.kāryayoḥ siddhy.asiddhī vadanti; tān prati yac cārvākā jalpanti tad darśayann āha ''sādhyavṛttinivṛttibhyām' ityādi /
L: tapo.japa.homādibhiḥ sādhyasya prepsita.kāryasya yā vṛttiḥ siddhir yā ca tair eva tapo.japādibhir aniṣṭasya sādhyasya vighnāder nivṛttir asiddhir abhāva iti yāvat; tābhyām sādhyavṛttinivṛttibhyām yā jane prītir jāyate sā nirarthā /
L: artha.śabdasya hetv.arthasyāpi bhāvān nirhetukā nirmūlā /teṣām mate hir yasmād dharmaḥ kāmān na para iti prāgvat // 

L573: upasamharann āha / kokāyatamate.apy evam samkṣayo 'yam niveditaḥ /
L: abhidheya.tātparyārthaḥ paryālocyaḥ subuddhibhiḥ //87//

L574: vyākhyā -- evam amunā prakāreṇa; apeḥ samuccayārthatvān na kevalam anyamateṣu samkṣepa ukto lokāyatamate.apy ayam anantaroktaḥ samkṣepo niveditaḥ /
L: nanu bauddhādi.mateṣu sarveṣv api samkṣepa evātra yady *ucyate; tarhi vistareṇa tat.paramārthaḥ katham avabotsyata ity āśaGkyāha ''abhidheya'' ityādi / 
L: abhidheyasya sarva.darśanavācyasyārthasya tātpatyārtho 'śeṣaviśeṣaviśiṣṭaḥ paramārthaḥ parisamantāt paurvāparyeṇālocyaḥ svayam vimarśanīyaḥ /
L: athavā 'locṛ darśane' iti dhātupāṭhād (Iṇ 177) ālocyas tat.tatat.tadīya.śāstrebhyo 'valokanīyaḥ subuddhibhiḥ sunipuṇamatibhih; samkṣiptaruci.sattvānugrahārthatvād asya sūtra.karaṇasya.iti /
L: athavā sarva.darśanasammatānām (tattvānām) parasparam virodham ākarṣya; kiṃkartavyatāmūḍhānām prāṇinām yat.(tat?).kartavyopadeśam āha 'abhidheya' ityādi /
L: abhidheyam (abhidheyaḥ?) sarva.darśanasambandhī pratipādyo 'rthah; tasya yas tātparyārthaḥ satyāsatyavibhāgena vyavasthāpitas tattvārthaḥ sa paryālocyaḥ /
L: samyag.vicāraṇīyo na punar yathā.uktamātro nirvicāram grāhyaḥ / kaih; subuddhibhiḥ suṣṭhu śobhanā mārgānusāriṇī pakṣapātarahitā buddhiḥ matir yeṣām te subuddhayah; tair na punaḥ kad.āgraha.grahilaiḥ / yad uktam --
L: ''āgrahī bata ninīṣati yuktim yatra tatra matir asya niviṣṭā /
L: pakṣapātarahitasya tu yuktir yatra tatra matir eti niveśam //1//''

L575: ayam atra bhāvārthaḥ -- sarva.darśanānām parsparam matavirodham ākarṇya mūḍhasya prāṇinaḥ sarva.darśanaspṛhayālutāyām nija.darśanaikapakṣapātitāyām vā durlabham svargāpavargasādhakatvam; ato madhyasthavṛttitayā vimarśanīyaḥ satyāsatyārthavibhāgena tāttviko 'rthah; vimṛśya ca śreyas.karaḥ panthābhyupagantavyo yatitavyam ca tatra kuśalamatibhiḥ / (jaiminīya.cārvākīyamatasvarūpanirṇayo nāma ṣaṣṭho 'dhikarah)[Eṇḍ of ṣḍṣ][Eṇḍ]
